Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Cosmos 3是一个统一的全模态世界模型家族，采用混合Transformer架构联合处理语言、图像、视频、音频和动作序列，在理解和生成任务上达到SOTA，并开源了代码、模型和数据集以推动Physical AI研究。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有模型通常针对单一模态或有限模态组合设计，缺乏一个能够统一处理Physical AI所需的所有关键模态（语言、图像、视频、音频、动作）的统一框架，限制了具身智能体的泛化能力。

Method: 采用混合Transformer架构（mixture-of-transformers），设计高度灵活的输入-输出配置，将视觉-语言模型、视频生成器、世界模拟器和世界-动作模型统一到单一框架中，支持语言、图像、视频、音频和动作序列的联合处理与生成。

Result: 在多种理解和生成任务上达到SOTA；后训练的模型被Artificial Analysis评为最佳开源文生图和图生视频模型，被RoboArena评为最佳策略模型。

Conclusion: 全模态世界模型可作为可扩展的通用骨干网络用于具身智能体，为Physical AI的开放研究和部署提供了基础性贡献。

Original Abstract: We introduce Cosmos 3, a family of omnimodal world models designed to jointly process and generate language, image, video, audio, and action sequences within a unified mixture-of-transformers architecture. By supporting highly flexible input-output configurations, Cosmos 3 seamlessly unifies critical modalities for Physical AI -- effectively subsuming vision-language models, video generators, world simulators, and world-action models into a single framework. Our evaluation demonstrates that Cosmos 3 establishes a new state-of-the-art across a diverse suite of understanding and generation tasks, demonstrating omnimodal world models as scalable, general-purpose backbones for embodied agents. Our post-trained Cosmos 3 models were ranked as the best open-source Text-to-Image and Image-to-Video models by Artificial Analysis, and the best policy model by RoboArena at the time the technical report was written. To accelerate open research and deployment in Physical AI, we make our code, model checkpoints, curated synthetic datasets, and evaluation benchmark available under the Linux Foundation's OpenMDW-1.1 https://openmdw.ai/license/1-1/ License at https://github.com/nvidia/cosmos}{github.com/nvidia/cosmos and https://huggingface.co/collections/nvidia/cosmos3 . The project website is available at https://research.nvidia.com/labs/cosmos-lab/cosmos3 .

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了Cosmos 3，一个全模态世界模型家族，旨在通过统一的混合Transformer架构联合处理并生成语言、图像、视频、音频和动作序列。通过支持高度灵活的输入-输出配置，Cosmos 3无缝统一了Physical AI的关键模态——有效地将视觉-语言模型、视频生成器、世界模拟器和世界-动作模型整合到单一框架中。我们的评估表明，Cosmos 3在一系列多样化的理解和生成任务上建立了新的最先进水平，证明了全模态世界模型可作为可扩展的通用骨干网络用于具身智能体。我们的后训练Cosmos 3模型在技术报告撰写时被Artificial Analysis评为最佳开源文生图和图生视频模型，并被RoboArena评为最佳策略模型。为加速Physical AI的开放研究和部署，我们在Linux Foundation的OpenMDW-1.1许可证下开源了代码、模型检查点、精选合成数据集以及评估基准，访问地址为https://github.com/nvidia/cosmos、https://huggingface.co/collections/nvidia/cosmos3。项目网站为https://research.nvidia.com/labs/cosmos-lab/cosmos3。

</details>


### [10] [Hand Trajectory Fusion for Egocentric Natural Language Query Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02962)
*Enmin Zhong,Carlos R. del-Blanco,Fernando Jaureguizar,Narciso García*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对自我中心自然语言查询（NLQ）定位任务，现有方法忽略了手部运动信息，而约41%的Ego4D NLQ查询涉及手-物交互。本文提出手部轨迹编码器，将手部骨骼序列转换为高语义手部运动学特征，通过带有自适应门控的交叉注意力融合策略，与预训练视频-文本特征对齐融合。在Ego4D NLQ v2验证集上，手-物交互查询提升2.54 R1@IoU=0.3，数量/状态查询提升4.32 R1@IoU=0.3，证明手部轨迹提供了外观之外的关键定位线索。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自我中心视频NLQ定位方法仅融合视频外观与文本查询，忽略了手部运动信息。然而，Ego4D数据集中约41%的自然语言查询在回答时涉及手-物操作或其直接结果，因此引入手部运动信息有望提升定位性能。

Method: 提出手部轨迹编码器，将手部骨骼序列转换为高语义的手部运动学特征；通过交叉注意力融合策略配合自适应门控，将这些特征与预训练的视频-文本特征进行对齐和融合。

Result: 在Ego4D NLQ v2验证集上，手-物交互查询提升2.54 R1@IoU=0.3，数量/状态查询提升4.32 R1@IoU=0.3，表明手部轨迹提供了外观之外的重要定位线索。

Conclusion: 手部轨迹能够提供视频外观信息之外的额外定位线索，有效提升自我中心视频NLQ定位性能，尤其在手-物交互和数量/状态类查询上效果显著。

Original Abstract: Egocentric Natural Language Query (NLQ) grounding asks a model to localize, in a long first-person video, the temporal interval that answers a free-form text query. Existing methods fuse video appearance with the query but ignore hand motion, despite the fact that roughly 41% of Ego4D NLQ queries are answered at a moment of hand--object manipulation or their immediate outcomes.We propose a hand-trajectory encoder for converting a sequence of hand skeletons into highly-semantic hand kinematic features, which are then aligned and combined with pretrained video--text features through a cross-attention fusion strategy with adaptive gating. On the Ego4D NLQ v2 validation split, the clearest gains appear for Hand-Object Interaction queries (+2.54 R1@IoU=0.3) and Quantity/State queries (+4.32 R1@IoU=0.3), indicating that hand trajectory provides grounding cues beyond appearance alone.

中文摘要翻译: 自我中心自然语言查询（NLQ）定位任务要求模型在一个长距离第一人称视频中，定位出回答自由形式文本查询的时间区间。现有方法融合了视频外观与文本查询，但忽略了手部运动信息，尽管在Ego4D NLQ数据集中约有41%的查询是在手-物操作时刻或其直接结果时刻被回答的。我们提出了一种手部轨迹编码器，用于将手部骨骼序列转换为高语义的手部运动学特征，然后通过一种带有自适应门控的交叉注意力融合策略，将这些特征与预训练的视频-文本特征进行对齐和融合。在Ego4D NLQ v2验证集上，最明显的提升出现在手-物交互查询（R1@IoU=0.3提升2.54）和数量/状态查询（R1@IoU=0.3提升4.32），这表明手部轨迹提供了外观信息之外的定位线索。

</details>


### [11] [Automated Report-Derived Oncology VQA Benchmark for Evaluating Vision-Language Models on 3D Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02809)
*Bo Liu,Hanxue Gu,Xiangru Li,Zheren Zhu,Jacob Ellison,Kang Wang,Janine M. Lupo,Yang Yang,Hui Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自动化智能体驱动的流水线，从私有放射学报告和3D肿瘤影像生成多选题VQA基准，无需人工标注即可实现实例级别的污染控制。评估发现视觉依赖高度数据集特定，甚至私有数据也无法保证无污染的视觉能力评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有医学影像VLM评估基准在规模、标注成本以及潜在数据泄露（污染）方面存在局限，亟需一种可扩展、临床可落地且污染可控的评估方法。

Method: 提出自动化智能体驱动的流水线，从配对的私有放射学报告和3D肿瘤影像中生成两类多选题：（1）基于临床报告模式的确定性RADS式问题；（2）由LLM从放射科医师发现中生成并经源报告验证的报告派生问题。在四个内部癌症队列中部署，无需逐题人工标注。

Result: 零样本评估六个VLM显示无主导模型且所有场景均有显著提升空间。盲法消融实验表明视觉依赖高度数据集特定：肝脏报告派生问题真正需要图像，而肺CT基本上无需图像即可解答——领先闭源模型在盲视时准确率反而超过有视觉输入时，表明即使私有临床数据也无法保证视觉能力的无污染评估。

Conclusion: 私有数据并不自动等同于无污染的视觉能力评估；该流水线作为开放智能体技能发布，可用于内部再部署。

Original Abstract: Evaluating vision-language models (VLMs) on medical images requires benchmarks that are clinically grounded, scalable, and controlled for evaluation confounds. Existing public benchmarks are limited in scale, manually annotated, or potentially leaked into VLM pretraining corpora. We present an automated agent-driven pipeline that generates multiple-choice VQA datasets directly from paired private radiology reports and 3D oncology imaging, producing two complementary question types: RADS-style questions deterministically derived from clinician-defined reporting schemas, and radiology report-derived questions generated by an LLM from radiologist findings and verified against the source report. Applied to four in-house cancer cohorts, the pipeline yields an instance-contamination-controlled benchmark without per-question human annotation. Zero-shot evaluation of six VLMs reveals no dominant model and substantial headroom across all cells. A blind ablation reveals that visual reliance is highly dataset-specific: liver Report-derived questions genuinely require the image, while Lung CT is essentially solvable without it - the leading closed model exceeds its sighted accuracy on Lung CT when blinded - indicating that even private clinical data does not guarantee a contamination-controlled read of visual capability. The pipeline is released as an open agent skill for in-house redeployment.

中文摘要翻译: 在医学图像上评估视觉-语言模型（VLM）需要具有临床基础、可扩展且对评估混杂因素可控的基准。现有的公开基准在规模上受限、需要人工标注或可能已泄露到VLM预训练语料中。我们提出了一种自动化智能体驱动的流水线，可直接从配对的私有放射学报告和3D肿瘤影像生成多选题VQA数据集，产生两种互补的问题类型：从临床定义的报告模式中确定性推导出的RADS式问题，以及由LLM根据放射科医师发现生成并经源报告验证的放射学报告派生问题。将该流水线应用于四个内部癌症队列，可在无需逐题人工标注的情况下产生实例级污染受控的基准。对六个VLM的零样本评估显示，没有主导模型，且所有场景下均有显著提升空间。盲法消融实验表明，视觉依赖高度依赖于具体数据集：肝脏报告派生问题确实需要图像，而肺CT基本上无需图像即可解答——领先的闭源模型在盲视状态下对肺CT的准确率甚至超过其有视觉输入时的准确率，这表明即使私有临床数据也无法保证视觉能力的污染受控评估。该流水线已作为开放智能体技能发布，可用于内部再部署。

</details>


### [12] [Principled Reflection Separation via Nonlinear Superposition and Feature Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02831)
*Qiming Hu,Mingjia Li,Yuntong Li,Xiaojie Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出可学习非线性叠加模型和双流交互框架，用于单幅图像反射分离，解决了传统线性混合模型在sRGB域中的局限性，在多个真实基准上取得优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有反射分离方法大多基于简化的线性混合假设（sRGB域线性组合模型），无法捕捉真实图像信号处理管线引入的非线性耦合，导致在复杂场景下分解效果受限。

Method: 提出可学习非线性叠加模型以更真实地刻画图层交互；构建广义双流交互框架，通过特征交换显式建模透射层与反射层之间的双向依赖关系，统一了激活、门控和注意力三种交互机制，兼容CNN和Transformer骨干网络。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准数据集上，该方法取得了优越的性能和强泛化能力。实验表明反射分离本质上是学习非线性形成与交互过程，而非逆转线性混合。

Conclusion: 反射分离的关键在于对非线性图像形成过程的建模与图层间交互的学习，这为设计更通用的图像分解模型提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Single-image reflection separation is fundamentally challenged by the entanglement of transmission and reflection layers under complex image formation processes. Existing approaches largely rely on simplified assumptions or independent modeling, limiting their ability to handle real-world scenarios. In this work, we revisit the problem from a unified perspective and identify a key issue of existing approaches, i.e., the widely adopted linear composition model in the sRGB domain fails to capture the nonlinear coupling introduced by real-world image signal processing pipelines. To address this, we introduce a learnable nonlinear superposition model that more faithfully characterizes layer interactions and improves decomposition fidelity. Building upon this formulation, we propose a generalized dual-stream interactive framework that explicitly models bidirectional dependencies between transmission and reflection through feature exchange. This framework unifies activation-, gating-, and attention-based interaction mechanisms, and is compatible with both CNN and Transformer backbones. Extensive experiments on diverse real-world benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves superior performance with strong generalization capability. More importantly, our study reveals that reflection separation is not about undoing a linear mixture, but about learning nonlinear formation and interaction}, offering new insights into the design of principled image decomposition models. Code and models are publicly available at https://mingcv.github.io/DIRS-Page.

中文摘要翻译: 单幅图像反射分离的根本挑战在于复杂成像过程中透射层与反射层的纠缠。现有方法大多依赖简化假设或独立建模，限制了其在真实场景中的表现。本文从统一视角重新审视该问题，指出现有方法的关键缺陷——在sRGB域广泛采用的线性组合模型无法捕捉真实图像信号处理管线引入的非线性耦合。为此，我们引入了一种可学习的非线性叠加模型，更真实地刻画了图层交互并提升了分解保真度。基于该模型，我们提出了一种广义双流交互框架，通过特征交换显式建模透射与反射之间的双向依赖关系。该框架统一了激活、门控和注意力三种交互机制，并兼容CNN和Transformer骨干网络。在多个真实世界基准上的大量实验表明，所提方法具有优越的性能和强泛化能力。更重要的是，本研究揭示了反射分离的本质并非逆转线性混合，而是学习非线性的图像形成与交互过程，为设计原则性的图像分解模型提供了新见解。代码和模型已开源：https://mingcv.github.io/DIRS-Page。

</details>


### [13] [Pathway-Structured Privileged Distillation for Deployable Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02877)
*Yongxin Guo,Hao Lu,Onur Koyun,Zhengjie Zhu,Muhammet Demir,Metin Gurcan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MoPE框架，通过知识蒸馏将转录组学信息作为特权特征融入病理图像分析，实现无需RNA数据的组织学-only推理，在多项基准和独立乳腺癌队列中显著提升性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 转录组学与组织病理学的多模态融合可改善癌症风险建模，但RNA测序在常规临床中难以广泛获取，亟需一种能在推理时无需RNA数据、仅在训练时利用分子信息的方法。

Method: 提出Mixture of Pathway Experts (MoPE)框架，采用特权蒸馏（privileged distillation）范式：将RNA衍生的通路信息编码为特权特征，通过记忆使用对齐（memory-usage alignment）将分子监督信号迁移至通路索引的病理专家模型（pathway-indexed pathology experts），实现训练时利用多模态信息、推理时仅需全切片图像（WSI）。

Result: 在多个公开基准和两个独立乳腺癌队列中，MoPE相比基线方法一致提升了仅使用WSI的推理性能；通路使用分析和人工审核的可视化检查提供了模型行为的可解释性证据。

Conclusion: 通路结构的特权蒸馏是一种有前景的途径，可在训练时利用分子信息，同时保持推理时无需RNA数据，为临床实际部署提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Integrating transcriptomics and histopathology can improve cancer risk modelling, yet practical use is constrained by the limited availability of RNA profiling in routine settings. Here we introduce Mixture of Pathway Experts (MoPE), a knowledge-distillation framework that reframes multimodal learning as privileged distillation for histology-only inference. MoPE is motivated by the partial observability between RNA profiles and whole-slide images: histology can capture morphology-linked consequences of certain molecular programmes, but cannot be expected to reconstruct the full transcriptomic state. MoPE encodes RNA-derived pathways and transfers the molecular supervision to pathway-indexed pathology experts through memory-usage alignment. Across diverse public benchmarks and two independent breast cancer cohorts, MoPE consistently improved WSI-only inference performance relative to baseline methods. Pathway-usage analyses and human-audited visual inspection provide bounded inspection of model behaviour and candidate morphology-linked readouts. These results support pathway-structured privileged distillation as a promising route to using molecular information during training while preserving RNA-free inference.

中文摘要翻译: 整合转录组学与组织病理学可以改善癌症风险建模，但RNA分析在常规临床环境中的有限可用性制约了其实际应用。本文提出混合通路专家（Mixture of Pathway Experts, MoPE）框架，这是一种知识蒸馏方法，将多模态学习重构为面向仅组织学推理的特权蒸馏。MoPE的动机源于RNA图谱与全切片图像之间的部分可观测性：组织学可以捕捉某些分子程序在形态学上的关联结果，但无法期望其重构完整的转录组状态。MoPE对RNA来源的通路进行编码，并通过记忆使用对齐将分子监督信号迁移至通路索引的病理专家模型。在多个公开基准和两个独立乳腺癌队列中，MoPE相较于基线方法一致提升了仅使用全切片图像的推理性能。通路使用分析与人工审核的可视化检查提供了模型行为的可控解释以及候选的形态学关联读数。这些结果支持通路结构的特权蒸馏作为一条有前景的途径，在训练时利用分子信息的同时保持无需RNA的推理。

</details>


### [14] [Tiny Collaborative Inference for Occlusion-Robust Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02894)
*Chieh-Tung Cheng,Mustafa Aslanov,Eiman Kanjo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了在内存小于1MB的超低端边缘设备上，通过MCUNet骨干网络、YOLOv2检测头和TensorFlow Lite量化结合的方案，实现抗遮挡的目标检测。实验表明，基于加权框融合(WBF)的决策级融合优于特征级融合，在非对称遮挡场景下mAP提升最高达+0.2736，三视角融合进一步提升了+0.3827 mAP，同时通信开销仅约1.3 KB。在双Coral Dev Board Micro的Wi-Fi点对点部署中，决策级融合实现了帧级覆盖增益+29.8%，验证了其在超低端硬件上提升遮挡鲁棒性的可行性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 超低端边缘设备（如IoT监控节点和搜救平台）需要本地运行计算机视觉任务，但受到内存和计算资源的严格限制；同时，多设备协作通信成本高，且遮挡导致检测精度下降。现有方案难以同时满足超低功耗硬件约束和遮挡鲁棒性需求。

Method: 采用MCUNet骨干网络+YOLOv2检测头+TensorFlow Lite量化的组合方案。评估了两种协作推理策略：特征级融合（拼接中间特征图）和决策级融合（加权框融合WBF）。在双Coral Dev Board Micro上实现了Wi-Fi点对点部署，并在主机关联USB中继基线基础上进行对比。

Result: WBF在非对称遮挡下mAP提升+0.2736，三视角融合mAP提升+0.3827，通信开销约1.3 KB/次。在301.9秒自主会话（108帧）中，融合输出61帧 vs 单板47帧，帧级覆盖增益+29.8%。决策级融合通信能耗远低于推理能耗。

Conclusion: 决策级融合（WBF）是超低端边缘设备上提升遮挡鲁棒性的可行方案，支持无主机多板操作，能够在极小内存（<1 MB SRAM）和有限通信开销下有效提升检测覆盖率和精度。

Original Abstract: Small edge devices such as IoT surveillance nodes and search-and-rescue (SAR) platforms are increasingly expected to run computer vision locally. On ultra-low-end hardware, however, object detection is limited by available memory and compute, by communication costs when several devices cooperate, and by the loss of accuracy caused by occlusion. The work evaluates occlusion-robust object detection on devices with less than 1 MB SRAM by combining an MCUNet backbone, a YOLOv2 detection head, and TensorFlow Lite quantisation. We evaluate two collaborative inference strategies: feature-level fusion, which concatenates intermediate feature maps, and decision-level fusion via Weighted Boxes Fusion (WBF). Under the tested occlusion settings, WBF outperforms feature-level fusion and gives gains of up to +0.2736 mAP in asymmetric occlusion scenarios. Extending fusion to three views improves accuracy further (up to +0.3827 mAP) while adding communication overhead (approximately 1.3 KB per exchange). The hardware experiments start with a host-assisted USB-relay baseline and then move to a Wi-Fi peer-to-peer deployment on two Coral Dev Board Micro units, where WBF runs on-device and communication energy remains small relative to inference. In a representative 301.9 s autonomous session comprising 108 frames, fused output is observed on 61 frames compared with 47 for Board 2 alone, a frame-level coverage gain of +29.8%. We also include a small exploratory decentralised federated learning (DFL) feasibility note, but do not treat it as a main result because performance remains limited under non-iid local data. The results support decision-level fusion as a viable option for improving occlusion robustness in small-scale edge object detection, including host-free multi-board operation on ultra-low-end hardware.

中文摘要翻译: 诸如物联网监控节点和搜救平台等小型边缘设备，越来越需要本地运行计算机视觉任务。然而，在超低端硬件上，目标检测受到可用内存和计算资源的限制、多设备协作时的通信成本以及遮挡导致的精度损失。本文评估了在内存小于1 MB SRAM的设备上，通过结合MCUNet骨干网络、YOLOv2检测头和TensorFlow Lite量化实现抗遮挡目标检测的方法。我们评估了两种协作推理策略：特征级融合（拼接中间特征图）和通过加权框融合实现的决策级融合。在所测试的遮挡设置下，WBF优于特征级融合，在非对称遮挡场景中mAP提升高达+0.2736。将融合扩展到三视角可进一步提高精度（mAP提升高达+0.3827），同时增加通信开销（每次交换约1.3 KB）。硬件实验从主机关联的USB中继基线开始，随后过渡到两个Coral Dev Board Micro单元上的Wi-Fi点对点部署，其中WBF在设备端运行，通信能耗相对于推理能耗保持在较小水平。在一个代表性的301.9秒自主会话中，共处理108帧，融合输出出现在61帧中，而板2单独处理仅为47帧，帧级覆盖增益为+29.8%。我们还包含了一个探索性的去中心化联邦学习可行性说明，但由于在非独立同分布本地数据下性能仍然受限，未将其作为主要结果。研究结果表明，决策级融合是提升小型边缘目标检测中遮挡鲁棒性的可行方案，包括在超低端硬件上实现无主机多板操作。

</details>


### [15] [Any2Poster: Any-Source Poster Generation Across Modalities and Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02915)
*Amogh Vinaykumar,Aiden Li,Suozhi Huang,Shilong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Any2Poster Bench基准，用于评估从多种输入模态（PDF、URL等）到海报的自动生成系统，同时提出Any2Poster Agent作为端到端基线系统，在信息保真度和视觉质量上取得显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自动海报生成评估局限于论文单模态输入、窄领域或浅层视觉相似度，缺乏可复现、多模态、多领域的系统化评估基准。

Method: 构建Any2Poster Bench基准，涵盖8种输入模态和5个内容领域，采用基于测验的事实保留与理解评估以及VLM驱动的视觉质量评估。同时提出Any2Poster Agent，实现异构源解析、内容组织、布局规划、渲染与迭代优化。

Result: Any2Poster Agent在基准上平均准确率达87.25%（模态）和87.28%（领域）；在PaperQuiz评估中，整体准确率从51.06-51.33%提升至72.58%，密度增强分数从116-121提升至145.16。

Conclusion: Any2Poster Bench与Any2Poster Agent为多模态、跨领域海报生成提供了可复用的评估资源和有竞争力的基线。

Original Abstract: Visual posters are a compact medium for communicating dense information, yet progress on automatic poster generation remains difficult to measure because existing evaluations are often restricted to paper-only inputs, narrow domains, or surface-level visual similarity. We introduce Any2Poster Bench, a benchmark for any-source poster generation that evaluates systems across eight input modalities--PDFs, URLs, PPTX, DOCX, Markdown, LaTeX, notebooks, and videos--and five content domains. Any2Poster Bench pairs each source with quiz-based probes of verbatim factual retention and interpretive understanding, together with VLM-based judgments of visual quality, layout, readability, content completeness, and logical flow, enabling reproducible assessment of both information fidelity and visual communication. To instantiate and validate this benchmark, we further present Any2Poster Agent, an end-to-end reference agent that parses heterogeneous sources, organizes salient content, plans poster layouts, renders posters, and iteratively refines them using visual feedback. On Any2Poster Bench, Any2Poster Agent achieves 87.25% average accuracy across input modalities and 87.28% across content domains. On PaperQuiz-style evaluation, where prior paper-to-poster agents are directly comparable, Any2Poster Agent improves over PosterAgent-4o from 51.06-51.33% to 72.58% overall accuracy and from 116-121 to 145.16 in density-augmented score. Together, Any2Poster Bench and Any2Poster Agent provide a reusable evaluation resource and a competitive baseline for studying multimodal, domain-general poster generation.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉海报是一种用于传达密集信息的紧凑媒介，然而自动海报生成方面的进展仍然难以衡量，因为现有评估通常局限于仅论文输入、狭窄领域或浅层视觉相似性。我们提出了Any2Poster Bench——一个面向任意来源海报生成的基准，它评估系统在八种输入模态（PDF、URL、PPTX、DOCX、Markdown、LaTeX、笔记本和视频）和五个内容领域上的表现。Any2Poster Bench将每个源与基于测验的逐字事实保留与解释性理解探针配对，同时结合基于VLM的视觉质量、布局、可读性、内容完整性和逻辑流畅性判断，从而实现对信息保真度和视觉传达的可复现评估。为了实例化和验证这一基准，我们进一步提出了Any2Poster Agent——一个端到端的参考智能体，它能够解析异构源、组织显著内容、规划海报布局、渲染海报，并利用视觉反馈迭代优化。在Any2Poster Bench上，Any2Poster Agent在输入模态上达到87.25%的平均准确率，在内容领域上达到87.28%。在直接可与先前论文到海报智能体比较的PaperQuiz式评估中，Any2Poster Agent将整体准确率从51.06-51.33%提升至72.58%，将密度增强分数从116-121提升至145.16。Any2Poster Bench与Any2Poster Agent共同为研究多模态、跨领域海报生成提供了可复用的评估资源和有竞争力的基线。

</details>


### [16] [Pixel Cube: Diffusion-based Portrait Video Relighting Through Realistic Lighting Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02919)
*Yufan Zhang,Yu Ji,Ayo Ajiboye,Rundi Wu,Yu Guo,Changxi Zheng,Jinwei Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于扩散模型的动态人像视频重光照方法，利用混合训练数据集（真实采集+渲染数据）和LED照明系统，实现逼真、时序一致且身份保持的视频重光照。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有动态人像视频重光照方法在逼真度、时序一致性和身份保持方面存在不足，需要一种能够处理复杂光照变化、保留面部细节并生成和谐自然效果的方法。

Method: 构建LED照明系统采集真实高动态范围（HDR）环境图控制下的动态人像视频数据，结合真实与渲染的混合训练数据集，利用预训练视频扩散模型的图像先验，以每帧HDR环境图作为光照控制信号，并引入合成背景图像控制相机曝光和色调，训练生成模型实现重光照。

Result: 在真实世界视频上使用多种环境图进行重光照实验，在逼真度、光照和谐性、时序一致性方面达到最先进水平，且对未见数据（新人物、新动作、新光照）具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法在动态人像视频重光照任务上取得了最优性能，能够生成真实感强、时序连贯、身份保持的重光照结果，并具有实际应用价值。

Original Abstract: We present a diffusion-based method for relighting dynamic portrait videos with photorealism and temporal consistency. Our method is fueled by a hybrid training dataset that consists of real-captured and rendered dynamic portrait videos with diverse subject appearances, facial motions, head poses, and known lighting conditions. Specifically, we construct an LED-based lighting system for realistic lighting emulation and high-speed video relighting data acquisition. By leveraging the image priors embedded in pre-trained video diffusion models, and using per-frame high dynamic range (HDR) environment map as lighting control, we train a high-performance generative model for realistic and identity-preserving dynamic portrait video relighting. In addition to the environment map control, our model uses a synthesized background image to enable control on the camera's exposure level and color tone. Our model can produce temporally consistent relit portrait video that looks realistic and harmonious under a provided new environment and faithfully preserve the subject's expression and fine facial features, including skin tone, wrinkles, and facial hair. Our model generalizes well to unseen data, in terms of the subject appearance, motion, and lighting condition. We perform extensive experiments on relighting in-the-wild videos with various environment maps and demonstrate practical applications on portrait photography. Results show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in photorealism, lighting harmony, and temporal consistency.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种基于扩散的方法，用于对动态人像视频进行重光照，实现了逼真的视觉效果和时间一致性。我们的方法由一个混合训练数据集驱动，该数据集包含真实采集和渲染的动态人像视频，具有多样的主体外观、面部运动、头部姿态和已知光照条件。具体而言，我们构建了一个基于LED的照明系统，用于实现逼真的光照模拟和高速视频重光照数据采集。通过利用预训练视频扩散模型中嵌入的图像先验，并以每帧高动态范围（HDR）环境图作为光照控制信号，我们训练了一个高性能的生成模型，用于实现逼真且身份保持的动态人像视频重光照。除环境图控制外，我们的模型还使用合成背景图像来控制相机的曝光水平和色彩基调。我们的模型能够生成时间上一致的、在提供的新环境下看起来真实且和谐的重光照人像视频，并忠实地保留主体的表情和精细面部特征，包括肤色、皱纹和胡须。我们的模型在主体外观、运动和光照条件方面对未见数据具有良好的泛化能力。我们使用多种环境图对野外视频进行了广泛的重光照实验，并展示了在人像摄影中的实际应用。结果表明，我们的方法在逼真度、光照和谐性和时间一致性方面达到了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [17] [ATLAS: A Large-Scale Evaluation Benchmark for Adversarial LiDAR Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02924)
*Mellon M. Zhang,Siddhant Panse,Zimo Fan,Akshal Dhal,Rishit Sarkar,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ATLAS是首个大规模、物理可实现的LiDAR感知黑盒攻击基准测试套件，揭示了强模型对注入攻击更脆弱的鲁棒性不对称现象，并追溯其根源到标准数据增强方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LiDAR感知评测依赖清洁数据，缺乏对物理黑盒传感器攻击（点注入/点移除）的鲁棒性评估；已有对抗攻击研究多聚焦攻击硬件与早期检测器，未系统评估现代感知模型。

Method: 构建ATLAS基准：（1）物理可实现的两种攻击模式——点注入与点移除；（2）基于真实驾驶序列生成攻击场景；（3）在多种当前最优LiDAR感知模型上进行系统评估。

Result: 发现鲁棒性不对称性：标准benchmark上更强的模型对移除攻击更鲁棒，但对注入攻击反而比弱模型更脆弱；该脆弱性根源在于标准目标数据库采样增强方法。

Conclusion: 现代LiDAR感知系统存在架构无关的鲁棒性缺陷，当前训练实践引入非预期的脆弱性；需将黑盒传感器鲁棒性作为显式考量纳入未来发展。

Original Abstract: Autonomous driving perception is typically evaluated on clean benchmark data, yet real-world deployment requires robustness to rare, structured, and potentially adversarial sensor anomalies. This gap is especially critical for LiDAR, where external actors can physically manipulate the sensing process to induce black-box perception failures without accessing the model. Existing LiDAR benchmarks provide little visibility into this failure mode. Prior adversarial LiDAR studies have largely centered on attack hardware, geometric and algorithmic defenses, and early-generation detectors, leaving the robustness of modern perception systems unexplored. To address this evaluation gap, we introduce ATLAS (Adversarial Temporal LiDAR Attack Suite), the first large-scale, physically grounded evaluation benchmark for LiDAR perception models under black-box sensor attacks, simulating the two primary attack modes -- point injection and point removal -- across real driving sequences. Evaluating a broad cross-section of current state-of-the-art LiDAR perception models, ATLAS reveals a surprising robustness asymmetry: models with stronger performance on standard benchmarks tend to better withstand removal attacks, yet are actually more vulnerable to injection attacks than weaker models. We trace this vulnerability to standard object database sampling augmentations, revealing how current training practices can induce architecture-agnostic robustness failures, and study initial directions for mitigating both attack modes. We release the ATLAS generation code to support extensible, reproducible evaluations as attack capabilities evolve, helping make black-box sensor robustness an explicit consideration in future LiDAR perception development.

中文摘要翻译: 自动驾驶感知通常在干净的基准数据上进行评估，然而实际部署需要应对罕见、结构化且可能具有对抗性的传感器异常。这一缺口对LiDAR尤为关键，因为外部行为者可以在不访问模型的情况下，通过物理方式操纵传感过程，引发黑盒感知失败。现有的LiDAR基准测试对此类失败模式的可见性很低。先前的LiDAR对抗攻击研究主要集中在攻击硬件、几何与算法防御以及早期检测器上，使得现代感知系统的鲁棒性未得到探索。为弥补这一评估缺口，我们提出了ATLAS（对抗性时间LiDAR攻击套件），这是首个大规模、物理可实现的LiDAR感知模型黑盒传感器攻击评估基准，模拟了两种主要攻击模式——点注入与点移除——覆盖真实驾驶序列。对当前多种最先进LiDAR感知模型的评估揭示了一个令人惊讶的鲁棒性不对称性：在标准基准上表现更强的模型更能承受移除攻击，但在注入攻击面前反而比弱模型更脆弱。我们将这一脆弱性追溯到标准的目标数据库采样增强方法，揭示了当前训练实践如何导致与架构无关的鲁棒性失败，并初步研究了缓解两种攻击模式的方向。我们开源ATLAS生成代码，支持随着攻击能力演进而进行可扩展、可复现的评估，助力使黑盒传感器鲁棒性成为未来LiDAR感知开发的显式考量因素。

</details>


### [18] [SaluNet: Enabling Total Plasticity in Normalization-Free Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02927)
*Mourad Zaied*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用可学习激活函数SALU替代所有归一化层，基于“总可塑性”范式构建SaluNet，在不使用归一化的情况下在图像分类任务上达到甚至超越传统方法性能，并发现归一化层会抑制网络的可塑性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 归一化层（BatchNorm、LayerNorm）长期以来被认为是深度网络稳定训练的关键，但本文发现标准归一化会导致可学习激活参数快速丧失适应性（可塑性抑制效应），因此希望设计一种不依赖归一化层、但能提供内在信号稳定性的激活机制。

Method: 提出SALU（饱和自适应线性单元），一种有界可学习激活函数 SALU(x;a,b)=ax/√(1+abx²)，无需批统计或外部仿射参数即可实现信号稳定。在此基础上构建SaluNet范式：SALU替代归一化层，SWALU和GALU替代标准激活函数，实现“总可塑性”。

Result: ResNet-18架构的SaluNet-C-18在CIFAR-10/100上分别达到97.35%/83.25%（无归一化），batch size=1时仍保持93.44%/76.23%（归一化架构失效）；Transformer的SaluNet-T在CIFAR-10/100上超过LayerNorm-GELU（90.92%→91.01%，66.54%→68.10%）；SaluNet-C-50在ImageNet-1K上达到78.67%（224×224）和79.23%（288×288）Top-1精度。

Conclusion: 归一化层会抑制总可塑性，而生物神经元天然具备总可塑性；SALU及其SaluNet范式无需归一化即可使深度网络有效学习，证明了可学习激活函数可以完全替代归一化层。

Original Abstract: Normalization layers such as BatchNorm and LayerNorm have long been considered essential for stable training in deep networks. This work demonstrates that they can be fully replaced by a single learnable activation mechanism. We identify a plasticity suppression effect induced by standard normalization: learnable activation parameters rapidly lose adaptability when paired with normalization layers. Motivated by this observation, we introduce SALU (Saturated Adaptive Linear Unit), \[ \operatorname{SALU}(x;a,b) = \frac{a x}{\sqrt{1 + a b x^2}},\quad a>0,\; b>0 \] a bounded, learnable activation that provides intrinsic signal stabilization without relying on batch statistics or external affine parameters. Building on SALU, we propose SaluNet, a paradigm grounded in total plasticity: SALU replaces normalization layers, while SWALU and GALU replace standard activations. With ResNet-18, SaluNet-C-18 achieves 97.35\% on CIFAR-10 and 83.25\% on CIFAR-100 without normalization, maintaining 93.44\% and 76.23\% at batch size 1 where normalized architectures fail. For transformers, SaluNet-T improves over LayerNorm-GELU from 90.92\% to 91.01\% on CIFAR-10 and from 66.54\% to 68.10\% on CIFAR-100. SaluNet-C-50 reaches 78.67\% Top-1 on ImageNet-1K at $224\times224$, and $79.23\%$ at $288\times288$. These results suggest normalization layers suppress total plasticity, a property biological neurons inherently possess, enabling deep networks to learn effectively.

中文摘要翻译: 长期以来，BatchNorm和LayerNorm等归一化层被认为是深度网络稳定训练的必要组件。本文证明，它们可以被单一的可学习激活机制完全替代。我们发现标准归一化会诱发一种可塑性抑制效应：当与归一化层配对时，可学习激活参数迅速丧失适应性。受此观察启发，我们提出SALU（饱和自适应线性单元），SALU(x;a,b)=ax/√(1+abx²)，a>0,b>0，这是一种有界、可学习的激活函数，无需依赖批统计或外部仿射参数即可提供内在的信号稳定。基于SALU，我们提出SaluNet，一种立足于总可塑性的新范式：SALU替代归一化层，SWALU和GALU替代标准激活函数。在ResNet-18上，SaluNet-C-18在CIFAR-10上达到97.35%，在CIFAR-100上达到83.25%，且无需归一化；在归一化架构失效的batch size为1的情况下，仍保持93.44%和76.23%的精度。对于Transformer，SaluNet-T在CIFAR-10上比LayerNorm-GELU从90.92%提升至91.01%，在CIFAR-100上从66.54%提升至68.10%。SaluNet-C-50在ImageNet-1K上224×224分辨率下达到78.67%的Top-1精度，288×288下达到79.23%。这些结果表明归一化层会抑制总可塑性，而生物神经元天然具备这一特性，从而使深度网络能够有效学习。

</details>


### [19] [CAD-to-CT Registration of Cylindrical Objects via Ellipse-Based Axis Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02935)
*Aleksander Ogonowski,Mikołaj Mrozowski,Daniel Więcek,Arkadiusz Ćwiek,Konrad Klimaszewski,Rafał Możdżonek,Adam Padee,Lech Raczyński,Piotr Wasiuk,Wojciech Wiślicki,Michał Matusiak,Sławomir Wronka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对圆柱形物体（电离室）的两阶段几何配准方法，无需强度校准或特征匹配即可实现CAD模型与CT扫描的精确配准，配准误差低于0.1°。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: CAD模型与CT扫描的精确配准对于建立体素成像中的真实几何基准至关重要，但传统基于强度的方法因CT灰度值缺乏校准参考而失效，基于点的方法（如ICP、RANSAC）则因理想化CAD几何与含噪CT数据之间缺乏特征对应关系而无法使用。

Method: 两阶段几何配准方法：(1) 通过检测CT切片中的椭圆横截面，对边缘检测轮廓进行椭圆拟合，经RANSAC去除异常值后对拟合椭圆中心进行PCA分析，估计3D旋转轴；(2) 对CAD模型进行体素化，沿检测到的轴定向，并通过平移调整最大化与CT扫描的体积重叠。

Result: 该方法在无需强度校准或特征匹配的情况下实现了稳健配准，倾斜和方向误差低于0.1°。

Conclusion: 配准后的CAD模型可为机器学习目标定位和工业CT工作流中的自动化分析等应用提供真实几何基准。

Original Abstract: Accurate registration of CAD models to CT scans is essential for establishing ground truth geometry in volumetric imaging. Obtaining reliable object masks is of growing importance in machine learning settings; as recent architectures grow more capable, huge datasets are required to fully utilise their capabilities. Traditional intensity-based methods fail when CT grayscale values lack calibration references, while point-based algorithms (e.g., ICP, RANSAC) require feature correspondence unavailable between idealized CAD geometry and noisy volumetric CT data.
  We propose a two-stage geometric registration method for cylindrical objects (ionization chambers) that takes advantage of the distinctive geometric features of the objects. First, we estimate the 3D rotation axis by detecting elliptical cross-sections across CT slices, fitting ellipses to edge-detected contours, and performing PCA on the fitted ellipse centers after RANSAC outlier removal. Second, we voxelize the CAD model, orient it along the detected axis, and maximize volumetric overlap with the CT scan through translational adjustment.
  This approach achieves robust registration with tilt and orientation errors below $0.1^\circ$ without intensity calibration or feature matching. Once registered, the aligned CAD model provides ground truth geometry for applications including machine learning-based object localization and automated analysis in industrial CT workflows.

中文摘要翻译: CAD模型与CT扫描的精确配准对于在体素成像中建立真实几何基准至关重要。随着近期架构能力的不断增强，获取可靠的物体掩膜在机器学习环境中的重要性日益增长，因为需要大规模数据集才能充分利用其能力。当CT灰度值缺乏校准参考时，传统的基于强度的方法会失效，而基于点的算法（如ICP、RANSAC）需要在理想化CAD几何与含噪体素CT数据之间建立特征对应关系，而这通常是无法实现的。我们提出了一种针对圆柱形物体（电离室）的两阶段几何配准方法，该方法利用了物体独特的几何特征。首先，我们通过检测CT切片中的椭圆横截面、对边缘检测轮廓进行椭圆拟合、以及在使用RANSAC去除异常值后对拟合椭圆中心进行主成分分析（PCA）来估计3D旋转轴。其次，我们对CAD模型进行体素化，沿检测到的轴定向，并通过平移调整最大化与CT扫描的体积重叠。该方法在无需强度校准或特征匹配的情况下实现了稳健配准，倾斜和方向误差低于0.1°。一旦配准完成，对齐后的CAD模型可为包括基于机器学习的目标定位和工业CT工作流中自动化分析在内的应用提供真实几何基准。

</details>


### [20] [The Road Ahead in Autonomous Driving: The KITScenes Multimodal Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02956)
*Richard Schwarzkopf,Fabian Immel,Alexander Blumberg,Jonas Merkert,Nils Rack,Kaiwen Wang,Fabian Konstantinidis,Julian Truetsch,Carlos Fernandez,Annika Bätz,Kevin Rösch,Marlon Steiner,Willi Poh,Yinzhe Shen,Royden Wagner,Felix Hauser,Dominik Strutz,Jaime Villa,Gleb Stepanov,Holger Caesar,Ömer Şahin Taş,Frank Bieder,Jan-Hendrik Pauls,Christoph Stiller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KITScenes Multimodal 是一个欧洲自动驾驶多模态数据集，配备高保真传感器（全局快门相机、400m+激光雷达、4D成像雷达、冗余GNSS/INS）、最完整的HD地图（所有交通元素3D建模+拓扑连接），覆盖不规则街道布局的城市，并提出了四个空间学习基准任务。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集在传感器保真度、地图完整性或地理多样性方面存在不足，缺乏同时具备高精度传感器、完整HD地图以及多样化城市布局的公开数据集。

Method: 构建了一个全同步传感器套件（高分辨率全局快门相机、400m以上远程激光雷达、4D成像雷达、冗余GNSS/INS定位），制作了目前最完整的HD地图（所有驾驶相关交通元素3D建模至重投影精度级别，具备完整拓扑连接性），并在街道布局不规则、混合交通模式的城市中采集数据。

Result: 数据集通过开源软件自动驾驶试验验证了地图质量；首次在公开数据集中实现了所有交通元素（如交通灯）的3D重投影精确建模与拓扑连接；提出了在线HD地图构建、远距离深度估计、新视角合成和端到端驾驶四项基准。

Conclusion: KITScenes Multimodal 通过高保真传感器、最完整HD地图和地理多样性，填补了现有自动驾驶数据集的空白，并为具身AI的空间学习提供了新的基准任务。

Original Abstract: Existing autonomous driving datasets have enabled major progress, but fall short in sensor fidelity, map completeness, or geographic diversity. We present KITScenes Multimodal, a European dataset built around high-fidelity sensors and maps. Our fully synchronized sensor suite combines high-resolution global-shutter cameras, long-range lidar beyond 400m, 4D imaging radar, and redundant GNSS/INS localization. Our HD maps are, to our knowledge, the most complete of any sensor dataset, validated through autonomous driving trials on open-source software. For the first time in a public dataset, all driving-relevant traffic elements, such as traffic lights, are mapped in 3D to a reprojection-accurate level with full topological connectivity. Recorded in cities with irregular street layouts and mixed traffic modes, our dataset complements existing datasets by broadening the available geographic diversity. We also introduce four benchmarks, each advancing spatial learning for embodied AI: online HD map construction, long-range depth estimation, novel view synthesis, and end-to-end driving. Project page: https://kitscenes.com/

中文摘要翻译: 现有的自动驾驶数据集虽推动了重大进展，但在传感器保真度、地图完整性或地理多样性方面仍存在不足。我们提出了KITScenes Multimodal，一个基于高保真传感器和高精地图构建的欧洲数据集。我们的全同步传感器套件结合了高分辨率全局快门相机、超过400米的远程激光雷达、4D成像雷达以及冗余GNSS/INS定位系统。据我们所知，我们的高精地图是所有传感器数据集中最完整的，并通过开源软件上的自动驾驶试验进行了验证。在公开数据集中，我们首次将所有驾驶相关的交通元素（如交通灯）以重投影精确级别进行3D建模，并具备完整的拓扑连接性。我们的数据采集于街道布局不规则且交通模式混合的城市，通过拓宽可用的地理多样性来补充现有数据集。我们还引入了四项基准任务，每项任务均推进面向具身AI的空间学习：在线高精地图构建、远距离深度估计、新视角合成以及端到端驾驶。项目页面：https://kitscenes.com/

</details>


### [21] [Towards Compact Autonomous Driving Perception with Balanced Learning and Multi-sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02979)
*Oskar Natan,Jun Miura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种紧凑型深度多任务学习模型，能够在单次前向传播中同时处理语义分割、深度估计、LiDAR分割和鸟瞰图投影等多种自动驾驶感知任务，通过自适应损失加权和中间传感器融合技术，以更少的参数实现更优或相当的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自动驾驶感知模型通常需要多个独立模型分别处理不同任务（如语义分割、深度估计、LiDAR分割等），导致计算开销大、推理速度慢，且难以充分利用多传感器（RGB相机、DVS、LiDAR）之间的互补信息。本文旨在设计一个紧凑的统一模型，在单次前向传播中完成多个感知任务，同时解决多任务学习中的不平衡学习问题。

Method: 1) 提出紧凑型深度多任务学习模型，在单次前向传播中同时处理语义分割、深度估计、LiDAR分割和鸟瞰图投影；2) 设计自适应损失加权算法，解决多任务中因任务数量多导致的学习不平衡问题；3) 通过数据预处理和中间传感器融合技术，处理并融合来自RGB相机、动态视觉传感器（DVS）和LiDAR的多模态输入。

Result: 1) 消融实验表明，使用所提方法训练的模型变体性能更优；2) 对比实验显示，模型在参数量少得多的情况下仍保持更好的性能，推理更快且GPU内存占用更低；3) 在3个不同的CARLA仿真数据集和1个真实世界nuScenes-lidarseg数据集上的结果具有一致性。

Conclusion: 所提出的紧凑型多任务学习模型能够高效地处理多种自动驾驶感知任务，通过自适应损失加权和传感器融合技术实现了更好的性能-效率权衡，且跨数据集结果一致，具有实际应用价值。

Original Abstract: We present a novel compact deep multi-task learning model to handle various autonomous driving perception tasks in one forward pass. The model performs multiple views of semantic segmentation, depth estimation, light detection and ranging (LiDAR) segmentation, and bird's eye view projection simultaneously without being supported by other models. We also provide an adaptive loss weighting algorithm to tackle the imbalanced learning issue that occurred due to plenty of given tasks. Through data pre-processing and intermediate sensor fusion techniques, the model can process and combine multiple input modalities retrieved from RGB cameras, dynamic vision sensors (DVS), and LiDAR placed at several positions on the ego vehicle. Therefore, a better understanding of a dynamically changing environment can be achieved. Based on the ablation study, the model variant trained with our proposed method achieves a better performance. Furthermore, a comparative study is also conducted to clarify its performance and effectiveness against the combination of some recent models. As a result, our model maintains better performance even with much fewer parameters. Hence, the model can inference faster with less GPU memory utilization. Moreover, the result tends to be consistent in 3 different CARLA simulation datasets and 1 real-world nuScenes-lidarseg dataset. To support future research, we share codes and other files publicly at https://github.com/oskarnatan/compact-perception.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种新颖的紧凑型深度多任务学习模型，用于在单次前向传播中处理多种自动驾驶感知任务。该模型无需其他模型支持，即可同时执行多视图语义分割、深度估计、激光雷达（LiDAR）分割和鸟瞰图投影。我们还提供了一种自适应损失加权算法，以解决因任务数量多而导致的学习不平衡问题。通过数据预处理和中间传感器融合技术，该模型能够处理并融合来自RGB相机、动态视觉传感器（DVS）以及安装在自车多个位置的LiDAR所获取的多模态输入，从而实现对动态变化环境的更好理解。基于消融研究，使用我们提出的方法训练的模型变体取得了更优的性能。此外，我们还进行了对比研究，以阐明该模型相对于近期一些模型组合的性能和有效性。结果表明，即使在参数量少得多的情况下，我们的模型仍能保持更好的性能，因此推理速度更快且GPU内存占用更低。此外，该结果在3个不同的CARLA仿真数据集和1个真实世界的nuScenes-lidarseg数据集上具有一致性。为支持未来研究，我们在https://github.com/oskarnatan/compact-perception上公开共享代码和其他文件。

</details>


### [22] [MUSE: A Unified Agentic Harness for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03005)
*Jianglin Lu,Hailing Wang,Xu Ma,Qihua Dong,Mingyuan Zhang,Yizhou Wang,Yun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MUSE框架，通过改进纯执行脚手架（而非微调模型）来显著提升冻结多模态大语言模型在视觉空间规划、感知、推理等任务上的表现，发现许多模型失败源于执行层缺陷而非模型本身不足。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在人类能轻松完成的任务（如从截图导航迷宫、选择正确拼图）上仍然失败，现有研究多集中于重新训练模型，本文提出互补性问题：仅通过改进执行脚手架，能从冻结的MLLM中 elicite 出多少额外能力。

Method: 提出MUSE（多模态统一结构化执行框架），为任意现成MLLM包装可组合的模块，包括任务表示、视觉处理、感知工具使用、结构化解析、确定性验证和验证器引导的修复，无需任何模型重新训练。

Result: 在视觉空间规划、视觉感知、多模态推理和细粒度视觉辨别等多个基准上，使用多个最先进MLLM进行评估，MUSE在所有设置中均优于裸模型，在困难实例上提升最大。分析发现许多MLLM失败源于执行层缺陷而非模型能力不足，可通过验证器引导修复来解决。

Conclusion: 强调了智能体多模态执行框架作为关键但未被充分探索的设计维度，为改进MLLM提供了除模型中心优化之外的正交通道。

Original Abstract: Despite rapid progress, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) still fail on tasks that humans solve effortlessly, such as navigating a grid maze from a screenshot or selecting the correct puzzle piece. Rather than retraining the model, we ask a complementary question: how much capability can be elicited from a frozen MLLM purely by improving the execution scaffold around it? We introduce MUSE, a multimodal unified structured execution harness that wraps any off-the-shelf MLLM with composable modules for task representation, visual processing, perception tool use, structured parsing, deterministic verification, and verifier-guided repair, without any model retraining. We evaluate MUSE across diverse benchmarks spanning visual spatial planning, visual perception, multimodal reasoning, and fine-grained visual discrimination, using multiple state-of-the-art MLLMs. MUSE delivers consistent gains over the bare model in all settings, with the largest jumps on challenging instances. Further analysis reveals that many MLLM failures arise from harness-level shortcomings rather than fundamental model deficits, and can be addressed through verifier-guided repair without touching the model. These findings highlight the agentic multimodal harness as a critical yet underexplored design dimension, offering an orthogonal avenue for improving MLLMs beyond model-centric optimization.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管取得了快速进展，多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在人类可以轻松完成的任务上仍然失败，例如从屏幕截图中导航网格迷宫或选择正确的拼图块。本文不重新训练模型，而是提出一个互补性问题：仅通过改进执行脚手架，能从冻结的MLLM中 elicite 出多少能力？我们提出了MUSE，一个多模态统一结构化执行框架，它为任意现成的MLLM包装了可组合的模块，用于任务表示、视觉处理、感知工具使用、结构化解析、确定性验证和验证器引导的修复，无需任何模型重新训练。我们在涵盖视觉空间规划、视觉感知、多模态推理和细粒度视觉辨别等多个基准上，使用多个最先进的MLLM评估了MUSE。在所有设置中，MUSE相较于裸模型都取得了持续的提升，在困难实例上提升幅度最大。进一步的分析表明，许多MLLM的失败源于执行层的缺陷，而非根本性的模型能力不足，并且可以通过验证器引导的修复来解决，无需修改模型。这些发现强调了智能体多模态执行框架是一个关键但尚未被充分探索的设计维度，为改进MLLM提供了除模型中心优化之外的正交通道。

</details>


### [23] [FCUS-rPPG: A Fast-Converging Unsupervised Framework for Remote Photoplethysmography via Gradient Oscillation Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03050)
*Jiajie Li,Yu Liu,Rencheng Song,Xun Chen,Juan Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FCUS-rPPG，一种快速收敛的无监督远程光电容积描记法框架，通过光谱共享骨干网络和统一优化策略（梯度掩蔽、损失景观平滑、零空间正则化），仅需一个训练周期即可达到SOTA性能，显著优于传统需数十至数百周期的方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无监督rPPG方法因噪声和不稳定梯度导致收敛慢、跨域泛化能力有限，亟需一种快速收敛且强泛化的解决方案。

Method: 1) 设计光谱共享骨干网络（spectrally shared backbone）实现BVP特征解耦并提升优化效率；2) 提出统一优化框架，包含：后验证掩蔽机制（过滤误导梯度）、扰动损失景观平滑策略（导向平坦极小值）、噪声感知零空间正则化（约束特征更新至噪声子空间正交补）。

Result: 在五个数据集上的实验表明，FCUS-rPPG仅需一个训练周期，而现有方法通常需要数十到数百个周期；在跨数据集评估中一致达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 该研究为无监督rPPG的实际部署提供了一种高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) enables non-contact extraction of blood volume pulse (BVP) signals using consumer-grade cameras. Recent unsupervised rPPG methods learn BVP representations without requiring ground-truth physiological annotations, yet their optimization is often hindered by noisy and unstable gradients, resulting in slow convergence and limited cross-domain generalization. In this paper, we propose FCUS-rPPG, a fast-converging unsupervised rPPG framework with strong generalization capability. Motivated by the observation that BVP representations exhibit both multi-spectral covariation and low-dimensional manifold structure, we design a spectrally shared backbone that facilitates BVP feature disentanglement while improving optimization efficiency. To jointly enhance convergence stability and generalization performance, we further develop a unified optimization framework operating at the gradient, loss-landscape, and feature-representation levels. Specifically, a post-verification masking mechanism filters out misleading gradients according to the weak-amplitude physiological prior of BVP signals; a perturbation-based loss landscape smoothing strategy steers optimization toward more generalizable flat minima; and a noise-aware null-space regularization constrains feature updates to the orthogonal complement of the noise subspace, thereby mitigating noise-induced representation drift. Extensive experiments on five datasets demonstrate that FCUS-rPPG requires only one training epoch, whereas existing methods typically require tens to hundreds of epochs. Notably, FCUS-rPPG consistently achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance in cross-dataset evaluations. This study provides an efficient and robust solution to the real-world deployment of unsupervised rPPG. The source code will be publicly available at https://github.com/JiaJieLee/FCUS-rPPG.

中文摘要翻译: 远程光电容积描记法（rPPG）能够利用消费级摄像头以非接触方式提取血容量脉冲（BVP）信号。现有的无监督rPPG方法无需真实生理标注即可学习BVP表示，但其优化过程常受到噪声和不稳定梯度的干扰，导致收敛缓慢且跨域泛化能力有限。本文提出FCUS-rPPG，一种快速收敛且具备强泛化能力的无监督rPPG框架。受BVP表示同时具有多光谱协变性和低维流形结构的观察启发，我们设计了光谱共享骨干网络，以促进BVP特征解耦并提升优化效率。为进一步联合提升收敛稳定性和泛化性能，我们开发了一个在梯度、损失景观和特征表示三个层面协同优化的统一框架。具体而言：后验证掩蔽机制根据BVP信号的弱幅值生理先验过滤误导性梯度；基于扰动的损失景观平滑策略引导优化朝向更具泛化性的平坦极小值；噪声感知零空间正则化将特征更新约束在噪声子空间的正交补空间内，从而减轻噪声引起的表示漂移。在五个数据集上的大量实验表明，FCUS-rPPG仅需一个训练周期，而现有方法通常需要数十到数百个周期。值得注意的是，FCUS-rPPG在跨数据集评估中持续达到最优（SOTA）性能。本研究为无监督rPPG的实际部署提供了一种高效且鲁棒的解决方案。源代码将在https://github.com/JiaJieLee/FCUS-rPPG 公开提供。

</details>


### [24] [ROBUST-WT: Robust Uncertainty-aware Segmentation Transform via Whitening and Training Enhancements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03069)
*Aqsa Naseer,Maryam Bibi,Syeda Samiya Urooj,Muhammad Khurram Shahzad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对WT-PSE跨域医学图像分割框架的四个局限性（训练增强不足、损失函数对边缘噪声敏感、缺少损失加权调度、缺乏消融控制开关），提出了四项改进策略，在眼底视盘分割任务上取得了性能提升，Dice达到0.956，ASD为13.31。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 原始WT-PSE框架在跨域医学图像分割中存在四个关键局限：训练增强不足以模拟真实扫描仪变化、逐像素二值交叉熵损失对边缘噪声敏感、缺乏调度式损失加权策略导致早期训练不稳定、缺少消融控制开关用于科学对比。本文旨在通过训练层面的改进克服这些局限，进一步提升分割性能。

Method: 提出四项改进：(1)域自适应增强，包括随机擦除、伽马校正和椒盐噪声；(2)混合BCE和Dice损失函数，实现噪声条件下的边缘感知分割；(3)基于课程学习的Dice权重调度策略；(4)命令行控制标志用于系统消融研究。

Result: 在眼底视盘分割基准上，改进后的模型最终epoch视盘Dice得分为0.956，ASD得分为13.31，优于基线的第5轮Dice得分0.939。

Conclusion: 训练层面的改进可以在不修改底层WT-PSE架构的情况下提供一致的性能提升。

Original Abstract: Generalized segmentation of medical images prevents performance degradation when different imaging devices and clinical protocols are used across multiple domains. The Whitening Transform-based Probabilistic Shape Regularization Extractor (WT-PSE), published in IEEE Transactions on Medical Imaging in 2024, addresses this challenge by employing feature decorrelation and Wasserstein distance-based knowledge distillation to achieve robust cross-domain segmentation. This study systematically examines improvements to the WT-PSE learning framework. Four limitations in the original implementation are identified: limited training augmentations that fail to simulate real scanner variations, reliance on per-pixel binary cross-entropy loss that is sensitive to edge noise, the absence of a scheduled loss weighting strategy that may destabilize early training, and the lack of ablation switches for controlled scientific comparison. To address these issues, we propose four enhancements: (1) domain-adaptive augmentation including random erasing, gamma correction, and salt-and-pepper noise; (2) a hybrid BCE and Dice loss function for improved edge-aware segmentation under noisy conditions; (3) a curriculum-based Dice weight scheduling strategy; and (4) command-line control flags for systematic ablation studies. Experiments on the fundus optic disc segmentation benchmark demonstrate that the improved pipeline achieves a final epoch optic-disc Dice score of 0.956 and an ASD score of 13.31, outperforming the baseline epoch-5 Dice score of 0.939. These results indicate that training-level improvements can provide consistent performance gains without modifying the underlying WT-PSE architecture.

中文摘要翻译: 医学图像的广义分割能够防止在使用不同成像设备和临床协议的多域场景中出现性能下降。发表于IEEE Transactions on Medical Imaging（2024）的基于白化变换的概率形状正则化提取器（WT-PSE）通过特征去相关和基于Wasserstein距离的知识蒸馏来解决这一挑战，实现了鲁棒的跨域分割。本研究系统性地审视了对WT-PSE学习框架的改进。原始实现中存在四个局限性：有限的训练增强无法模拟真实的扫描仪变化、依赖对边缘噪声敏感的逐像素二值交叉熵损失、缺少可能破坏早期训练稳定性的调度式损失加权策略、以及缺乏用于受控科学比较的消融开关。为解决这些问题，我们提出四项增强：(1)域自适应增强，包括随机擦除、伽马校正和椒盐噪声；(2)混合BCE和Dice损失函数，以改善噪声条件下的边缘感知分割；(3)基于课程学习的Dice权重调度策略；(4)用于系统消融研究的命令行控制标志。在眼底视盘分割基准上的实验表明，改进后的流程在最终epoch上达到了0.956的视盘Dice得分和13.31的ASD得分，优于基线第5轮的0.939 Dice得分。这些结果表明，训练层面的改进可以在不修改底层WT-PSE架构的情况下提供一致的性能提升。

</details>


### [25] [TGV-KV: Text-Grounded KV Eviction for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03075)
*Jizhihui Liu,Ruizi Han,Miao Zhang,Rui Shao,Xuebo Liu,Weili Guan,Yaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于文本引导的KV缓存驱逐方法TGV-KV，通过文本-视觉预算分配、文本加权排序和文本优先保留三个子模块，在保持99.2%全KV精度的同时将端到端吞吐量提升52.6%，有效解决了视觉-语言模型中视觉模态冗余导致的KV缓存内存膨胀问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有KV缓存驱逐方法大多针对语言模型设计，忽视了视觉与文本之间的固有模态差异，导致在视觉-语言模型中性能显著下降。本文通过系统分析VLM中的模态差距，认为视觉信息的重要性应以文本引导为基础。

Method: TGV-KV包含三个子模块：(1) 文本-视觉预算分配(TVB)，基于互信息为每层分配预算；(2) 文本加权排序(TWR)，评估文本优先级并基于加权文本-图像注意力对视觉重要性进行排序；(3) 文本优先保留(TPR)策略，战略性保留文本KV以避免信息严重丢失。

Result: 在五种不同大小和架构的模型上评估，TGV-KV在LLaVA-NeXT的VizWiz-VQA任务上保持了99.2%的全KV准确率，在极端保留预算5%下将端到端吞吐量提升了52.6%。

Conclusion: TGV-KV通过文本引导的视觉信息重要性评估，在显著降低KV缓存内存消耗的同时，最大限度地保持了模型性能，为VLM的高效推理提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) inherit the auto-regressive generation paradigm and cache the keys and values (KV) of all previous tokens to accelerate inference, resulting in memory consumption that scales linearly with context length. This issue is particularly pronounced in VLMs due to substantial redundancy in the visual modality. Although KV cache eviction approaches can effectively reduce inference memory, they often incur significant performance degradation in VLMs, as most are designed for language models and overlook the inherent gap between text and vision. By systematically analyzing the modality gap in VLMs in this work, we argue that the importance of visual information should be grounded in textual guidance and accordingly propose a Text-Grounded KV Eviction method for VLMs (TGV-KV). TGV-KV comprises three submodules: (1) Text-Vision Budgeting (TVB) assigns budget to each layer based on the mutual information interaction. (2) Text-Weighted Ranking (TWR) assesses the priority of text and ranks vision importance based on weighted text-image attention. (3) Text-Prioritised Retention (TPR) policy strategically preserves text KV to avoid acute information loss. We evaluate TGV-KV across five models with different sizes and architectures, showing that TGV-KV preserves 99.2% full-KV accuracy on the VizWiz-VQA task with LLaVA-NeXT and boosts end-to-end throughput by 52.6% with an extreme retention budget of 5%. Code is available at https://github.com/Danielement321/TGV-KV.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）继承了自回归生成范式，并缓存所有先前token的键和值（KV）以加速推理，导致内存消耗随上下文长度线性增长。由于视觉模态中存在大量冗余，这一问题在VLM中尤为突出。尽管KV缓存驱逐方法可以有效减少推理内存，但它们在VLM中往往导致显著的性能下降，因为这些方法大多是为语言模型设计的，忽略了文本与视觉之间的固有差距。通过在本工作中系统分析VLM中的模态差距，我们认为视觉信息的重要性应以文本引导为基础，并据此提出一种面向VLM的基于文本引导的KV驱逐方法（TGV-KV）。TGV-KV包含三个子模块：（1）文本-视觉预算分配（TVB），基于互信息交互为每层分配预算；（2）文本加权排序（TWR），评估文本的优先级并基于加权的文本-图像注意力对视觉重要性进行排序；（3）文本优先保留（TPR）策略，战略性保留文本KV以避免严重的信息丢失。我们在五种不同大小和架构的模型上评估了TGV-KV，结果显示TGV-KV在LLaVA-NeXT的VizWiz-VQA任务上保持了99.2%的全KV准确率，并在极端保留预算5%下将端到端吞吐量提升了52.6%。代码已开源：https://github.com/Danielement321/TGV-KV。

</details>


### [26] [Hierarchical Federated Learning with Dynamic Clustering and Adaptive Regularization for Robust Infrastructure Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03084)
*Yuhu Feng,Keisuke Maeda,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层联邦学习框架，通过宏观动态梯度聚类和微观自适应邻近正则化，解决结构健康监测中跨结构类型和本地数据分布的双重异质性挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 数据驱动的计算机视觉SHM模型因隐私和安全法规面临数据孤岛困境，现有联邦学习在应用于全国性基础设施网络时受限于"双重异质性"：宏观层面不同结构类型间的物理差异，以及微观层面本地数据集内的统计不平衡。

Method: 提出分层联邦学习框架，包含两层协同优化策略：宏观层采用动态梯度聚类机制，根据结构退化轨迹自动将分布式客户端聚合为专家群组；微观层设计DRAPR模块，通过实时计算每个客户端的统计非独立同分布强度分数，基于标签偏斜度和梯度发散度自适应调节邻近惩罚，从而校准本地更新、减轻客户端漂移并防止少数损坏类别的灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在大规模真实结构检测数据集上的综合评估表明，宏观聚类与微观正则化的分层集成能够有效中和双重异质性，为复杂基础设施检测生成高度鲁棒且专业化的诊断模型。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层联邦学习框架通过宏观聚类和微观正则化的协同机制成功解决了双重异质性挑战，实现了面向复杂基础设施检测的高度鲁棒和专业化诊断模型。

Original Abstract: The deployment of data-driven computer vision models for structural health monitoring (SHM) is heavily constrained by the data silo dilemma due to stringent privacy and security regulations. While federated learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving collaborative alternative, its application to nationwide infrastructure networks is severely hindered by the challenge of ``double heterogeneity'': macro-level physical divergence across disparate structural types and micro-level statistical imbalances within local datasets. To overcome this challenge, this paper proposes a novel hierarchical federated learning framework. The framework orchestrates a synergistic two-tier optimization strategy. At the macro-level, a dynamic gradient-based clustering mechanism autonomously aggregates distributed clients into specialized expert groups based on their structural degradation trajectories, circumventing the need for prior geographical metadata. Concurrently, at the micro-level, an intra-cluster Dynamic Region-Adaptive Proximal Regularization (DRAPR) module computes a real-time statistical Non-IID Intensity Score for each client. By adaptively modulating a proximal penalty based on local label skewness and gradient divergence, DRAPR effectively calibrates local updates, mitigates client drift, and prevents the catastrophic forgetting of minority damage classes. Comprehensive evaluations on a large-scale, real-world structural inspection dataset demonstrate that the hierarchical integration of macro-clustering and micro-regularization successfully neutralizes dual-level heterogeneity, yielding highly robust and specialized diagnostic models for complex infrastructure inspection.

中文摘要翻译: 在结构健康监测(SHM)中，数据驱动的计算机视觉模型的部署受到数据孤岛困境的严重制约，其原因在于严格的隐私和安全法规。尽管联邦学习(FL)提供了一种保护隐私的协作替代方案，但其在全国性基础设施网络中的应用却受到"双重异质性"挑战的严重阻碍：宏观层面不同结构类型间的物理差异，以及微观层面本地数据集内的统计不平衡。为克服这一挑战，本文提出了一种新颖的分层联邦学习框架。该框架编排了一种协同的双层优化策略。在宏观层面，一种基于动态梯度聚类的机制根据结构退化轨迹自动将分布式客户端聚合成专门的专家群组，从而规避了对先验地理元数据的需求。同时，在微观层面，一个簇内动态区域自适应邻近正则化(DRAPR)模块为每个客户端计算实时的统计非独立同分布强度分数。通过基于局部标签偏斜度和梯度发散度自适应调节邻近惩罚，DRAPR有效校准了本地更新，减轻了客户端漂移，并防止了少数损坏类别的灾难性遗忘。在大规模真实结构检测数据集上的综合评估表明，宏观聚类与微观正则化的分层集成成功中和了双重异质性，为复杂基础设施检测生成了高度鲁棒且专业化的诊断模型。

</details>


### [27] [Zero-Shot 3D Question Answering via Hierarchical View-to-Token Transportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03100)
*Dongsheng Wang,Dawei Su,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KeyVT，一种在视图和令牌两级层次化收集输入上下文的方法，通过结合像素特征与相机参数评估视图重要性，并在最优传输框架下识别代表性令牌，以在有限输入预算下保留更多任务相关3D细节，实现零样本3D场景理解性能的提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有零样本3D场景理解方法依赖2D视觉语言模型(VLMs)，通过采样多个2D视图来回答3D点云问题。该范式下输入上下文质量至关重要，但如何在有限的输入预算（视图数量/令牌数量）内尽可能多地保留与任务相关的3D细节是一个关键挑战。

Method: 提出KeyVT，一种层次化输入上下文收集方法。1) 视图级：结合像素特征与相机参数，基于语义内容和几何位置评估视图重要性，选择空间一致且任务相关的视图。2) 令牌级：在最优传输(OT)框架下，将视图令牌和关键令牌视为嵌入空间中的两个离散分布，通过最小化OT距离来识别覆盖所有视图特征的代表性令牌。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的基准数据集上评估，KeyVT相比现有免调优方法有显著改进，性能可与基于训练的方法相媲美。

Conclusion: 提出的层次化视图-令牌选择机制能有效在有限输入预算下保留3D场景的任务相关细节，提升了零样本3D场景理解的性能。

Original Abstract: Recently, zero-shot 3D scene understanding via 2D Vision-Language Models (VLMs) has gained increasing research interest due to their promising spatial reasoning capabilities. Typically, multiple 2D views are sampled from a 3D point cloud and fed into pre-trained VLMs to answer a given question. This paradigm highlights the critical role of input context quality and raises the challenge of retaining as many task-relevant 3D details as possible under a limited input budget. We propose \texttt{KeyVT}, a hierarchical approach for input context collection at both the view and token levels. Specifically, we combine pixel features with camera parameters and assess view importance based on both semantic content and geometric position, resulting in spatially consistent and task-relevant views. Furthermore, we address redundancy among patches across selected views by identifying representative tokens under the optimal transport (OT) framework, where view tokens and key tokens are formulated as two discrete distributions in the embedding space. These key tokens are expected to cover all view features by minimizing the OT distance. We evaluate our framework on three widely used benchmarks, demonstrating significant improvements over existing tuning-free methods and performance comparable to training-based approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 近期，通过2D视觉-语言模型(VLMs)进行零样本3D场景理解因其有前景的空间推理能力而受到越来越多的研究关注。通常，从3D点云中采样多个2D视图并输入到预训练的VLMs来回答给定问题。该范式突显了输入上下文质量的关键作用，并提出了在有限输入预算下尽可能多地保留与任务相关的3D细节的挑战。我们提出\texttt{KeyVT}，一种在视图和令牌两个层次上进行输入上下文收集的层次化方法。具体而言，我们将像素特征与相机参数相结合，并基于语义内容和几何位置评估视图重要性，从而获得空间一致且与任务相关的视图。此外，我们通过最优传输(OT)框架识别代表性令牌来解决所选视图中各分块间的冗余问题，其中视图令牌和关键令牌被视为嵌入空间中的两个离散分布。这些关键令牌通过最小化OT距离来覆盖所有视图特征。我们在三个广泛使用的基准数据集上评估了所提框架，结果表明其相比现有免调优方法有显著改进，且性能可与基于训练的方法相媲美。

</details>


### [28] [Inverting the Generation Process of Denoising Diffusion Implicit Models: Empirical Evaluation and a Novel Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03111)
*Yan Zeng,Masanori Suganuma,Takayuki Okatani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合方法，结合梯度下降直接求逆和不动点法，用于从DDIM生成图像中准确恢复初始潜变量（噪声图），在三个数据集上显著优于现有方法，并引入了自插值测试作为新的评估指标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法在逆转DDIM图像生成过程以恢复初始潜变量时精度不足，且虽然能较好地完成重建任务，但无法准确预测初始潜变量，限制了扩散模型在图像生成和编辑中的应用。

Method: 提出混合求逆方法：第一步采用基于梯度下降的直接求逆，后续步骤采用不动点法进行迭代优化。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在初始潜变量预测和重建精度上均显著优于现有方法；自插值测试进一步揭示，现有方法重建尚可但潜变量预测失败，而本方法在所有指标上均表现最佳。

Conclusion: 本文提出的混合求逆方法能有效提升DDIM潜变量恢复的准确性，为扩散模型在图像生成与编辑中的应用提供了有价值的见解和改进方向。

Original Abstract: This paper studies the problem of inverting the DDIM image generation process to recover latent variables, particularly the initial noise map, from a generated image. Existing methods often struggle with accuracy in this task. We propose a novel hybrid approach that combines direct inversion via gradient descent for the first step, followed by a fixed-point method for subsequent steps. Empirical evaluations across three datasets demonstrate that our method significantly improves the prediction of initial latent variables while achieving superior reconstruction accuracy. Additionally, we introduce a new evaluation, called the self-interpolation test, which assesses the quality of images generated from interpolated points between the true and predicted latent maps, offering deeper insights into performance. Our results reveal that while existing methods perform reasonably well in reconstruction, they consistently fail to accurately predict the initial latent variables, resulting in poor performance on the self-interpolation test. In contrast, our method outperforms all others across all metrics, providing valuable insights into diffusion models and enhancing their applications in image generation and editing.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究从DDIM图像生成过程中逆向恢复潜在变量（特别是初始噪声图）的问题。现有方法在此任务中通常精度不足。我们提出了一种新颖的混合方法，将第一步的基于梯度下降的直接求逆与后续步骤的不动点法相结合。在三个数据集上的经验评估表明，我们的方法显著改进了初始潜在变量的预测，同时实现了更优的重建精度。此外，我们引入了一项名为自插值测试的新评估方法，用于评估从真实潜变量图与预测潜变量图之间的插值点生成图像的质量，从而提供对性能更深入的洞察。我们的结果表明，尽管现有方法在重建方面表现尚可，但它们始终无法准确预测初始潜在变量，导致在自插值测试中表现不佳。相比之下，我们的方法在所有指标上均优于其他方法，为扩散模型提供了有价值的见解，并增强了其在图像生成与编辑中的应用。

</details>


### [29] [FAF-CD: Frequency-Aware Fusion for Change Detection under Imperfect Multimodal Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03114)
*Yufan Wang,Sokratis Makrogiannis,Chandra Kambhamettu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FAF-CD，一种频率感知的混合框架，用于异质遥感变化检测，在多种扰动条件下取得SOTA性能，同时降低计算开销。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现实遥感变化检测常面临异质观测（如跨传感器、光照、季节差异），特别是EO-SAR灾害制图中，非目标变化（如光照、季节差异）容易与结构损伤混淆，现有方法难以有效区分真实变化与干扰伪变化。

Method: 提出FAF-CD框架，包含：(1) DINOv3预训练的ConvNeXt编码器；(2) 线性复杂度VMamba解码器；(3) 矫正感知三分支融合模块，结合可变形空间对齐、傅里叶变换和哈尔小波变换比较，并通过自适应门控聚合多尺度互补线索。

Result: 在BRIGHT验证集上，异质EO-SAR适配相比NeXt2Former-CD提升干净和扰动tc-mIoU/tc-mAP；在LEVIR-CD和WHU-CD上分别达到0.924和0.955的cF1；在两种二值数据集扰动测试中获得最佳平均扰动cIoU/cF1；相比NeXt2Former-CD减少约24 GFLOPs计算量。

Conclusion: FAF-CD在异质遥感变化检测中兼顾精度与效率，对扰动具有鲁棒性，且泛化能力强，可同时适用于异质EO-SAR和同质光学变化检测任务。

Original Abstract: Remote sensing change detection for real-world monitoring often relies on imperfect heterogeneous observations, where pre- and post-event images may be asynchronous, cross-sensor, or affected by illumination, seasonal, and modality shifts. This setting is especially challenging for EO-SAR disaster mapping, where nuisance variation can resemble structural damage. We propose FAF-CD, a frequency-aware hybrid framework with a DINOv3-pretrained ConvNeXt encoder and a linear-complexity VMamba-based decoder. Its rectification-aware tri-branch fusion module combines deformable spatial alignment with Fourier and Haar-wavelet comparisons, using adaptive gating to aggregate complementary cues across scales. On BRIGHT validation, a matched heterogeneous EO-SAR adaptation improves clean and perturbed tc-mIoU/tc-mAP over NeXt2Former-CD. FAF-CD also generalizes to binary optical CD, achieving 0.924 cF1 on LEVIR-CD and 0.955 cF1 on WHU-CD, and obtains the best average perturbed cIoU/cF1 on both binary datasets among M-CD and NeXt2Former-CD under pseudo-change-aligned stress tests. It further reduces cost by approximately 24 GFLOPs relative to NeXt2Former-CD while maintaining or improving accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 面向真实世界中监测任务的遥感变化检测通常依赖于不完备的异质观测数据，其中事件前与事件后的图像可能存在异步采集、跨传感器差异，或受到光照、季节及模态变化的影响。这种设置在EO-SAR灾害制图场景中尤为具有挑战性，因为非目标扰动变化可能与结构损伤表现相似。本文提出FAF-CD，一种频率感知的混合框架，采用DINOv3预训练的ConvNeXt编码器和线性复杂度的VMamba解码器。其矫正感知三分支融合模块将可变形空间对齐与傅里叶变换及哈尔小波变换比较相结合，并通过自适应门控机制来聚合跨尺度的互补线索。在BRIGHT验证集上，匹配的异质EO-SAR适配相比NeXt2Former-CD提升了干净和扰动条件下的tc-mIoU/tc-mAP指标。FAF-CD同样能够泛化到二值光学变化检测任务，在LEVIR-CD上达到0.924的cF1，在WHU-CD上达到0.955的cF1，并在两种二值数据集上的伪变化对齐压力测试中，相比M-CD和NeXt2Former-CD取得了最佳的平均扰动cIoU/cF1。此外，相对于NeXt2Former-CD，FAF-CD在保持或提升精度的同时，减少了约24 GFLOPs的计算开销。

</details>


### [30] [GuidedBridge: Training-freely Improving Bridge Models with Prior Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03119)
*Zehua Chen,Yucheng Yang,Binjie Yuan,Kaiwen Zheng,Jun S. Liu,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Prior Guidance (PG)的无训练桥接引导方法，通过引入弱先验并与已见先验对比来增强先验利用，并设计了频率调制先验引导(FMPG)以适配桥接生成动力学，在多种图像翻译任务上一致提升了预训练桥接模型的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 先前基于分类器无引导(CFG)和自动引导(AG)的方法在扩散模型的噪声到数据生成中取得了进展，而桥接模型引入了数据到数据的生成过程，可以利用有指导性的干净先验。受之前方法通过创建去噪结果之间的质量差异作为引导的启发，本文旨在为桥接模型提出一种无需额外训练、能够有效利用先验信息的引导方法。

Method: 提出Prior Guidance (PG)方法：在桥接预训练中引入未见过的弱先验，阻碍先验利用以降低去噪效果；然后将其与已见先验对比，通过缩放因子突出并增强先验利用。进一步分析桥接过程中的先验利用机制，设计频率调制先验引导(FMPG)，根据桥接生成动力学对低频和高频分量分别调整引导尺度。针对图像修复任务，提出CFG-FMPG级联框架，先通过CFG生成带噪隐表示，再将其作为生成先验与FMPG结合使用。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的PG方法在多种图像翻译任务上一致地提升了预训练桥接模型的性能。

Conclusion: PG系列方法无需额外训练即可有效增强桥接模型中的先验利用，FMPG通过频率自适应引导进一步提升了生成质量，CFG-FMPG框架在不降低推理效率的情况下实现了CFG和FMPG的优势互补。

Original Abstract: Guidance methods, such as classifier-free guidance (CFG) and auto-guidance (AG), have advanced noise-to-data generation in diffusion models. Recently, bridge models have introduced a data-to-data generative process that can exploit an instructive clean prior. In this work, inspired by previous methods creating quality difference between denoising results as guidance, we propose a training-free bridge guidance method, termed Prior Guidance (PG). Specifically, we introduce a weak prior, which is unseen during bridge pre-training, hindering prior exploitation and thereby degrading denoising result. Then, we contrast it with the seen prior to highlight and enhance prior exploitation via a scaling factor. Moreover, we analyze the underlying mechanism of prior exploitation in the bridge process and design frequency-modulated prior guidance (FMPG), which tailors the guidance scale to low- and high-frequency bands coherent with bridge generative dynamics. To address prior exploitation in image in-painting, we develop a cascaded framework, CFG-FMPG, which first generates a noisy hidden representation via CFG and then exploits it as a generative prior with FMPG, fulfilling their complementary strengths without compromising inference efficiency. Experiments demonstrate that our PG methods consistently improve pre-trained bridge models across diverse image translation tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 引导方法，如无分类器引导(CFG)和自动引导(AG)，推动了扩散模型中从噪声到数据生成的发展。近期，桥接模型引入了一种数据到数据的生成过程，可以利用有指导性的干净先验。在本工作中，受先前通过创建去噪结果间的质量差异作为引导的方法启发，我们提出了一种无需训练的桥接引导方法，称为先验引导(Prior Guidance, PG)。具体而言，我们引入一个弱先验，该先验在桥接预训练期间未见，阻碍先验利用从而降低去噪结果；然后，我们将它与已见先验进行对比，通过缩放因子突出并增强先验利用。此外，我们分析了桥接过程中先验利用的底层机制，并设计了频率调制先验引导(FMPG)，该方法将引导尺度适配到与桥接生成动力学相一致的低频和高频频带上。为解决图像修复中的先验利用问题，我们开发了一个级联框架CFG-FMPG，该框架首先通过CFG生成带噪隐表示，然后将其作为生成先验与FMPG结合使用，在不牺牲推理效率的情况下实现了两者的互补优势。实验表明，我们的PG方法在多种图像翻译任务上一致地提升了预训练桥接模型的性能。

</details>


### [31] [KC-3DGS: Kurtosis-Constrained Gaussian Splatting for High-Fidelity View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03120)
*Vivekjyoti Banerjee,Abhay Yadav,Rama Chellappa,Aniket Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KC-3DGS通过在小波域中引入基于自然图像统计的三种损失函数（多尺度小波系数对齐损失、监督峰度集中损失、跨频带协方差惩罚），增强了3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）在稀疏视图和密集视图下的感知质量，作为一种即插即用的正则化策略可无缝集成到现有3DGS管线中。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准像素空间损失（L1、SSIM）只约束整体重建误差，允许优化过程在不同频率尺度上重新分配误差，导致过平滑和结构伪影，尤其在监督有限的稀疏视图设置中更为严重。

Method: 提出KC-3DGS，包含三个组件：(1) 多尺度小波系数对齐损失，显式惩罚缺失的高频细节；(2) 监督峰度集中损失，鼓励渲染图像的频率统计（重尾分布）与真实图像匹配；(3) 跨频带协方差惩罚，促进频率特化。理论分析表明像素空间损失存在一族不可区分的扰动，而联合目标函数排除了退化解。

Result: 在MipNeRF360、Tanks&Temples、MVImgNet、DeepBlending和WRIVA-ULTRRA数据集上一致提升了感知质量。在WRIVA-ULTRRA户外数据集上DreamSim提升9.48%，同时PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS均有改善。在仅12张训练图像的稀疏视图设置下，MipNeRF360上PSNR提升达0.5 dB。

Conclusion: KC-3DGS作为一种即插即用的正则化策略，能无缝集成到现有3DGS管线中，显著提升渲染图像的感知质量，尤其在户外和稀疏视图等挑战性场景下表现突出。

Original Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables real-time novel view synthesis by representing scenes as collections of anisotropic Gaussians optimized via differentiable rasterization. However, standard pixel-space losses (L1, SSIM) constrain only aggregate reconstruction error, permitting the optimization to redistribute error across frequency scales. This leads to oversmoothing and structural artifacts, particularly in sparse-view settings where supervision is limited. We propose KC-3DGS, which augments 3DGS training with wavelet-domain supervision based on natural image statistics. Our method combines three components: (1) a multi-scale wavelet coefficient alignment loss that explicitly penalizes missing high-frequency detail, (2) a supervised kurtosis concentration loss that encourages rendered images to match the heavy-tailed frequency statistics of ground-truth images, and (3) a cross-band covariance penalty that promotes frequency specialization. We provide theoretical analysis showing that pixel-space losses admit a family of indistinguishable perturbations under wavelet redistribution, and that our joint objective excludes degenerate solutions. Experiments across MipNeRF360, Tanks&Temples, MVImgNet, DeepBlending, and WRIVA-ULTRRA demonstrate consistent improvements in perceptual quality. On the challenging WRIVA-ULTRRA outdoor dataset, KC-3DGS achieves a 9.48% improvement in DreamSim while also improving PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS. In sparse-view settings with only 12 training images, our method improves PSNR by up to 0.5 dB on MipNeRF360 while maintaining perceptual quality. The approach integrates seamlessly into existing 3DGS pipelines as a plug-and-play regularization strategy.

中文摘要翻译: 3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）通过将场景表示为各向异性高斯分布的集合，并经由可微分光栅化进行优化，实现了实时的新视角合成。然而，标准像素空间损失（L1、SSIM）仅约束整体重建误差，允许优化过程在不同频率尺度上重新分配误差，导致过平滑和结构伪影，尤其在监督有限的稀疏视图设置中更为严重。我们提出KC-3DGS，该方法基于自然图像统计，以小波域监督增强3DGS训练。我们的方法包含三个组件：(1) 多尺度小波系数对齐损失，显式惩罚缺失的高频细节；(2) 监督峰度集中损失，鼓励渲染图像匹配真实图像的重尾频率统计特性；(3) 跨频带协方差惩罚，促进频率特化。我们提供的理论分析表明，像素空间损失在小波重分布下允许一族不可区分的扰动，而我们的联合目标函数排除了退化解。在MipNeRF360、Tanks&Temples、MVImgNet、DeepBlending和WRIVA-ULTRRA数据集上的实验一致地提升了感知质量。在具有挑战性的WRIVA-ULTRRA户外数据集上，KC-3DGS在DreamSim指标上提升了9.48%，同时PSNR、SSIM和LPIPS也均有改善。在仅12张训练图像的稀疏视图设置下，我们的方法在MipNeRF360上将PSNR提升了0.5 dB，同时保持了感知质量。该方法作为一种即插即用的正则化策略，可无缝集成到现有的3DGS管线中。

</details>


### [32] [Disentangling Visual and Factual Correctness in LVLMs' Visualization Literacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03142)
*Soohyun Lee,Jaeyoung Kim,Seokhyeon Park,Sihyeon Lee,Jiwon Song,Bohyoung Kim,Hyunjoo Song,Jinwook Seo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CVLAT框架，通过反事实可视化测试分离LVLM的视觉推理与事实记忆，发现许多模型的高视觉准确率实为事实回忆，并揭示了模型在视觉证据与事实先验冲突时的仲裁机制差异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有评估无法区分LVLM的回答是基于真实视觉推理还是训练中学到的事实先验，导致对模型可视化理解能力的误判。

Method: 提出Counterfactual Visualization Literacy Assessment Test (CVLAT)，包含能力归一化的仲裁指标和视觉-事实依赖指数（VFRI），通过构建反事实图表使视觉证据与事实先验冲突，从而分离两种来源的正确性。

Result: (1) 多个模型在标准测试中达到人类水平，但实为事实回忆；随机化数据测试会低估真实能力。(2) CVLAT分类发现多数模型为视觉导向，少数为事实导向，部分模型处于临界。(3) 提示干预可改变优先级但效果高度模型依赖且方向不对称，高图表阅读能力不预测提示可控性。

Conclusion: 高视觉准确率不足以证明忠实的视觉推理；可靠的视觉分析集成需要同时评估可视化素养和模型在视觉证据与事实先验冲突时的仲裁能力。

Original Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) show strong visualization interpretation, yet it is unclear whether their responses reflect genuine reasoning over visual evidence or factual priors learned during training. Current evaluations mix these two sources, obscuring when correct visual interpretation is overridden by memorized facts. We present a framework that isolates visual correctness from factual correctness, revealing validity limitations in existing visualization literacy assessments. Across three experiments with 15 state-of-the-art LVLMs: (1) several models reach human-level performance on standard tests (VLAT), but this may reflect factual recall rather than visual understanding, while randomized-data tests (reVLAT) underestimate literacy when correct visual interpretation is superseded by factual priors. (2) Using our Counterfactual Visualization Literacy Assessment Test (CVLAT) with capability-normalized arbitration metrics, we classify models by the sign of their visual-factual reliance index (VFRI), revealing a visualization-oriented majority and a factual knowledge-oriented minority, though several near-zero cases warrant caution. A human baseline (N=30) on the same counterfactual items confirms that people overwhelmingly follow the chart under conflict, providing a human reference point. (3) Prompt-based intervention can shift prioritization, but its effectiveness is highly model-dependent and direction-asymmetric, and high chart-reading capability does not predict prompt-controllability. Overall, high visualization accuracy is not sufficient evidence of faithful visual reasoning: reliable integration into visual analytics requires evaluating not only visualization literacy but also how models arbitrate between visual evidence and factual priors when the two diverge. Benchmark and code: https://github.com/JaeyoungKim-HCIL/CVLAT

中文摘要翻译: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）展现出强大的可视化解释能力，但尚不清楚它们的回答是基于对视觉证据的真实推理，还是基于训练中学到的事实先验。当前的评估混淆了这两种来源，掩盖了正确视觉解释被记忆性事实覆盖的情况。我们提出了一个将视觉正确性与事实正确性分离的框架，揭示了现有可视化素养评估的有效性局限。通过对15个最先进LVLM进行三项实验：(1) 多个模型在标准测试（VLAT）中达到人类水平，但这可能反映的是事实回忆而非视觉理解，而随机化数据测试（reVLAT）在正确视觉解释被事实先验取代时会低估素养。(2) 使用我们提出的反事实可视化素养评估测试（CVLAT）和能力归一化的仲裁指标，根据模型视觉-事实依赖指数（VFRI）的符号进行分类，揭示了大多数模型为可视化导向，少数为事实知识导向，但部分接近零的案例需谨慎对待。在同一反事实项目上的人类基线实验（N=30）证实，人们在冲突情况下绝大多数会遵循图表，提供了人类参考点。(3) 基于提示的干预可以改变优先级，但其效果高度依赖模型且方向不对称，高图表阅读能力并不能预测提示可控性。总体而言，高可视化准确率不足以证明忠实的视觉推理；要可靠地集成到视觉分析中，不仅需要评估可视化素养，还需要评估模型在视觉证据与事实先验产生分歧时的仲裁能力。基准测试与代码：https://github.com/JaeyoungKim-HCIL/CVLAT

</details>


### [33] [$A^2$: Smaller Self-Supervised ViTs Localize Better than Larger Ones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03148)
*Sreehari Rammohan,Huy Ha,Carl Vondrick*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出A²方法，利用小型自监督ViTs注意力图定位更佳但大型ViTs特征更丰富的互补特性，通过解耦"看哪里"（小模型注意力）和"提取什么"（大模型嵌入）来实现鲁棒视觉分类，在多个基准上表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 研究发现小型自监督ViTs的注意力图比大型ViTs能更好地定位前景目标，但大型ViTs能从每个图块中提取更丰富的表示。同时需要两者优势，即良好的定位能力和丰富的特征表示。

Method: 提出A²方法，解耦定位与特征提取：使用小型注意力模型定位注意力峰值区域并裁剪图像块，再使用大型嵌入模型对裁剪区域进行特征嵌入。完全使用预训练特征，无需组标签，也无需为每个数据集训练注意力或骨干网络。

Result: 在5个基准测试中，A²与骨干匹配的损失级方法（如DFR）性能相当，并在更强的分布偏移下优于端到端注意力训练方法。

Conclusion: 通过解耦定位和特征提取，可以在不增加额外训练成本的情况下，同时获得小模型良好的前景定位能力和大模型丰富的特征表示，实现鲁棒的视觉分类。

Original Abstract: Robust visual classification often depends on localizing the main foreground objects in an image while ignoring contextual distractors. Surprisingly, we find that the attention maps of smaller self-supervised ViTs localize foreground objects better than those of larger ViTs. However, we still need large ViTs, because they extract richer representations from each patch. To get the best of both worlds, good localization and rich representations, we propose $A^2$, a simple method that leverages this inverse scaling finding by decoupling where to look (a small attention model) from what to extract (a large embedding model): we crop around the attention peaks of a small model and embed the crops with a larger model. $A^2$ uses entirely pretrained features, requires no group labels, and does not require per-dataset attention or backbone training. Across 5 benchmarks, $A^2$ is competitive with backbone-matched loss-level methods like DFR, and outperforms end-to-end attention training under stronger distribution shifts.

中文摘要翻译: 鲁棒的视觉分类通常依赖于定位图像中的主要前景对象，同时忽略上下文干扰物。令人惊讶的是，我们发现较小的自监督ViTs的注意力图比大型ViTs能更好地定位前景对象。然而，我们仍然需要大型ViTs，因为它们能从每个图块中提取更丰富的表示。为了兼得二者之长——良好的定位能力和丰富的表示——我们提出了A²，一种简单的方法，利用这种逆缩放发现，将"看哪里"（小型注意力模型）与"提取什么"（大型嵌入模型）解耦：我们裁剪小型模型注意力峰值周围的区域，并用大型模型对裁剪区域进行嵌入。A²完全使用预训练特征，无需组标签，也无需为每个数据集训练注意力或骨干网络。在5个基准测试中，A²与骨干匹配的损失级方法（如DFR）竞争力相当，并在更强的分布偏移下优于端到端注意力训练方法。

</details>


### [34] [NVIDIA OmniDreams: Real-Time Generative World Model for Closed-Loop Autonomous Vehicle Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03159)
*NVIDIA,:,Aarti Basant,Amlan Kar,Despoina Paschalidou,Fangyin Wei,Francesco Ferroni,Guillermo Garcia Cobo,Haithem Turki,Huan Ling,Jaewoo Seo,James Lucas,Jay Zhangjie Wu,Jialiang Wang,Jonathan Lorraine,Jun Gao,Kai He,Katarina Tothova,Kevin Xie,Michał Tyszkiewicz,Qi Wu,Riccardo de Lutio,Ruilong Li,Sanja Fidler,Seung Wook Kim,Tianchang Shen,Tianshi Cao,Tobias Pfaff,William Lew,Xindi Wu,Xuanchi Ren,Yifan Lu,Yuxuan Zhang,Zan Gojcic,Zian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniDreams 是一个基于 Cosmos 扩散模型构建的基础生成式世界模型，通过中继和后训练 21k 小时驾驶场景数据，能够自回归地实时生成动作条件视频，用于闭环仿真中评估和训练自动驾驶策略，相比传统神经模拟器能泛化到极端天气等长尾场景，且后训练的世界-动作模型（WAM）在 NuRec 数据集上以 1/5 参数超越了 VLA 策略模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于重建的神经模拟器受限于初始采集数据，难以泛化到高度动态或新颖场景（如极端天气、不可预测的智能体行为），瓶颈在于闭环仿真中安全评估长尾场景的驾驶策略。

Method: 基于 Cosmos 扩散模型进行中继和后训练（21k 小时驾驶场景），构建基础生成式世界模型 OmniDreams；其以自回归方式基于历史帧、当前模拟器状态和即时驾驶动作生成逼真的传感器观测；部署于 Alpamayo 1 策略模型和 AlpaSim 协调器的闭环系统。

Result: 后训练得到的 WAM（世界-动作模型）在 Physical AI AV NuRec 数据集上超越 VLA 基线的 Alpamayo 1.5 研究策略模型，同时参数量仅为后者的 1/5。

Conclusion: OmniDreams 作为实时世界模型，既能为自动驾驶策略提供可扩展的闭环训练与评估环境，又有潜力作为策略架构的骨干网络。

Original Abstract: As autonomous vehicle capabilities advance, the safe evaluation of driving policies in long-tail scenarios remains a critical bottleneck. In closed-loop simulation, the driving policy model actively interacts with the environment, where its actions dynamically update the simulator state and directly influence the next set of generated sensor observations. While recent reconstruction-based neural simulators offer photorealism, they are fundamentally constrained by their initial captured data and struggle to generalize to highly dynamic or novel scenes. To overcome these limitations, we introduce OmniDreams, a foundation generative world model mid- and post-trained from the Cosmos diffusion model to autoregressively generate action-conditioned videos in real time. By leveraging the rich visual priors of Cosmos and mid- and post-training on 21k hours of driving scenarios, OmniDreams synthesizes complex, unobserved phenomena that are hard for traditional simulators to capture, such as extreme weather and unpredictable dynamic agent behaviors. Crucially, it autoregressively conditions its photorealistic sensor generation on past frames, the current simulator state, and immediate driving actions. Deployed in a closed-loop system with the Alpamayo 1 policy model and AlpaSim orchestrator, OmniDreams acts as a highly responsive, reactive environment, providing a scalable and comprehensive solution for training and evaluating next-generation autonomous driving policies. We additionally show preliminary results indicating that a world-action model (WAM) post-trained from OmniDreams achieves strong performance on the Physical AI Autonomous Vehicles NuRec dataset, surpassing the VLA-based Alpamayo 1.5 research policy model while using only 1/5 the total parameters. These results highlight the potential for a real-time world model like OmniDreams to also serve as a backbone for policy architectures.

中文摘要翻译: 随着自动驾驶能力的提升，在长尾场景中安全评估驾驶策略仍是一个关键瓶颈。在闭环仿真中，驾驶策略模型与环境主动交互，其动作动态更新模拟器状态并直接影响下一组生成的传感器观测。虽然近期基于重建的神经模拟器具有照片级真实感，但它们在根本上受限于初始采集的数据，难以泛化到高度动态或新颖的场景。为克服这些限制，我们提出了 OmniDreams，一个基于 Cosmos 扩散模型进行中继和后训练的基础生成式世界模型，能够自回归地实时生成动作条件视频。通过利用 Cosmos 丰富的视觉先验以及在 21k 小时驾驶场景上的中继和后训练，OmniDreams 合成了传统模拟器难以捕获的复杂、未观测到的现象，例如极端天气和不可预测的动态智能体行为。关键的，它以自回归方式基于历史帧、当前模拟器状态和即时驾驶动作生成照片级逼真的传感器观测。在部署于 Alpamayo 1 策略模型和 AlpaSim 协调器组成的闭环系统时，OmniDreams 作为一个高响应性、反应式的环境，为训练和评估下一代自动驾驶策略提供了可扩展且全面的解决方案。我们还展示了初步结果：从 OmniDreams 后训练得到的 WAM（世界-动作模型）在 Physical AI 自动驾驶车辆 NuRec 数据集上取得了强劲性能，超越了基于 VLA 的 Alpamayo 1.5 研究策略模型，而参数量仅为后者的 1/5。这些结果凸显了像 OmniDreams 这样的实时世界模型也有潜力作为策略架构的骨干。

</details>


### [35] [SRENet: Spectral Re-Entry Network for Point Cloud Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03160)
*Qiuxia Wu,Jiarui Lan,Wenxiong Kang,Zhiyong Wang,Kun Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SRENet，一种基于频谱感知的框架，通过小波分解将点云序列动作特征分解为低频和高频分量，并利用频谱重入块和对比学习策略，在三个基准数据集上达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 点云序列的稀疏不规则结构和时序不一致性导致时空表示学习困难，现有方法难以同时捕捉全局运动上下文和细粒度时序动态信息。

Method: 提出SRENet框架，包含：(1) 频谱分解块(SDeBlock)，沿时间和空间轴进行小波分析，将特征分解为高低频分量并施加频率特异性注意力；(2) 频谱重入块(SReBlock)，对语义融合中失真的时序频率结构进行二次时序分解以恢复残差动态；(3) 频谱感知学习策略，通过对比损失和课程学习（从低频到高频逐步聚焦）增强频率子空间的判别能力。

Result: 在MSR-Action3D、NTU-RGBD和NTU-RGBD120三个数据集上，SRENet达到当前最优性能，验证了频率建模在点云动作理解中的有效性。

Conclusion: 从频率视角显式建模全局上下文和细粒度时序动态是点云动作识别的有效途径，频谱分解与重入机制能有效提升时空表示学习能力。

Original Abstract: Recognizing human actions from point cloud sequences is critical for 3D perception driven applications such as autonomous driving and human-computer interaction. However, the irregular structure and temporal inconsistency of point clouds pose unique challenges for spatio-temporal representation learning, especially in capturing both global motion context and fine-grained temporal dynamics. We propose SRENet, a spectral-aware framework designed to explicitly learn both global context and fine-grained temporal dynamics of motion from a frequency perspective for action recognition. SRENet introduces a Spectral Decomposition Block (SDeBlock) that performs wavelet-based analysis along temporal and spatial axes, disentangling features into low- and high-frequency components with frequency-specific attention. To recover residual dynamics and re-align temporal frequency structures distorted during semantic fusion, a Spectral Re-entry Block (SReBlock) performs secondary temporal decomposition. Furthermore, a spectral-aware learning strategy is devised to enhance discriminability in both frequency subspaces via contrastive loss and a curriculum schedule that gradually shifts focus from low- to high-frequency spaces in line with coarse to detailed motion patterns. Extensive experiments on MSR-Action3D, NTU-RGBD and NTU-RGBD120 demonstrate that SRENet achieves state-of-the-art performance, validating the effectiveness of frequency modeling in point cloud-based action understanding.

中文摘要翻译: 从点云序列中识别人类动作对于自动驾驶和人机交互等3D感知驱动应用至关重要。然而，点云的不规则结构与时序不一致性给时空表示学习带来了独特挑战，尤其是在捕捉全局运动上下文和细粒度时序动态方面。本文提出SRENet，一种频谱感知框架，旨在从频率视角显式学习动作的全局上下文和细粒度时序动态。SRENet引入频谱分解块（SDeBlock），沿时间和空间轴进行小波分析，将特征解耦为低频和高频分量，并施加频率特异性注意力。为恢复残差动态并重对齐语义融合过程中失真的时序频率结构，频谱重入块（SReBlock）执行二次时序分解。此外，本文设计了一种频谱感知学习策略，通过对比损失和课程学习机制（逐步将焦点从低频空间转移至高频空间，对应从粗略到精细的运动模式）增强两个频率子空间的判别能力。在MSR-Action3D、NTU-RGBD和NTU-RGBD120上的大量实验表明，SRENet达到了当前最优性能，验证了频率建模在点云动作理解中的有效性。

</details>


### [36] [JAVEDIT: Joint Audio-Visual Instruction-Guided Video Editing with Agentic Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03168)
*Yinan Chen,Chuming Lin,Zhennan Chen,Yuxiang Zeng,Junwei Zhu,Yali Bi,Xijie Huang,Chengming Xu,Donghao Luo,Zhucun Xue,Xiaobin Hu,Chengjie Wang,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang,Shuicheng Yan*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前基于指令的视频编辑技术取得了显著进展，但音频-视频联合编辑领域仍缺乏专用数据集和标准化评测基准，这限制了该方向的研究发展。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建JAVEdit-100k数据集：通过四种精心设计的生成流程并配合智能体参与的质量控制机制，构建了包含约10万编辑三元组（涵盖主体编辑、语音编辑等五种类别）的大规模高质量数据集。提出JAVEditBench基准：包含精心挑选的源视频和人工对齐的指令。提出JAVEdit基线模型：作为指令引导音频-视频联合编辑的基线方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，JAVEdit模型在六项评价指标中的五项上优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过构建大规模数据集、标准化基准和基线模型，本文有效推动了音频-视频联合编辑领域的研究，填补了数据与评测方面的空白。

Original Abstract: While instruction-based video editing has seen significant progress, joint audio-visual editing remains constrained by the absence of dedicated datasets and benchmarks. To bridge this gap, we present JAVEdit-100k, the first large-scale, high-quality dataset tailored for instruction-guided joint audio-visual editing. Focusing on human-centric videos, JAVEdit-100k comprises approximately 100K editing triplets spanning five distinct categories, including subject editing and speech editing. This dataset is rigorously constructed via four meticulously designed generation pipelines, seamlessly paired with an agent-in-the-loop quality control mechanism. Furthermore, to address the lack of standardized evaluation within the field, we introduce JAVEditBench, a comprehensive benchmark featuring curated source videos and human-aligned instructions across all editing categories. Finally, we propose JAVEdit, a pioneering baseline model for instruction-guided joint audio-visual editing. Experiments show that \model\ outperforms all baselines on five of six evaluation metrics.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管基于指令的视频编辑已取得显著进展，但音频-视觉联合编辑仍受限于专用数据集和基准评测的缺失。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了JAVEdit-100k——首个面向指令引导的音频-视频联合编辑的大规模高质量数据集。该数据集以人为中心视频为主要对象，包含约10万个编辑三元组，涵盖主体编辑和语音编辑等五个不同类别。该数据集通过四种精心设计的生成流程严格构建，并配有智能体参与的质量控制机制。此外，为解决该领域缺乏标准化评测的问题，我们引入了JAVEditBench——一个综合性基准，其包含精挑细选的源视频以及覆盖所有编辑类别的人工对齐指令。最后，我们提出了JAVEdit，一个用于指令引导的音频-视频联合编辑的开创性基线模型。实验表明，JAVEdit在六项评价指标中的五项上优于所有基线方法。

</details>


### [37] [Ask When It Pays: Cost-Aware Open-Ended Interaction for Instance Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03175)
*Xunyi Zhao,Sihao Lin,Gengze Zhou,Zerui Li,Shijie Li,Wei Tao,Jiajun Liu,Qi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种代价敏感的交互式实例目标导航方法，通过信息增益分析确定问题类型及其成本权重，并构建新基准与加权成功率指标，使智能体仅在预期不确定性降低超过交互代价时才发起查询，从而高效消解语言歧义。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有交互式导航方法将轻量级澄清查询与路线级指导混为一谈，智能体可通过重复高信息量提问来提升成功率，而非高效消解底层歧义，缺乏代价敏感交互的研究。

Method: (1) 对导航语料进行信息增益分析，识别降低导航不确定性的线索，得到紧凑的问题类型集合及数据驱动权重；(2) 构建用于诊断交互行为与效率的基准；(3) 提出加权成功率(W-SR)指标，按查询代价惩罚；(4) 提出零样本MLLM导航器，仅在预期不确定性降低超过交互代价时选择性查询。

Result: 构建了基于问题类型分类的交互行为诊断基准，并设计了可衡量代价敏感交互效率的加权成功率指标。

Conclusion: 将交互式IGN重新定义为代价敏感的不确定性降低问题，通过信息增益分析派生出问题类型与权重，使智能体更高效地利用交互来消解歧义。

Original Abstract: Instance Goal Navigation (IGN) requires an embodied agent to find a specific object instance among distractors from an underspecified natural-language description. Such ambiguity often cannot be resolved from perception and language alone, making interaction with an oracle a natural mechanism for disambiguation. Prior interactive methods allow oracle queries but treat lightweight clarification and route-level guidance alike, letting agents boost success rate through repeated high-information questions rather than by resolving the underlying ambiguity efficiently.
