By imposing a strict low-rank constraint, LRNOFF inherently acts as a powerful implicit regularizer, preventing noise memorization and ensuring the extraction of robust spectral bases. Furthermore, we develop an efficient computational implementation termed LRNOFF-Fast, which drastically reduces computational and memory overhead while preserving theoretical optimality. Experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves state-of-the-art performance.

中文摘要翻译: 图信号去噪是图信号处理中的一项基础任务。节点导向的滤波方法虽然增强了空间自适应性，但其依赖图傅里叶变换导致谱刚性。相反，新兴的分数阶域变换提供了关键的谱灵活性，却因其全局共享的滤波范式而受到根本限制，无法适应局部拓扑变化。为弥合这一差距，本文提出一种广义的节点导向分数阶滤波（NOFF）框架，该框架在各种分数阶变换上无缝集成了局部空间适应性与主动谱调制。然而，直接为所有顶点分配独立的满秩滤波器会导致参数空间过大，从而在随机噪声上产生严重的过拟合。为缓解这一问题，我们引入了低秩NOFF（LRNOFF）架构。通过施加严格低秩约束，LRNOFF本质上充当了一个强大的隐式正则化器，防止噪声记忆并确保提取鲁棒的谱基。此外，我们开发了一种高效的计算实现方案LRNOFF-Fast，在保持理论最优性的同时大幅降低了计算和内存开销。在真实世界数据集上的实验表明，所提框架达到了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [128] [Instantaneous Risk Minimization for Secure Integrated Sensing and Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03372)
*Chao Ge,Na Zhao,Yuan Shen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对安全集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统，提出一种鲁棒波束赋形框架，通过最小化瞬时信息泄露风险而非传统遍历保密率，实现最坏情况下的物理层安全保证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统设计关注最大化遍历保密率，无法应对瞬时窃听风险；本文旨在最小化瞬时信息泄露风险，同时兼顾感知精度和合法用户服务质量，实现每次传输的实时安全保证。

Method: 构建多目标优化问题，联合抑制最坏情况窃听者信干噪比、提升感知精度并保证合法用户QoS；提出分层迭代算法，外层基于更新后的感知性能连续细化不确定性区域，内层在细化后的不确定性区域下优化波束赋形。

Result: 理论分析和仿真结果表明，该方法能够在实际复杂度下实现每次传输的安全保证。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保证安全性的同时兼顾感知性能与通信质量，为ISAC系统提供了一种实用的鲁棒安全方案。

Original Abstract: To ensure worst-case physical layer security, this paper proposes a robust beamforming framework for secure integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems. Different from conventional designs that focus on maximizing the ergodic secrecy rate, the proposed method aims to minimize instantaneous information leakage risk. We formulate a multi-objective optimization problem that jointly suppresses the worst-case eavesdropper signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR), improving sensing accuracy, and ensuring the quality of service (QoS) for legitimate users. To address the resulting non-convex problem, we develop a hierarchical iterative algorithm, in which the outer loop refines the continuous uncertainty regions based on the updated sensing performance, and the inner loop optimizes beamforming under the refined uncertainty regions. Theoretical analysis and simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves per-transmission security guarantees with practical complexity.

中文摘要翻译: 为确保最坏情况下的物理层安全，本文针对安全集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统提出了一种鲁棒波束赋形框架。与关注最大化遍历保密率的传统设计不同，所提方法旨在最小化瞬时信息泄露风险。我们构建了一个多目标优化问题，联合抑制最坏情况下窃听者的信干噪比（SINR）、提升感知精度并保证合法用户的服务质量（QoS）。为求解由此产生的非凸问题，我们开发了一种分层迭代算法，其中外层循环基于更新后的感知性能不断细化连续不确定性区域，内层循环则在细化后的不确定性区域下优化波束赋形。理论分析和仿真结果表明，所提方法能够以实际可行的复杂度实现每次传输的安全保证。

</details>


### [129] [Voxel-CKM: Voxelized Radio Frequency Radiance Fields for Fast and Few-Shot CKM Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03531)
*Hanlei Li,Guangyi Zhang,Kequan Zhou,Yunlong Cai,Guanding Yu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出Voxel-CKM，一种基于体素化射频辐射场的信道知识地图快速构建方法，通过显式体素网格、向量矩阵分解、发射机先验和全变分正则化，在少量测量下实现快速收敛和高性能信道预测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有信道知识地图构建方法训练时间长达数小时甚至数天，且需要密集测量数据，导致部署成本高昂，亟需一种快速且支持少样本学习的CKM构建方法。

Method: 1) 用显式体素网格替代隐式神经表示，高效捕获无线信道的空间变化；2) 引入紧凑的向量-矩阵（VM）分解，将体素网格参数化为少量矩阵和向量以加速收敛；3) 融入发射机先验作为归纳偏置，实现少样本学习；4) 提出全变分（TV）正则化损失以缓解过拟合并稳定优化。

Result: 实验表明，Voxel-CKM在训练收敛速度上显著提升，并在少样本场景下取得了更优的性能。

Conclusion: Voxel-CKM通过体素化辐射场、VM分解、发射机先验和TV正则化的结合，有效解决了CKM构建中训练慢和测量密集的问题，实现了快速且少样本的信道知识地图构建。

Original Abstract: Channel knowledge maps (CKMs) are designed to predict channel state information (CSI) from user locations, thereby enabling low-overhead CSI acquisition. However, existing CKM construction methods often require hours-to-days of training time and dense measurements, resulting in substantial deployment cost. In this paper, we propose Voxel-CKM, a novel voxelized radio frequency (RF) radiance field framework for fast and few-shot CKM construction. The core idea is to replace implicit neural representations with explicit voxel grids to efficiently capture the spatial variation of wireless channels. Building upon this, we further introduce a compact vector-matrix (VM) decomposition to parameterize these voxel grids using a small set of matrices and vectors, which significantly accelerates convergence and facilitates fast CKM construction. To enable few-shot learning, we incorporate a transmitter prior as an inductive bias to guide the learning process under sparse measurements. Additionally, a total-variation (TV) regularization loss is proposed to mitigate overfitting and stabilize optimization. Experiments show that Voxel-CKM substantially accelerates training convergence and improves performance in the few-shot regime.

中文摘要翻译: 信道知识地图（CKM）旨在根据用户位置预测信道状态信息（CSI），从而实现低开销的CSI获取。然而，现有CKM构建方法通常需要数小时到数天的训练时间以及密集的测量数据，导致高昂的部署成本。本文提出Voxel-CKM，一种新颖的体素化射频（RF）辐射场框架，用于快速且少样本的CKM构建。核心思想是用显式体素网格替代隐式神经表示，以高效捕获无线信道的空间变化。在此基础上，我们进一步引入紧凑的向量-矩阵（VM）分解，将体素网格参数化为少量矩阵和向量，这显著加速了收敛并促进了快速CKM构建。为实现少样本学习，我们将发射机先验作为归纳偏置融入模型，以指导稀疏测量下的学习过程。此外，提出全变分（TV）正则化损失以缓解过拟合并稳定优化。实验表明，Voxel-CKM显著加快了训练收敛速度，并在少样本场景下提升了性能。

</details>


### [130] [Chasing Lightning: Detecting, Characterizing, and Identifying a Powerful Space-Based GNSS Interference Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03673)
*Zachary L. Clements,Argyris Kriezis,Todd E. Humphreys*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文基于2019-2026年间地面GNSS参考站网络数据，开发了接收功率检测框架，结合到达时间差测量技术，识别出造成欧洲、格陵兰和加拿大广域干扰的源为俄罗斯“闪电”轨道早期预警卫星星座。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 天基干扰源具有极广的地理覆盖范围和潜在的GNSS干扰质性升级风险，因此识别和定位此类干扰源至关重要。

Method: (1) 基于接收功率的检测框架；(2) 分析干扰事件的空间、时间和频谱模式；(3) 融合接收功率与到达时间差测量的识别技术。

Result: 自2019年以来，该天基干扰源已造成数十次强大的瞬态广域干扰事件，覆盖欧洲大陆、格陵兰和加拿大地区。

Conclusion: 该GNSS干扰源被确认为俄罗斯“闪电”轨道上的早期预警卫星星座。

Original Abstract: This paper analyzes and identifies a space-based Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) interference source that has caused scores of powerful transient wide-area interference events over continental Europe, Greenland, and Canada since 2019. While terrestrial or near-terrestrial sources are primarily responsible for the recent uptick in GNSS interference worldwide, space-based interferers are of special concern given their potential for vast geographic reach and their portent of a qualitative escalation in GNSS interference. Based on data collected between 2019 and 2026 from a network of terrestrial GNSS reference stations, this paper (1) develops a received-power-based detection framework; (2) details the spatial, temporal, and spectral patterns of wide-area interference events caused by the source; (3) presents and analyzes identification techniques that blend received-power and time-difference-of-arrival measurements; and (4) applies these techniques to confidently identify the GNSS interference source as a constellation of Russian early warning satellites in Molniya ("lightning") orbits.

中文摘要翻译: 本文分析并识别了一个天基全球导航卫星系统（GNSS）干扰源，该源自2019年以来已对欧洲大陆、格陵兰和加拿大造成了数十次强大的瞬态广域干扰事件。虽然地面或近地面源是近期全球GNSS干扰增多的主要原因，但天基干扰源因其潜在的大范围地理覆盖能力以及可能造成GNSS干扰质性升级的预兆而备受关注。基于2019年至2026年间从地面GNSS参考站网络收集的数据，本文：(1) 开发了一种基于接收功率的检测框架；(2) 详细描述了该源引起的广域干扰事件的空间、时间和频谱模式；(3) 提出并分析了融合接收功率与到达时间差测量的识别技术；(4) 应用这些技术，将该GNSS干扰源可靠地识别为俄罗斯“闪电”轨道上的早期预警卫星星座。

</details>


### [131] [Constrained Pinching Antenna Array Design for Sum-Rate Maximization in Multi-User PASS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03830)
*Minghao Jin,Anna Li,Tianwei Hou,Qiang Ni,Arumugam Nallanathan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对多用户可捏合天线系统（PASS）中现有PA阵列设计存在波束调整精度有限或部署成本过高的问题，提出了一种实用的约束型可捏合天线阵列（C-PAA）方案，通过联合优化阵列中心位置和细粒度天线分布来最大化系统总和速率，并利用函数单峰特性实现高效一维搜索及闭式近似解，性能优于传统固定间距和现有PA阵列基准。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有可捏合天线（PA）阵列设计在多用户PASS场景中面临两难：要么波束自适应精度有限，要么部署成本过高。因此，需要一种更实用的、兼具高精度和低成本的天线阵列方案。

Method: 将多个PA分组为可移动阵列（C-PAA），在波长尺度内精细调整天线位置；建模为以阵列中心位置和天线分布为变量的和速率最大化问题；刻画C-PAA结构特性并推导阵列孔径显式上界；建立有效信道增益和可达速率的可处理近似；利用系统和速率函数在实用条件下的单峰特性进行高效一维搜索，并推导出近优阵列中心位置的闭式近似解。

Result: 理论分析验证了所提近似的准确性；仿真表明C-PAA方案性能接近理想上界，且显著优于传统固定间距和现有PA阵列基准方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的C-PAA方案在实际约束下实现了高精度波束调谐与低部署成本的良好折中，为柔性室内无线通信提供了一种高效、实用的天线阵列设计方法。

Original Abstract: Pinching antenna systems (PASS) have recently emerged as a promising architecture for flexible indoor wireless communications. However, most existing pinching antenna (PA) array designs for multi-user PASS either offer limited beam adaptation accuracy or require prohibitively high deployment cost. In this paper, we investigate a more practical constrained pinching antenna array (C-PAA)-assisted downlink PASS, where multiple PAs are grouped into a movable array and can be finely adjusted within the array at the wavelength scale. To improve the system spectral efficiency, a sum-rate maximization problem is formulated by jointly considering the array-center position and the fine-grained antenna distribution within the C-PAA. First, the structural properties of the C-PAA are characterized, and an explicit upper bound on the array aperture is derived. Then, tractable approximations for the effective channel gain and the achievable user rate are developed. Furthermore, the optimization problem of the multi-user sum-rate is analyzed, where the system sum-rate function is shown to exhibit a favorable unimodal behavior under practically relevant conditions, which enables an efficient one-dimensional search for the optimal C-PAA position. To further reduce the computational complexity, a closed-form approximate solution for the near-optimal array-center position is derived. Numerical results verify the accuracy of the developed analysis and demonstrate that the proposed C-PAA scheme closely approaches the ideal upper bound and significantly outperforms conventional fixed-spacing and existing PA array benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 可捏合天线系统（PASS）近期已成为一种有前景的柔性室内无线通信架构。然而，现有面向多用户PASS的可捏合天线（PA）阵列设计要么波束自适应精度有限，要么部署成本过高。本文研究了一种更实用的约束型可捏合天线阵列（C-PAA）辅助的下行PASS，其中多个PA被分组为一个可移动阵列，并可在波长尺度内进行精细调整。为提升系统频谱效率，本文联合优化C-PAA的阵列中心位置和细粒度天线分布，构建了一个和速率最大化问题。首先，刻画了C-PAA的结构特性，并推导了阵列孔径的显式上界。随后，建立了有效信道增益和可达用户速率的可处理近似。进一步分析了多用户和速率优化问题，表明在实际相关条件下系统和速率函数呈现出有利的单峰特性，从而能够对最优C-PAA位置进行高效一维搜索。为进一步降低计算复杂度，推导了近优阵列中心位置的闭式近似解。数值结果验证了所提分析的准确性，并表明所提出的C-PAA方案性能接近理想上界，且显著优于传统固定间距和现有PA阵列基准方案。

</details>


### [132] [Stability Analysis for Autoregressive Sampling Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03942)
*Daniele Gerosa,Thomas Eriksson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文研究了AR(1)抖动采样集在Paley-Wiener信号上的密度与稳定性，发现尽管这类采样集在均值和几乎必然意义下具有正确的渐近密度，但几乎必然不是稳定采样集；然而相应的抖动sinc矩阵以高概率具有良好的条件数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 受ADC中时钟抖动建模为AR(1)过程的近期进展启发，探究AR(1)抖动采样集对Paley-Wiener信号的密度与稳定性性质。

Method: 理论分析：研究AR(1)抖动采样集的渐近密度（均值与几乎必然意义下）和稳定性；有限维分析：研究对应的抖动sinc矩阵的条件数。

Result: 1) AR(1)抖动采样集在均值和几乎必然意义下具有正确的渐近密度；2) 但几乎必然不是稳定采样集；3) 对应的抖动sinc矩阵以高概率具有良好的条件数。

Conclusion: 虽然AR(1)抖动采样集在无限维意义下几乎必然不稳定，但在有限维情形下以高概率表现良好，这为实际应用提供了理论支撑。

Original Abstract: Motivated by recent developments in stochastic modeling of clock jitter in Analog-to-Digital Converters (ADCs) as autoregressive processes of order one (AR(1)), we study the density and stability properties of AR(1)-jittered sampling sets for Paley-Wiener signals. We show that, despite having the correct asymptotic density both on average and almost surely, such sets almost surely fail to be stable sampling sets. We complement this negative result with a finite-dimensional analysis, showing that the corresponding jittered sinc matrices are nonetheless well-conditioned with high probability.

中文摘要翻译: 受模数转换器(ADC)中时钟抖动被建模为一阶自回归过程(AR(1))的最新进展启发，我们研究了用于Paley-Wiener信号的AR(1)抖动采样集的密度与稳定性性质。我们证明，尽管这类采样集在均值和几乎必然意义下都具有正确的渐近密度，但它们几乎必然不是稳定采样集。我们通过有限维分析对这一负面结果进行了补充，表明对应的抖动sinc矩阵以高概率仍然是良条件的。

</details>


### [133] [SNF-PRP: A Covert Integrating Sensing and Communications Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03960)
*Dhrumil Bhatt,Vidushi Kumar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于亚噪声基底伪随机探测（SNF-PRP）的隐蔽感知框架，通过KL散度保证ε-隐蔽性，利用OFDM系统的扩频增益，实现5G NR场景下的隐蔽ISAC。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有ISAC系统的探测信号易被敌方拦截，暴露感知活动和传输信息；传统物理层安全方法虽能抗截获但信号可被检测，无法隐蔽感知行为本身。

Method: 提出SNF-PRP框架：基于KL散度建立ε-隐蔽性保证；利用N_sc倍扩频增益（突破现有宽带分析）；推导了达到目标CRB所需的最小积分长度的闭式解。

Result: 5G NR n78仿真显示：在-12dB和-15dB探测功率下，测距精度<0.5m，测速精度<0.5m/s，KL散度低于隐蔽阈值5.8倍，验证了隐蔽性与感知性能的联合可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的SNF-PRP框架能在亚噪声功率水平下实现隐蔽感知，同时满足高精度感知需求，为ISAC系统的隐蔽性提供了理论保证与可行方案。

Original Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) enables simultaneous sensing and data transmission but exposes a critical vulnerability: probing signals may be intercepted, revealing both the transmitted information and the act of sensing itself. Existing physical layer security approaches mitigate interception yet operate with detectable signals, leaving sensing activity observable to a passive warden. This paper introduces sub-noise-floor pseudo-random probing (SNF-PRP), a covert sensing framework for OFDM-based ISAC systems under an energy-detection adversary model. SNF-PRP establishes an $ε$-covertness guarantee via Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence, exploits an $N_{\mathrm{sc}}$-fold spreading gain absent from prior wideband analyses, and derives in closed form the minimum integration length required to achieve a target Cramér-Rao bound (CRB). Simulations under 5G~NR n78 numerology confirm sub-0.5\,m range and sub-0.5\,m/s velocity accuracy with KL divergence $5.8\times$ below the covertness threshold, validating joint feasibility at $-12$\,dB and $-15$\,dB probing powers.

中文摘要翻译: 集成感知与通信（ISAC）实现了同步感知和数据传输，但暴露了一个关键脆弱性：探测信号可能被拦截，从而泄露传输信息以及感知行为本身。现有的物理层安全方法虽然能够缓解信号拦截问题，但其信号仍可被检测，使得感知活动对被动监控者而言是可观测的。本文提出了亚噪声基底伪随机探测（SNF-PRP）——一种基于OFDM的ISAC系统的隐蔽感知框架，适用于能量检测 adversary 模型。SNF-PRP通过KL散度建立ε-隐蔽性保证，利用了现有宽带分析中缺失的N_sc倍扩频增益，并推导了达到目标Cramér-Rao界所需的最小积分长度的闭式解。在5G NR n78参数下的仿真证实：测距精度优于0.5米，测速精度优于0.5米/秒，KL散度低于隐蔽阈值5.8倍，在-12 dB和-15 dB的探测功率下验证了联合可行性。

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Echo-POSED: Geometric Self-Distillation for Echocardiography Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02634)
*Elias Stenhede,Edvart Grüner Bjerke,Joanna Sulkowska,Eivind Bjørkan Orstad,Ole Jakob Elle,Ulysse Côté-Allard,Arian Ranjbar*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: Echo-POSED 是一个自监督框架，无需专家标注即可从2D超声图像实时推荐探头调整，通过从3D超声体积中切片2D视图训练，实现探头运动等变性和心脏相位不变性，在SO(3)×SO(3)上学习位姿表示，指导平均角度误差为8.2度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有TTE引导方法依赖专家标注的视图或追踪的探头轨迹，成本高、泛化差。作者希望实现无需标注、实时且具有几何一致性的探头调整推荐，降低超声成像的操作门槛。

Method: 采用自监督学习框架，从常规采集的3D超声体积中切片2D视图作为训练数据，强制模型对探头运动具有等变性（equivariance），同时对心脏相位具有不变性（invariance），从而在SO(3)×SO(3)空间上学习位姿表示。

Result: 在留出测试集和外部公开3D-TTE数据集（含跨厂商数据）上，Echo-POSED在虚拟扰动下保持几何一致性；在患者内心脏运动条件下的引导模拟中，引导视角与目标视角的平均角度误差为8.2度。

Conclusion: Echo-POSED实现了无需专家标注、实时且几何一致的TTE探头引导，在患者内和患者间引导模拟中均表现良好，具有跨厂商数据的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: We introduce Echo-POSED, a self-supervised framework for real-time transthoracic echocardiography (TTE) guidance that recommends probe adjustments directly from 2D ultrasound images, without the need for expert-labelled views or tracked probe trajectories. Instead, it trains on 2D views sliced from routinely acquired 3D echocardiography volumes, enforcing equivariance to probe motions while remaining invariant to cardiac phase, yielding a pose representation on $\mathrm{SO}(3)\times\mathrm{SO}(3)$. Across a held-out split and public external 3D--TTE datasets (including vendor shift), Echo-POSED maintains geometric consistency under virtual perturbations and enables intra- and inter-patient guidance simulations, achieving a combined mean angular error of 8.2 degrees between the guided and target views in intra-patient simulations with cardiac motion.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了Echo-POSED，一种用于实时经胸超声心动图（TTE）引导的自监督框架，它直接从2D超声图像推荐探头调整，无需专家标注的视图或追踪的探头轨迹。相反，它从常规采集的3D超声体积中切片2D视图进行训练，强制对探头运动具有等变性，同时保持对心脏相位的不变性，从而在SO(3)×SO(3)上学习位姿表示。在留出测试集和外部公开3D-TTE数据集（包括跨厂商数据）上，Echo-POSED在虚拟扰动下保持几何一致性，并支持患者内和患者间引导模拟，在带有心脏运动的患者内模拟中，引导视角与目标视角的平均角度误差为8.2度。

</details>


### [135] [Sparse-View Lung Nodule Volumetry from Digitally Reconstructed Radiographs via AReT: Anatomy-Regularized TensoRF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02639)
*Spoorthi M,Suja Palaniswamy*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文发现并修复了TensoRF在X射线衰减场应用中的一个失败模式：默认密度偏移-10会抑制梯度流动，导致稀疏视图医学重建失败。通过将密度偏移设为0，提出AReT框架，仅用三个正交X射线投影即可稳定重建肺结节，在LIDC-IDRI数据集上实现了高精度体积测量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有NeRF方法需要密集多视角采集，不适用于仅能获取少量X射线投影的临床场景。TensoRF在医学稀疏视图重建中存在未报告的失败模式，亟需解决并设计适用于稀疏视图胸部成像的重建框架。

Method: 1) 将TensoRF的密度偏移从默认-10调整为0，恢复梯度流动；2) 提出AReT（解剖正则化张量辐射场）框架，利用冠状面、矢状面和轴面三个正交投影进行重建；3) 引入胸部解剖感知的正则化，结合L1稀疏性和全变分平滑约束。

Result: 在19名患者的LIDC-IDRI数据集上，AReT在临床可操作结节（>=10mm，n=14）上达到Pearson r=0.983（p<0.0001），中位绝对体积误差11.4%，系统偏差近零（-77.3 mm³），比球体积近似方法提升8.4倍。解剖感知正则化始终优于生成先验引导方法。

Conclusion: 修复密度偏移问题后，仅需三个正交X射线投影即可实现肺结节的稳定体积重建。AReT框架结合解剖正则化在稀疏视图医学成像中优于生成先验方法，具有临床实用价值。

Original Abstract: We identify and resolve a previously unreported failure mode in TensoRF when applied to X-ray attenuation fields: the default density shift of -10, originally introduced for RGB scene reconstruction, suppresses density gradients and prevents sparse-view medical reconstruction regardless of learning rate or regularization strategy. Setting the density shift to zero restores gradient flow and enables stable volumetric reconstruction of pulmonary nodules from only three orthogonal X-ray projections. Building on this, we propose AReT, an anatomy-regularized tensorial radiance field framework for lung nodule reconstruction using coronal, sagittal, and axial projections from the LIDC-IDRI dataset (19 patients, radiologist-annotated nodules). Unlike existing NeRF approaches requiring dense multi-view acquisition, AReT is designed for sparse-view thoracic imaging and incorporates chest-anatomy-aware regularization combining L1 sparsity and total variation smoothness. A systematic comparison across 11 reconstruction strategies shows anatomy-aware regularization consistently outperforms generative-prior-guided approaches. Evaluated against radiologist consensus segmentations, AReT achieves Pearson r=0.983 (p<0.0001) for clinically actionable nodules >=10 mm (n=14), median absolute volumetric error of 11.4%, near-zero systematic bias of -77.3 mm^3, and 8.4x improvement over spherical volume approximation.

中文摘要翻译: 我们发现并解决了一个此前未被报道的TensoRF在X射线衰减场应用中的失败模式：默认的-10密度偏移（最初为RGB场景重建引入）会抑制密度梯度，无论采用何种学习率或正则化策略，都会阻碍稀疏视图医学重建。将密度偏移设置为0可恢复梯度流动，并实现仅从三个正交X射线投影对肺结节进行稳定的体积重建。在此基础上，我们提出AReT，一个解剖正则化的张量辐射场框架，利用LIDC-IDRI数据集（19名患者，放射科医生标注的结节）的冠状面、矢状面和轴面投影进行肺结节重建。与需要密集多视角采集的现有NeRF方法不同，AReT专为稀疏视图胸部成像设计，并引入了结合L1稀疏性和全变分平滑性的胸部解剖感知正则化。对11种重建策略的系统比较表明，解剖感知正则化始终优于生成先验引导方法。与放射科医生共识分割相比，在临床可操作结节（>=10mm，n=14）上，AReT实现了Pearson r=0.983（p<0.0001），中位绝对体积误差为11.4%，系统偏差近零（-77.3 mm³），比球体积近似方法提升8.4倍。

</details>


### [136] [Learning to Refine: Spectral-Decoupled Iterative Refinement Framework for Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02661)
*Yunlong Zhou,Chen Zhao,Danyang Peng,Fanfan Ji,Xiao-Tong Yuan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出频谱解耦迭代细化框架(SDIR)，通过渐进式频率解耦细化解决降水临近预测中回归模型过度平滑与扩散模型幻觉之间的权衡问题，在保持空间精度的同时实现与扩散模型相当的频谱保真度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习方法存在关键权衡：回归模型产生过度平滑、频谱衰减的预测，模糊对流细节并违反湍流幂律；扩散模型生成真实但缺乏物理基础的虚幻预测。需要一种既能保持物理一致性又能避免模糊与幻觉的确定性框架。

Method: 提出SDIR框架，包括：(1) 双路径设计——SFG-Former(尺度自适应Transformer)提取稳定低频天气图骨架，FR-Refiner(尺度条件傅里叶神经算子)迭代细化高频纹理残差；(2) 物理一致功率谱密度(PCPSD)损失函数，通过动态掩码强制执行湍流一致的频谱分布。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，SDIR在空间精度上显著优于SOTA方法，同时频谱保真度与基于扩散的方法相当，实现了可靠的高分辨率业务化临近预测。

Conclusion: 频谱解耦的渐进式细化策略有效消除了回归模型的模糊和扩散模型的幻觉，为高分辨率业务化降水临近预测提供了可行的确定性方案。

Original Abstract: Accurate precipitation nowcasting is vital for disaster mitigation, but deep learning methods face a key trade-off: regression models produce over-smoothed, spectrally decaying predictions that blur convective details and violate turbulence power laws; diffusion models generate realistic yet unanchored hallucinations lacking physical grounding. We propose Spectral-Decoupled Iterative Refinement (SDIR), a deterministic framework that reformulates nowcasting as progressive frequency-decoupled refinement. SDIR first extracts a stable low-frequency synoptic skeleton, then iteratively refines high-frequency textures under physical constraints, eliminating both blurring and hallucinations. It features a dual-path design: the Synoptic Frequency-Guided Former (SFG-Former) with Scale-Adaptive Transformers for global structure, and the Fourier Residual Refiner (FR-Refiner) with Scale-Conditioned Fourier Neural Operators for fine residuals. A Physically Consistent Power Spectral Density (PCPSD) loss with dynamic masking enforces a turbulence-consistent spectral distribution. Experiments on three benchmarks show SDIR significantly outperforms SOTA methods in spatial accuracy while achieving spectral fidelity competitive with diffusion-based methods, enabling reliable high-resolution operational nowcasting. Code link: https://github.com/RuntimeWarning/SDIR.

中文摘要翻译: 精确的降水临近预测对减灾至关重要，但深度学习方法面临关键权衡：回归模型产生过度平滑、频谱衰减的预测，模糊了对流细节并违反湍流幂律；扩散模型生成真实但缺乏物理基础的虚幻预测。我们提出频谱解耦迭代细化框架(SDIR)，一种将临近预测重新表述为渐进式频率解耦细化的确定性框架。SDIR首先提取稳定的低频天气图骨架，然后在物理约束下迭代细化高频纹理，同时消除模糊和幻觉。它采用双路径设计：具有尺度自适应Transformer的天气图频率引导Former(SFG-Former)用于全局结构，以及具有尺度条件傅里叶神经算子的傅里叶残差细化器(FR-Refiner)用于精细残差。物理一致功率谱密度(PCPSD)损失通过动态掩码强制执行湍流一致的频谱分布。在三个基准上的实验表明，SDIR在空间精度上显著优于最先进方法，同时频谱保真度与基于扩散的方法相当，实现了可靠的高分辨率业务化临近预测。代码链接：https://github.com/RuntimeWarning/SDIR。

</details>


### [137] [Depth from Dual Differential Defocus and Stereo Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02906)
*Junjie Luo,Wei Xu,Dylan Chu,Emma Alexander,Qi Guo*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出D³S Consensus算法，该算法基于物理原理，通过闭式解统一了离焦深度（DfD）与立体视觉，在超出相机景深的扩展工作范围内实现高精度深度估计。相比传统三角测量方法，在相同误差容忍度下以10倍更小的基线达到相当的工作范围，使紧凑型被动双目测距仪成为可能。原型系统仅4mm基线和12mm EFL，可从单次快照生成900×1800像素深度图，在0.3-1.64m范围内平均绝对误差为1cm，超越了某些商用立体相机。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基于三角测量的深度估计系统（如立体视觉和离焦深度）存在精度与基线大小/系统体积之间的权衡：大基线可提高精度但体积大，小基线则工作范围受限。本文旨在设计一种紧凑、高精度、可在扩展工作范围内工作的被动双目深度估计系统。

Method: 提出D³S Consensus算法：给定一对双离焦立体图像，利用新的离焦深度理论——双微分离焦（D³）与立体视觉（S）以耦合方式联合估计一个超定深度集，然后通过在这些物理上独立的线索之间强制执行一致性来选择最可信的深度预测，从而剔除不可靠的估计。

Result: 1) 分析表明D³S在相同误差容忍度下，以10倍更小的基线达到与传统三角测量系统相当的工作范围。2) 原型系统仅4mm基线、12mm EFL，从单次快照生成900×1800像素深度图，在0.3-1.64m范围内平均绝对误差为1cm，超越了某些体积更大的商用立体相机。

Conclusion: D³S Consensus通过物理驱动的闭式解统一DfD与立体视觉，显著缩小了被动双目测距仪的体积（基线可缩小10倍），同时保持甚至超越了传统大基线系统的深度估计精度，为紧凑型高精度深度感知设备提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: We introduce D^3S Consensus, a physics-based, closed-form algorithm that unifies depth-from-defocus (DfD) and stereo to achieve highly accurate depth estimation throughout an extended working range beyond the depth-of-field (DoF) of cameras. Given a pair of dual-defocus stereo images, the method estimates an overdetermined set of depth using a novel DfD theory, Dual Differential Defocus (D^3), and (S)tereo in a coupled fashion. It then picks the most confident depth prediction from the set by enforcing consensus between these physically independent cues to reject unreliable estimates.
  Analysis shows that D^3S achieves a comparable working range under the same error tolerance with 10x smaller baseline than previous triangulation-based depth estimation systems. This enables compact passive binocular rangefinders with substantially smaller form factors than conventional stereo and DfD designs. We demonstrate the first D^3S prototype with only 4 mm baseline and 12 mm EFL. It generates up to 900 x 1800-pixel depth maps with 1-cm mean absolute error over 0.3-1.64 m from a snapshot acquisition. This has surpassed the reported accuracy of certain commercially available stereo cameras with much larger form factors.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了D³S Consensus，一种基于物理原理的闭式算法，它统一了离焦深度（DfD）与立体视觉，可在超出相机景深（DoF）的扩展工作范围内实现高精度深度估计。给定一对双离焦立体图像，该方法利用一种新颖的离焦深度理论——双微分离焦（D³）与立体视觉（S）以耦合方式估计一个超定深度集，然后通过在这些物理上独立的线索之间强制执行一致性来选择最可信的深度预测，从而剔除不可靠的估计。分析表明，在相同误差容忍度下，D³S以10倍更小的基线即可达到与传统基于三角测量的深度估计系统相当的工作范围。这使得紧凑型被动双目测距仪能够实现比传统立体视觉和离焦深度设计显著更小的外形尺寸。我们展示了首个D³S原型系统，其基线仅为4mm，有效焦距为12mm。该系统可从单次快照生成高达900×1800像素的深度图，在0.3-1.64米范围内平均绝对误差为1厘米。这一精度已超越了某些外形尺寸更大的商用立体相机所报告的精度水平。

</details>


### [138] [SMAC: Spatial-Modal Joint Modeling and Adaptive Representation Collapse for Multimodal Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03370)
*Meijing Gao,Qitai Sun,Huanyu Sun,Bingxuan Yang,Bingzhou Sun,Xu Chen,Yonghao Yan,Yuxuan Yang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于空间-模态卷积融合与蒸馏提示的多模态多目标跟踪框架（SMAC），通过解耦3D卷积、幅相分解、表示坍缩网络和蒸馏提示引导模块，解决复杂光照下多模态跟踪中空间与模态特征联合建模不足及固定融合策略适应性有限的问题，在UniRTL数据集上取得了63.31 HOTA和79.21 MOTA的领先性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法在复杂光照条件下对空间特征和模态特征的联合建模不够充分，且固定融合策略难以适应多变的环境，导致多模态多目标跟踪性能受限。

Method: 首先构建空间-模态融合骨干网络：Basic模块通过解耦3D卷积进行空间特征提取与模态交互，Mixed模块通过幅相分解建模非线性跨模态关联；其次设计表示坍缩网络实现自适应多模态融合，其中蒸馏提示引导（DPG）模块在教师监督下生成动态模态权重，全局模态差异聚合（GMDA）模块保留多模态表示坍缩过程中的判别性信息。

Result: 在UniRTL数据集上，所提方法在RNT模态下达到63.31 HOTA和79.21 MOTA，超越了多种现有先进方法，同时保持了良好的推理效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的空间-模态卷积融合与蒸馏提示引导框架有效解决了复杂光照下多模态多目标跟踪的挑战，并在公开数据集上取得了优越性能。

Original Abstract: Multimodal multi-object tracking (MOT) under complex illumination remains challenging due to insufficient joint modeling of spatial and modal features and the limited adaptability of fixed fusion strategies. To address these issues, this paper proposes a spatial-modal convolution fusion and distillation-prompt-based multimodal MOT framework. A spatial-modal fusion backbone is first constructed, where a Basic module performs spatial feature extraction and modal interaction via decoupled 3D convolution, while a Mixed module models nonlinear cross-modal correlations through amplitude-phase decomposition. In addition, a representation collapse network is designed for adaptive multimodal fusion. A Distillation Prompt Guidance (DPG) module generates dynamic modal weights under teacher supervision, and a Global Modal Difference Aggregation (GMDA) module preserves discriminative information during multimodal representation collapse. Extensive experiments on the UniRTL dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method. The proposed tracker achieves 63.31 HOTA and 79.21 MOTA on the RNT modality, outperforming several state-of-the-art methods while maintaining favorable inference efficiency. The source code and pretrained models are publicly available at https://github.com/QitaiSun/SMAC.

中文摘要翻译: 复杂光照条件下的多模态多目标跟踪（MOT）仍具有挑战性，原因在于对空间和模态特征的联合建模不足以及固定融合策略的适应性有限。针对这些问题，本文提出了一种基于空间-模态卷积融合与蒸馏提示的多模态MOT框架。首先构建空间-模态融合骨干网络，其中Basic模块通过解耦3D卷积进行空间特征提取和模态交互，Mixed模块通过幅相分解建模非线性跨模态关联。此外，设计了表示坍缩网络用于自适应多模态融合。蒸馏提示引导（DPG）模块在教师监督下生成动态模态权重，全局模态差异聚合（GMDA）模块在多模态表示坍缩过程中保留判别性信息。在UniRTL数据集上的大量实验验证了所提方法的有效性。该跟踪器在RNT模态上实现了63.31 HOTA和79.21 MOTA，在保持良好推理效率的同时优于多种现有先进方法。源代码和预训练模型已公开于https://github.com/QitaiSun/SMAC。

</details>


### [139] [When BBR Meets Live Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03468)
*Xu Yan,Tong Li,Bo Wu,Cheng Luo,Jiuxiang Zhu,Laizhong Cui*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文针对BBR拥塞控制算法在直播场景中带宽估计不准确导致的启动阶段难以退出和稳定阶段发送速率偏低两个问题，提出了辅助组件BBR-Copilot，通过智能生成额外数据主动产生精确的带宽测量样本，实验表明该方法有效提升了BBR在直播场景下的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: BBR算法原本为批量数据传输设计，在直播场景中由于带宽估计不准确，存在两个关键问题：(i) 启动阶段难以退出，导致严重自损丢包；(ii) 稳定阶段发送速率低于可用带宽。因此需要改进BBR以适应直播场景。

Method: 提出BBR-Copilot辅助拥塞控制组件，与BBR协同工作，通过智能创建和发送额外数据来主动生成精确的带宽测量样本，并将原型系统在QUIC协议上实现。

Result: 基于QUIC实现了BBR-Copilot原型，并通过实验测试床进行评估，实验结果表明BBR-Copilot有效增强了BBR在直播场景中的性能。

Conclusion: BBR-Copilot通过主动生成精确带宽估计样本，有效解决了BBR在直播场景中的两大问题，提升了直播传输性能。

Original Abstract: Recently, industrial pioneers like Amazon, Tencent, ByteDance, and Huawei have been adopting BBR as their congestion control algorithm for live-streaming applications, including TikTok Live. However, BBR, originally crafted for bulk data transmission, faces multiple challenges in live-streaming scenarios. In this paper, we first explore two key issues associated with BBR due to inaccurate bandwidth estimation in live-streaming scenarios: (i) BBR cannot easily exit its startup phase, resulting in a fierce self-inflicted loss. (ii) BBR sends data at a lower rate than the available bandwidth during its stable phase. We then propose BBR-Copilot, an auxiliary congestion control component that cooperates with BBR, making BBR better adapt to live-streaming scenarios. BBR-Copilot allows for proactively generating accurate bandwidth measurement samples by smartly creating and sending extra data. We implement the BBR-Copilot prototype upon QUIC and evaluate it via testbed. Experimental evaluation results show that BBR-Copilot effectively enhances BBR's performance in live-streaming scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 近期，亚马逊、腾讯、字节跳动和华为等工业先驱已开始采用BBR作为其直播应用（包括TikTok Live）的拥塞控制算法。然而，BBR最初为批量数据传输而设计，在直播场景中面临多重挑战。本文首先探讨了直播场景中由于带宽估计不准确而导致的BBR两个关键问题：(i) BBR难以退出启动阶段，导致严重的自损丢包；(ii) BBR在稳定阶段以低于可用带宽的速率发送数据。随后，我们提出了BBR-Copilot，一个与BBR协同工作的辅助拥塞控制组件，使BBR更好地适应直播场景。BBR-Copilot通过智能创建和发送额外数据，主动生成精确的带宽测量样本。我们在QUIC协议上实现了BBR-Copilot原型，并通过测试床进行实验评估。实验评估结果表明，BBR-Copilot有效增强了BBR在直播场景中的性能。

</details>


### [140] [SEAOTTER: Sensor Embedded Autoencoding with One-Time Transcode for Efficient Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03940)
*Dan Jacobellis,Neeraja J. Yadwadkar*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出SEAOTTER压缩框架，结合学习型潜变量与标准JPEG兼容性，在200:1压缩比下比AVIF编码快7倍、解码快3.5倍，ImageNet top-1准确率提升8%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有标准编解码器（如JPEG/MPEG）带宽效率低，而新型编解码器（如AV1/AVIF）编码资源需求高且缺乏定制ASIC支持；不对称自编码器虽质量高但解码成本高且不兼容JPEG标准基础设施。

Method: 提出传感器嵌入式自编码器结合一次性转码高效重建（SEAOTTER）框架，包含可学习的JPEG颜色和量化变换，支持通用和任务感知的转码流水线，编码器预训练并冻结。

Result: 在200:1压缩比下，相比AVIF：编码速度快7倍，解码速度快3.5倍，ImageNet top-1准确率提升8%，且保持与JPEG基础设施的兼容性。

Conclusion: SEAOTTER在极端带宽和功耗约束下，兼顾了学习型压缩的紧凑性与标准JPEG的广泛可用性，显著优于现有方案。

Original Abstract: In robotics systems, vast amounts of visual data are easily captured at high resolution using low-cost, low-power hardware. Yet, limited bandwidth and on-device compute resources prevent full utilization when transmitted via conventional codecs like JPEG/MPEG. Newer codecs, like AV1/AVIF, improve the rate-distortion trade-off, but demand far more resources for encoding, impractical without custom ASICs. Recent asymmetric autoencoders deliver high quality under extreme power and bandwidth constraints, but add prohibitive decoding cost and use bespoke formats that ignore decades of infrastructure built around standards like JPEG. To address these limitations, we introduce a compression framework for cloud robotics based on a Sensor Embedded Autoencoder paired with a One-Time Transcode for Efficient Reconstruction (SEAOTTER). Because the sensor, cloud, and consumer stages face very different power and bandwidth budgets, SEAOTTER combines the compactness of a learned latent with the broad usability of a standard JPEG file. Since naive transcoding degrades performance, we propose a learnable JPEG color and quantization transform that enables increased accuracy for global, dense, and vision-language-based perception. Using SEAOTTER, we train both general-purpose and task-aware transcoding pipelines for a pre-trained, frozen encoder. At a compression ratio of 200:1 and compared to AVIF, we observe 7 times faster encoding, 3.5 times faster decoding, and +8% ImageNet top-1 accuracy, while retaining compatibility with JPEG infrastructure. Our code is available at https://github.com/UT-SysML/seaotter .

中文摘要翻译: 在机器人系统中，使用低成本、低功耗硬件可以轻松捕获大量高分辨率视觉数据。然而，有限的带宽和机载计算资源限制了通过传统编解码器（如JPEG/MPEG）传输时的充分利用。新型编解码器（如AV1/AVIF）改善了率失真权衡，但需要更多的编码资源，在没有定制ASIC的情况下难以实用。最新的不对称自编码器在极端功耗和带宽约束下提供了高质量，但增加了高昂的解码成本，且使用定制格式忽视了围绕JPEG等标准建立的基础设施。为解决这些限制，我们提出了一种基于传感器嵌入式自编码器与一次性转码高效重建（SEAOTTER）的云机器人压缩框架。由于传感器、云端和消费端面临截然不同的功耗和带宽预算，SEAOTTER结合了学习型潜变量的紧凑性与标准JPEG文件的广泛可用性。由于朴素转码会降低性能，我们提出了一种可学习的JPEG颜色和量化变换，能够提高全局、密集和视觉语言感知的准确性。使用SEAOTTER，我们为预训练且冻结的编码器训练了通用和任务感知的转码流水线。在200:1的压缩比下，与AVIF相比，我们观察到编码速度提升7倍，解码速度提升3.5倍，ImageNet top-1准确率提高8%，同时保持与JPEG基础设施的兼容性。我们的代码可在https://github.com/UT-SysML/seaotter获取。

</details>

### [141] [Short-Term Synaptic Plasticity Stabilizes Goal-Conditioned Dynamics in a PFC-Inspired Reservoir Model for Multistep Goal-Directed Action Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03481)
*Jin Nakamura,Yuichi Katori*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文研究短时突触可塑性（STP）如何通过动态调制有效连接，在行为时间尺度上将目标信息稳定为可用的目标条件动力学，从而提升噪声环境下目标导向动作选择的鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 前额叶皮层（PFC）通过递归回路维持目标信息以指导动作规划，但在行为时间尺度上如何将目标信息保持为可用的动作相关形式尚不清楚。本文探究短时突触可塑性（STP）是否能稳定目标信息，使其形成可用于动作的目标条件动力学。

Method: 将STP融入PFC启发的储层计算模型，结合基底节启发的时序差分学习进行读出层训练。在含延迟执行的多步目标导向动作选择任务中，评估有无STP的配对模型（各100个独立生成网络）的表现，并进行了状态噪声测试、时间分辨解码、状态空间可分性分析、动作价值差异分析、增益匹配与STP状态扰动对照、有效连接分析及时间常数网格搜索。

Result: 无噪声时有无STP模型均表现良好（91.8% vs 75.8%）；加入状态噪声后，无STP模型成功率降至49.5%，而STP模型几乎不变（89.2%）。STP在延迟期将目标信息保持为动作相关的目标条件动力学，有效连接分析显示STP使延迟期出现目标特异性模式且随时间增强，而无STP时有效连接时间不变。网格搜索发现易化主导的STP时间常数范围与高成功率相关。

Conclusion: STP通过动态调制目标依赖的有效递归连接，支持鲁棒的目标条件动力学，为理解前额叶皮层如何在噪声环境下维持动作可用的目标信息提供了计算机制。

Original Abstract: The prefrontal cortex (PFC) maintains goal information for action planning, but how recurrent circuits preserve it in an action-usable form over behavioral timescales remains unclear. Here we ask whether short-term synaptic plasticity (STP) can stabilize goal information as action-usable, goal-conditioned dynamics. We incorporated STP into a PFC-inspired reservoir computing model with basal-ganglia-inspired temporal-difference readout learning, and evaluated paired models with and without STP across 100 independently generated networks in a multistep goal-directed action-selection task with delayed execution. Goal identity was highly decodable during the delay even without STP, so STP was not required to form a linearly readable goal representation. Under state noise, however, success without STP fell from 75.8% to 49.5%, whereas the model with STP remained essentially unchanged (91.8% without noise versus 89.2% under noise; paired Cohen's dz=1.31). Time-resolved decoding, state-space separability, and action-value-difference analyses showed that STP preserved goal information as action-relevant goal-conditioned dynamics available at later action opportunities. Gain-matched and STP-state perturbation controls argued against a simple fixed recurrent-scaling explanation and supported online, history-dependent synaptic modulation. Effective-connectivity analyses showed delay-period goal-specific patterning that increased toward the later part of the trial with STP, where it should be read as goal- and task-state-conditioned patterning; effective connectivity without STP was time-invariant. A grid search identified a facilitation-dominant range of STP time constants associated with high success rates. These results suggest that STP supports robust goal-conditioned dynamics through dynamic modulation of goal-dependent effective recurrent connectivity.

中文摘要翻译: 前额叶皮层（PFC）保持目标信息以进行动作规划，但递归回路如何在行为时间尺度上将其保持为可用的动作相关形式仍不清楚。本文探究短时突触可塑性（STP）是否能将目标信息稳定为可用的、目标条件动力学。我们将STP融入PFC启发的储层计算模型，并结合基底节启发的时序差分学习进行读出层训练，在含延迟执行的多步目标导向动作选择任务中，评估了有无STP的配对模型（各100个独立生成网络）。在延迟期，即使没有STP，目标身份也具有高度可解码性，因此STP并非形成线性可读目标表征所必需。然而，在状态噪声下，无STP模型成功率从75.8%降至49.5%，而含STP模型几乎保持不变（无噪声91.8% vs 有噪声89.2%；配对Cohen's dz=1.31）。时间分辨解码、状态空间可分性及动作价值差异分析显示，STP将目标信息保持为可在后续动作时机使用的动作相关目标条件动力学。增益匹配和STP状态扰动对照实验排除了简单的固定递归缩放解释，支持在线、历史依赖的突触调制。有效连接分析显示，含STP时延迟期出现目标特异性模式，并在试验后期增强——即在需要读出目标与任务状态条件模式时增强；而无STP时有效连接是时间不变的。网格搜索识别出与高成功率相关的易化主导型STP时间常数范围。这些结果表明，STP通过动态调制目标依赖的有效递归连接，支持鲁棒的目标条件动力学。

</details>


### [142] [BEAST3D: Animal behavioral analysis and neural encoding from multi-view video via Gaussian splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02937)
*Yanchen Wang,Lenny Aharon,Wangshu Zhu,Kyle Daruwalla,Linghua Zhang,Jiaru Zou,Selmaan Chettih,Helen Hou,Liam Paninski,Matthew R Whiteway*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: BEAST3D是一个自监督预训练框架，通过从无标注的多视角视频中学习3D视觉表示，实现动物行为分析，可仅用四个视角重建3D结构，并有效迁移到新视角合成、多视角姿态估计和神经编码等下游任务。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法面临两难：监督式姿态估计需要大量人工标注，而通用3D重建模型在实验室动物实验的稀疏视角和专业图像上表现不佳，无法充分利用多视角视频中的3D信息。

Method: BEAST3D使用视觉Transformer预测3D高斯泼溅（3D Gaussian Splats），通过可微渲染重建保留视角，同时从背景中分割动物。模型直接利用已知相机参数进行条件化，无需像通用模型那样从密集重叠视角估计相机几何。

Result: 在四个物种上进行了全面评估，BEAST3D学习到丰富的视角不变特征，在新视角合成、多视角姿态估计和神经编码三个下游任务上均有效迁移，验证了学习到的3D表示的质量。

Conclusion: BEAST3D为现代多视角实验室记录中的行为分析建立了一个充分利用3D结构的通用框架，解决了动物实验中3D行为分析的标注困难与视角稀疏问题。

Original Abstract: Multi-view video recordings are increasingly used to capture the 3D movements of animals in experimental settings, yet extracting rich 3D representations from these recordings remains challenging. Supervised pose estimation requires extensive manual annotation, while general-purpose 3D reconstruction models trained on generic scene datasets fail on the specialized imagery and sparse-view setting of laboratory experiments. We address these limitations with BEAST3D, a self-supervised pretraining framework that learns 3D visual representations from unlabeled, calibrated multi-view video. BEAST3D uses a vision transformer to predict 3D Gaussian splats that reconstruct held-out views through differentiable rendering, while simultaneously segmenting the animal from the background. BEAST3D reconstructs 3D structure with as few as four views by conditioning directly on known camera parameters--unlike general-purpose models, which must estimate camera geometry from dense overlapping viewpoints that are seldom available in lab settings. Through comprehensive evaluation across four species, we demonstrate that BEAST3D produces rich, viewpoint-invariant features that transfer effectively to three downstream tasks: novel view synthesis, which validates the quality of the learned 3D representations; multi-view pose estimation, which provides the sparse keypoint trajectories widely used in behavioral analysis; and neural encoding, which relates 3D behavioral features to simultaneously recorded neural activity. BEAST3D thus establishes a versatile framework for behavioral analysis that leverages 3D structure in modern multi-view laboratory recordings.

中文摘要翻译: 多视角视频录制越来越多地被用于捕捉实验环境中动物的3D运动，但从这些录制中提取丰富的3D表示仍然具有挑战性。监督式姿态估计需要大量人工标注，而基于通用场景数据集训练的通用3D重建模型在实验室实验的专业图像和稀疏视角设置下表现不佳。我们通过BEAST3D解决了这些局限性，这是一个自监督预训练框架，能够从未标注的、已标定的多视角视频中学习3D视觉表示。BEAST3D使用视觉Transformer预测3D高斯泼溅，通过可微渲染重建保留视角，同时将动物从背景中分割出来。BEAST3D通过直接以已知相机参数为条件，仅用四个视角即可重建3D结构——这与通用模型不同，后者必须从实验室环境中很少具备的密集重叠视角来估计相机几何。通过在四个物种上的全面评估，我们证明BEAST3D产生丰富的、视角不变的特征，这些特征有效迁移到三个下游任务：新视角合成（验证了学习到的3D表示的质量）、多视角姿态估计（提供行为分析中广泛使用的稀疏关键点轨迹）以及神经编码（将3D行为特征与同时记录的神经活动相关联）。因此，BEAST3D为行为分析建立了一个利用现代多视角实验室记录中3D结构的通用框架。

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Human-in-the-Loop Contextual Bandits for Short-Term Rental Dynamic Pricing: Structural Equivalence of Historical Warm-Up and Approval-Gated Live Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02595)
*Oleg Miroshnichenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出人类参与的门控老虎机框架（HITL-GB），利用历史定价数据作为暖启动材料，将短期租赁市场的冷启动周期从约150轮压缩至约30轮，并证明人类审批约束在高风险领域中可转化为统计资产而非部署限制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 短期租赁市场的动态定价面临三大挑战：定价决策财务风险高、算法需要可解释性、市场反馈稀疏（每晚仅一个预订结果）。纯在线老虎机学习的冷启动周期长达数周甚至数月，在实践中不可行。因此需要一种能在人类审批约束下高效初始化定价策略的方法。

Method: 提出HITL-GB（Human-in-the-Loop Gated Bandit）框架：上下文老虎机算法生成价格建议，人类代理保留接受、修改或拒绝建议的最终权威。证明在审批约束下，历史定价数据（来自先前确定性策略）在结构上等价于在线策略暖启动数据，可用于初始化老虎机的后验分布。形式化定义了审批门控奖励信号，推导了基于正则化岭回归的历史数据暖启动流程。

Result: 在真实短期租赁生产数据上（匿名城市市场，2个房间，2022年4月至2026年4月，共1,461个夜间定价场景）验证：暖启动流程将层次化因子汤普森采样（HF-TS）家族智能体的有效冷启动从约150轮压缩至约30轮。

Conclusion: 结构等价性结果具有领域无关性：任何需要人类审批的高风险领域（包括临床药物剂量、信贷发放、内容审核、放射诊断）都满足相同条件，可从同一暖启动策略中受益。在受监管行业中，强制性人类监督不是部署约束，而是统计资产。

Original Abstract: Dynamic pricing in short-term rental (STR) markets presents a distinctive challenge for online learning algorithms: pricing decisions carry significant financial risk, operators require explainability, and market feedback is sparse (one booking outcome per listed night). We introduce the Human-in-the-Loop Gated Bandit (HITL-GB) framework, in which a contextual bandit algorithm generates price recommendations but a human agent retains authority to accept, modify, or reject each recommendation before it is applied. We show that under this approval constraint, historical pricing data -- collected under a prior deterministic policy -- is structurally equivalent to on-policy warm-up data for initialising the bandit's posterior, bypassing the weeks-to-months cold-start period that renders pure online bandit learning impractical in sparse-feedback markets. We formalise the approval-gated reward signal, derive a regularised ridge-regression warm-up procedure from historical episodes, and validate the approach on real STR production data (anonymised urban market, 2 rooms, April 2022 -- April 2026, 1,461 nightly pricing episodes). Our warm-up procedure compresses effective cold-start from ~150 episodes to ~30 episodes when initialising agents from the Hierarchical Factored Thompson Sampling (HF-TS) family. We further argue that the structural equivalence result is domain-agnostic: any high-stakes domain where human approval is legally or operationally required -- including clinical drug dosing, credit origination, content moderation, and radiological diagnosis -- satisfies the same conditions and benefits from the same warm-up strategy. In regulated industries, mandatory human oversight is thus a statistical asset rather than a deployment constraint.

中文摘要翻译: 短期租赁（STR）市场的动态定价为在线学习算法带来了一项独特的挑战：定价决策伴随着显著的财务风险，运营商需要可解释性，而市场反馈是稀疏的（每列出的夜间仅有一个预订结果）。我们引入了人类参与的门控老虎机（HITL-GB）框架，其中上下文老虎机算法生成价格建议，但人类代理保留在价格建议被应用之前接受、修改或拒绝每条建议的最终权威。我们证明，在这一审批约束下，历史定价数据——在先前确定性策略下收集的——在结构上等价于用于初始化老虎机后验分布的在线策略暖启动数据，从而绕过了数周到数月的冷启动期，而正是该冷启动期使得纯在线老虎机学习在稀疏反馈市场中变得不切实际。我们形式化定义了审批门控奖励信号，从历史 episodes 中推导出正则化岭回归暖启动流程，并在真实 STR 生产数据上验证了该方法（匿名城市市场，2个房间，2022年4月至2026年4月，共1,461个夜间定价 episodes）。我们的暖启动流程在从层次化因子汤普森采样（HF-TS）家族初始化智能体时，将有效冷启动从约150轮压缩至约30轮。我们进一步论证，结构等价性结果是领域无关的：任何需要人类审批作为法律或运营要求的高风险领域——包括临床药物剂量、信贷发放、内容审核和放射诊断——都满足相同的条件，并能从相同的暖启动策略中受益。在受监管行业中，强制性人类监督因此是一种统计资产，而非部署约束。

</details>


### [144] [Spectral Asymptotics of Neural Network Loss Landscapes: An Exact Decomposition of the Curvature Exponent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02596)
*Anherutowa Calvo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了神经网络中Hessian曲率指数α随层类型变化的根本原因，提出了谱对齐分解理论，并基于此推导出架构自适应的预条件子Spectral Newton，在视觉基准上超越AdamW。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 解释为什么不同层类型（卷积、注意力、MLP）中Hessian曲率指数α存在系统性差异（α≈2, ≈1, <1），并建立统一的理论框架来理解和预测这一现象。

Method: 提出谱对齐分解：α = 2 + dlogΦ_k / dlogσ_k，将曲率指数分解为常数项与Kronecker因子特征基与梯度奇异方向之间对齐度Φ_k的对数导数之和。进一步推导出谱传递恒等式s = αγ，将曲率指数、有效梯度秩衰减指数γ和Hessian衰减指数s联系起来。基于这些理论，设计架构自适应预条件子T(σ;α)并实现Spectral Newton优化器。

Result: 在93个层、5种架构和3个数据集上，通过独立拟合α和γ，谱传递恒等式s=αγ的预测中位误差仅约2%，且无自由参数。zeta函数对参与率的界表明每层曲率集中于一个有效方向。Spectral Newton在α≈2的视觉基准上超越AdamW。

Conclusion: 曲率指数α的变化可以归结为谱对齐的几何问题，LayerNorm、残差连接和softmax头通过影响谱对齐来解释α的差异。谱传递恒等式提供了强有力的理论约束，架构自适应的预条件子具有实际优化价值。

Original Abstract: The curvature exponent $α$ in $h_k \propto σ_k^α$ -- governing how Hessian eigenvalues scale with gradient singular values -- varies systematically across layer types ($α\approx 2$ for convolutions, $\approx 1$ for transformer attention, $< 1$ for MLP up-projections). Why? We prove the Spectral Alignment Decomposition: $α= 2 + d\logΦ_k / d\logσ_k$, where $Φ_k$ measures alignment between Kronecker factor eigenbases and gradient singular directions. This reduces "why does $α$ vary?" to a geometric question we answer for LayerNorm, residual connections, and softmax heads. The decomposition implies a spectral transfer identity $s = αγ$ linking curvature exponent, effective gradient rank-decay $γ$, and Hessian decay exponent $s$. The identity is algebraic; its empirical content is that $α$ and $γ$, fit on independent data (HVPs vs. SVD), recover $s$ to ~2% median error across 93 layers, five architectures, and three datasets -- with no free parameters. A zeta-function bound on participation ratio shows curvature concentrates onto effectively one direction per layer. As a proof of concept, we derive the architecture-adaptive preconditioner $T(σ;α)$ and show that Spectral Newton -- implementing $T$ in the gradient singular basis -- outperforms AdamW on vision benchmarks where $α\approx 2$.

中文摘要翻译: 曲率指数α——在h_k ∝ σ_k^α中控制Hessian特征值如何随梯度奇异值缩放——在不同层类型之间呈现系统性变化（卷积层α≈2，Transformer注意力层α≈1，MLP上投影层α<1）。为何如此？我们证明了谱对齐分解：α = 2 + dlogΦ_k / dlogσ_k，其中Φ_k衡量Kronecker因子特征基与梯度奇异方向之间的对齐程度。这将“α为何变化”简化为一个几何问题，我们针对LayerNorm、残差连接和softmax头给出了回答。该分解蕴含一个谱传递恒等式s = αγ，将曲率指数、有效梯度秩衰减指数γ和Hessian衰减指数s联系起来。该等式是代数性的；其实证意义在于，独立拟合的α和γ（分别来自HVP和SVD）在93个层、五种架构和三个数据集上恢复出s的中位误差约为2%——且无任何自由参数。基于zeta函数的参与比率界表明曲率集中在每层的一个有效方向上。作为概念验证，我们推导出架构自适应预条件子T(σ;α)，并展示了在梯度奇异基中实施T的Spectral Newton优化器在α≈2的视觉基准上优于AdamW。

</details>


### [145] [Making Brain-Computer Interfaces More Secure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02597)
*Md Fahimul Kabir Chowdhury,Gahangir Hossain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级定制CNN架构，用于提升EEG-Based BCI系统在梯度对抗攻击下的鲁棒性。在两个EEG数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比EEGNet、DeepConvNet和SleepEEGNet等现有模型，在对抗扰动下具有更好的分类性能和鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有EEG-Based BCI研究主要关注分类准确率提升，忽视了安全性和鲁棒性。近期研究表明EEG-Based BCI易受对抗攻击，微小的精心设计的扰动即可导致误诊，因此评估模型对抗鲁棒性对可靠部署至关重要。

Method: 提出一种轻量级定制卷积神经网络（CNN）架构，在梯度对抗攻击场景下，使用两个EEG数据集进行评估，并与EEGNet、DeepConvNet、SleepEEGNet三种针对EEG定制的CNN模型进行对比。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提模型在对抗扰动下的分类性能持续优于基线模型，展现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 轻量级架构在增强EEG-Based BCI系统在对抗条件下的可靠性方面具有潜力。

Original Abstract: The development of brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) based on electroencephalograms (EEGs) has advanced significantly mainly to machine learning. Although the majority of earlier research has been on increasing classification accuracy, relatively little focus has been placed on security and robustness. According to recent research, EEG-based BCIs are susceptible to adversarial attacks, which can cause misdiagnosis due to minute, well-crafted disturbances. Evaluating model robustness against such perturbations is therefore critical for ensuring reliable deployment. In this study, we propose a lightweight custom Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) architecture to investigate adversarial robustness in EEG-based BCIs. The suggested method is assessed using two EEG datasets and contrasted with three novel CNN models tailored to EEG, namely EEGNet, DeepConvNet, and SleepEEGNet, under gradient-based adversarial attack scenarios. According to experimental findings, the suggested model continuously performs better in classification under adversarial perturbations compared to baseline models, indicating improved robustness. These findings highlight the potential of lightweight architectures for enhancing the reliability of EEG-based BCI systems under adversarial conditions.

中文摘要翻译: 基于脑电图（EEG）的脑机接口（BCI）的发展在很大程度上得益于机器学习而取得了显著进展。尽管早期研究主要致力于提高分类准确率，但对安全性和鲁棒性的关注相对较少。近期研究表明，基于EEG的BCI系统易受对抗攻击，微小的精心设计的扰动即可导致误诊。因此，评估模型对此类扰动的鲁棒性对于确保可靠部署至关重要。在本研究中，我们提出一种轻量级定制卷积神经网络（CNN）架构，用于研究EEG-Based BCI中的对抗鲁棒性。该方法使用两个EEG数据集进行评估，并与三种针对EEG定制的CNN模型（即EEGNet、DeepConvNet和SleepEEGNet）在基于梯度的对抗攻击场景下进行对比。实验结果表明，所提模型在对抗扰动下的分类性能持续优于基线模型，展现出更强的鲁棒性。这些发现凸显了轻量级架构在增强EEG-Based BCI系统在对抗条件下可靠性的潜力。

</details>


### [146] [Assessing Region-Level EEG Contributions to Cognitive Workload Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02598)
*Jacob Wong,Sohan Singh,Prannaya Gupta,Jin Xing Ang,Kritika Johari,U-Xuan Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种区域级评估框架，通过跨四个公开EEG数据集的大规模分析，发现额叶和前中央电极组在工作负荷预测中表现最优，且比全头皮基线提升约15-20%的相对排名位置，同时使用更少的电极。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管EEG被广泛用于工作负荷评估，但不同任务、数据集和受试者之间，区域级EEG贡献的一致性仍不明确。因此需要系统评估各头皮区域在工作负荷预测中的稳定性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出了一个区域级评估框架：从属于解剖学定义的头皮区域的电极中提取特征，分别训练和评估模型；使用模型无关、基于性能的方法量化区域重要性；在混合受试者和受试者独立两种评估协议下进行实验，并采用基于排名的策略聚合结果以保证鲁棒性。

Result: 在所有数据集和受试者独立评估中，额叶电极组以更少的电极数量在全头皮基线的相对排名位置上提升了约15-20%；额中央区域显示出最稳定的预测效用，而后部和枕部区域在不同实验条件下的一致性较差。

Conclusion: 工作负荷相关的EEG信息最稳定地保留在额叶和额中央电极组中，这支持设计高效且可泛化的基于EEG的工作负荷监测系统。

Original Abstract: Accurate and generalizable estimation of cognitive workload from electroencephalography (EEG) is critical for human-centered and safety-critical systems. Although EEG is widely used for workload assessment, the consistency of region-level EEG contributions across tasks, datasets, and subjects remains unclear. This paper presents a region-level evaluation framework for EEG-based workload prediction in which models are trained and evaluated using features extracted exclusively from electrodes belonging to anatomically defined scalp regions. We perform a large-scale analysis across four publicly available EEG workload datasets spanning diverse task demands, recording hardware, and electrode montages. Region importance is quantified using a model-agnostic, performance-based approach under both mixed-subject and subject-independent evaluation protocols, with results aggregated using a rank-based strategy to ensure robustness across experimental configurations. Across all datasets and subject-independent evaluations, frontal electrode groups outperform the full-scalp baseline by approximately 15-20% in relative rank position while using substantially fewer electrodes. Fronto-central regions exhibit the most stable predictive utility, whereas posterior and occipital regions contribute less consistently across experimental conditions. These findings indicate that workload-relevant EEG information is most consistently retained within frontal and fronto-central electrode groups, supporting the design of efficient and generalizable EEG-based workload monitoring systems.

中文摘要翻译: 从脑电图（EEG）中准确且可泛化地估计认知工作负荷，对于以人为中心和安全性关键的系统至关重要。尽管EEG被广泛用于工作负荷评估，但不同任务、数据集和受试者之间区域级EEG贡献的一致性仍不明确。本文提出了一种基于EEG的工作负荷预测的区域级评估框架，其中模型使用仅从属于解剖学定义的头皮区域的电极中提取的特征进行训练和评估。我们在四个公开可用的EEG工作负荷数据集上进行了大规模分析，这些数据集涵盖了不同的任务需求、记录硬件和电极排列。使用模型无关、基于性能的方法，在混合受试者和受试者独立两种评估协议下量化区域重要性，并通过基于排名的策略聚合结果以确保跨实验配置的鲁棒性。在所有数据集和受试者独立评估中，额叶电极组在全头皮基线的相对排名位置上提升了约15-20%，同时使用的电极数量显著减少。额中央区域表现出最稳定的预测效用，而顶叶和枕叶区域在不同实验条件下贡献的一致性较低。这些发现表明，工作负荷相关的EEG信息最稳定地保留在额叶和额中央电极组中，为设计高效且可泛化的基于EEG的工作负荷监测系统提供了支持。

</details>


### [147] [Learning to See via Epiretinal Implant Stimulation in silico with Model-Based Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03118)
*Jacob Lavoie,Marwan Besrour,William Lemaire,Jean Rouat,Réjean Fontaine,Eric Plourde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在强化学习环境rlretina中，利用各向同性和各向异性的电刺激形状（笔触）来渲染视网膜图像，训练深度强化学习智能体学习组合这些形状以生成更清晰的视觉图像，从而改善人工视网膜假体的视觉感知效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视网膜疾病（如黄斑变性、视网膜色素变性）导致感光层退化，现有的视网膜上植入物（epiretinal implants）电刺激存活的视网膜神经节细胞时会产生沿轴突束拉伸的各向异性形状（类似笔触），影响视觉清晰度。尽管可以通过避开轴突束来获得各向同性的像素状形状，但视觉感知质量仍有限。因此，本文希望利用各向异性和各向同性形状的组合来渲染更可辨的图像，以提升人工视觉的清晰度。

Method: 本文构建了一个名为rlretina的强化学习环境，将任务形式化为基于笔触的渲染任务。训练深度强化学习智能体学习组合各向同性和各向异性的电刺激形状（笔触）来形成图像。采用基于模型的生成方式，使用经心理物理学验证的轴突映射模型来模拟不同虚拟患者的感知图像。对比了基于误差的指标和基于感知的指标作为奖励函数的有效性。

Result: 实验表明，训练的深度强化学习智能体能够生成比基线方法（naive method）更可辨（intelligible）的图像，且适用于不同虚拟患者的视网膜模型。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种新的视网膜上电刺激图像生成方法，即利用各向异性光幻视形状进行基于笔触的渲染，为改善人工视觉修复中的视敏度提供了新的思路，是该方向的第一步工作。

Original Abstract: Objective: Diseases such as age-related macular degeneration and retinitis pigmentosa cause the degradation of the photoreceptor layer. One approach to restore vision is to electrically stimulate the surviving retinal ganglion cells with a microelectrode array such as epiretinal implants. Epiretinal implants are known to generate visible anisotropic shapes elongated along the axon fascicles of neighboring retinal ganglion cells. Recent work has demonstrated that to obtain isotropic pixel-like shapes, it is possible to map axon fascicles and avoid stimulating them by inactivating electrodes or lowering stimulation current levels. Avoiding axon fascicle stimulation aims to remove brushstroke-like shapes in favor of a more reduced set of pixel-like shapes. Approach: In this study, we propose the use of isotropic and anisotropic shapes to render intelligible images on the retina of a virtual patient in a reinforcement learning environment named rlretina. The environment formalizes the task as using brushstrokes in a stroke-based rendering task. Main Results: We train a deep reinforcement learning agent that learns to assemble isotropic and anisotropic shapes to form an image. We investigate which error-based or perception-based metrics is adequate to reward the agent. The agent is trained in a model-based data generation fashion using the psychophysically validated axon map model to render images as perceived by different virtual patients. We show that the agent can generate more intelligible images compared to the naive method in different virtual patients. Significance: This work shares a new way to address epiretinal stimulation that constitutes a first step towards improving visual acuity in artificially-restored vision using anisotropic phosphenes.

中文摘要翻译: 目的：年龄相关性黄斑变性和视网膜色素变性等疾病会导致感光层退化。恢复视力的一种方法是通过微电极阵列（如视网膜上植入物）对存活的视网膜神经节细胞进行电刺激。已知视网膜上植入物产生的可见光幻视形状会沿着邻近视网膜神经节细胞的轴突束呈各向异性拉伸。最近的研究表明，为了获得各向同性的像素状形状，可以通过映射轴突束并采用失活电极或降低刺激电流水平的方式来避免刺激轴突束。避免轴突束刺激旨在去除笔触状的形状，转而使用更有限的像素状形状集。方法：在本研究中，我们提出在名为rlretina的强化学习环境中，利用各向同性和各向异性形状在虚拟患者的视网膜上渲染可辨别的图像。该环境将任务形式化为基于笔触的渲染任务中的笔触使用问题。主要结果：我们训练了一个深度强化学习智能体，该智能体学会组合各向同性和各向异性形状以形成图像。我们研究了基于误差或基于感知的指标中哪种适合作为奖励函数。智能体以基于模型的数据生成方式进行训练，使用经心理物理学验证的轴突映射模型来渲染不同虚拟患者感知的图像。我们表明，与基线方法相比，该智能体能在不同虚拟患者中生成更可辨别的图像。意义：这项工作分享了一种解决视网膜上电刺激问题的新方法，为利用各向异性光幻视改善人工视觉修复中的视敏度迈出了第一步。

</details>


### [148] [Testing the Test: Score-Direction Instability in Class-Split Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02601)
*Alejandro Ascarate,Leo Lebrat,Rodrigo Santa Cruz,Clinton Fookes,Olivier Salvado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了数据集内类划分评估在异常检测中可能因异常类与正常分布在表示空间重叠而失效，导致分数崩塌或反转，并提出邻域类泄露诊断指标来预测分数方向的不稳定性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据集内类划分评估被广泛用作无条件的分布外异常检测的代理指标，但当held-out异常类与正常分布在表示空间重叠时，该评估协议可能变得病态，异常分数可能趋于随机或反转，因此需要诊断这种不稳定性。

Method: 提出一种无需训练的简单诊断指标——邻域类泄露（neighborhood class leakage），用于预测异常检测中分数方向的不稳定性。

Result: 在Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10和Imagenette数据集上，无论是在像素空间还是VAE隐空间，邻域类泄露指标都能有效预测分数方向的稳定性。实验表明当异常类与正常类在表示空间中重叠时，异常检测性能会严重退化。

Conclusion: 类划分的异常检测基准应被视为几何依赖的压力测试，而非无条件异常检测能力的证据。

Original Abstract: Within-dataset class-split evaluation is widely used as a proxy for fully unconditional out-of-distribution anomaly detection. We show that this protocol can become ill-posed when the held-out anomaly class overlaps the normal mixture in representation space. In this regime, anomaly scores may collapse toward chance or even invert, and the preferred score direction can depend on the unknown anomaly class. We introduce a simple training-free diagnostic, neighborhood class leakage, and show that it predicts score-direction instability across Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Imagenette, in both pixel and VAE latent spaces. Our results suggest that class-split AD benchmarks should be treated as geometry-dependent stress tests rather than unconditional evidence of anomaly-detection ability.

中文摘要翻译: 数据集内类划分评估被广泛用作全无条件的分布外异常检测的代理指标。我们表明，当被留出的异常类在表示空间中与正常混合分布重叠时，该评估协议可能变得病态。在这种状态下，异常得分可能趋近于随机甚至发生反转，且最优的得分方向可能取决于未知的异常类。我们提出了一种无需训练的简单诊断指标——邻域类泄露，并证明该指标能够在Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10和Imagenette数据集上，在像素空间和VAE隐空间中预测得分方向的不稳定性。我们的结果表明，类划分的异常检测基准应被视为依赖几何结构的压力测试，而非无条件异常检测能力的证据。

</details>


### [149] [Graph Mamba Survival Analysis Based on Topology-Aware ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02602)
*Yuanfang Chen,Peiqiang Yan,Yuntao Shou,Qian Zhao,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TopoMamSurv，一种基于拓扑感知排序的图Mamba生存分析框架，通过TAO策略、双向Mamba模块和GCN融合，在WSI生存分析中实现了线性复杂度下的长距离依赖建模与双向空间上下文建模，在五个TCGA数据集上验证了性能优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Transformer在WSI生存分析中O(N²)复杂度导致计算瓶颈；Mamba虽具有线性复杂度，但对输入数据顺序高度敏感，传统节点排序方法（如基于节点度或子图大小）未能充分考虑图数据的拓扑连通性，限制了Mamba的序列建模性能；且Mamba单向架构无法利用图像的双向空间结构。

Method: 提出拓扑感知排序（TAO）策略以适配Mamba对输入顺序的敏感性；设计双向Mamba模块实现图像双向空间上下文建模；集成图卷积网络（GCN），形成"局部聚合-全局捕获"的分层特征学习架构。

Result: 可视化实验证实TAO策略提取的节点具有更高相似性；在五个TCGA数据集上验证了框架的全面性能优势。

Conclusion: 通过TAO、双向语义建模和分层特征融合的系统设计，有效调和了WSI分析中长距离依赖建模、计算效率与空间结构利用之间的矛盾。

Original Abstract: In computational pathology, Whole Slide Images (WSIs) survival analysis is crucial for patient prognosis assessment, but it faces multiple technical challenges. Although the Transformer captures long-range dependencies through its self-attention mechanism, its $O(N^2)$ time complexity causes a severe computational bottleneck in large-scale WSIs graph structures. The Mamba model breaks through the Transformer's computational bottleneck with linear complexity. But, owing to Mamba's high sensitivity to the order of input data, traditional node sorting methods in Graph Mamba, such as those based on node degree or subgraph size, fail to adequately account for the topological connectivity of graph data. This inadequacy consequently restricts the performance of Mamba's sequential modeling. Moreover, its unidirectional architecture cannot leverage the bidirectional spatial structure of images. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a novel Graph Mamba survival analysis framework based on topology-aware ordering (TopoMamSurv) to adapt to the sequential sensitivity of Mamba. Our visualization experiments further confirmed that the nodes extracted through the topology-aware ordering (TAO) strategy indeed exhibit higher similarity. Furthermore, we designed a bidirectional Mamba module and integrated a Graph Convolutional Network (GCN) to achieve bidirectional spatial context modeling of images, forming a hierarchical feature learning architecture for "local aggregation - global capture." This framework effectively reconciles the contradiction between long-range dependency modeling, computational efficiency, and spatial structure utilization in WSIs analysis through its systematic design of TAO, bidirectional semantic modeling, and hierarchical feature fusion. This framework has been validated for its comprehensive performance advantage on five TCGA datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 在计算病理学中，全切片图像的生存分析对于患者预后评估至关重要，但面临多项技术挑战。虽然Transformer通过自注意力机制捕获长距离依赖，但其O(N²)时间复杂度在大规模WSI图结构中造成严重的计算瓶颈。Mamba模型以线性复杂度突破了Transformer的计算瓶颈。然而，由于Mamba对输入数据顺序的高度敏感性，图Mamba中传统的节点排序方法（如基于节点度或子图大小）未能充分考虑图数据的拓扑连通性，进而限制了Mamba序列建模的性能。此外，其单向架构无法利用图像的双向空间结构。针对这些挑战，本文提出了一种基于拓扑感知排序的新型图Mamba生存分析框架（TopoMamSurv），以适应Mamba对序列顺序的敏感性。我们的可视化实验进一步证实，通过拓扑感知排序策略提取的节点确实表现出更高的相似性。此外，我们设计了一个双向Mamba模块，并集成图卷积网络以实现图像的双向空间上下文建模，形成了"局部聚合-全局捕获"的分层特征学习架构。该框架通过TAO、双向语义建模和分层特征融合的系统设计，有效调和了WSI分析中长距离依赖建模、计算效率与空间结构利用之间的矛盾。该框架在五个TCGA数据集上验证了其全面的性能优势。

</details>


### [150] [Auditable Climate Risk Intelligence from Fragmented ESG Data: Deterministic Orchestration and Imbalance-Aware Learning for Scope 1-3 Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02604)
*Karan Sehgal,Khawar Naveed Bhatti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个确定性气候风险智能框架，整合单一数据源编排、时序异常检测、不平衡集成学习和可解释性治理，用于可审计的ESG验证，并构建了合成基准数据集以支持开放可重复性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: ESG与气候风险数据在Scope 1/2/3报告环境中高度碎片化，传统验证流程缺乏来源感知的可审计性、隐藏漂移检测以及面向可重复性的治理机制。

Method: 框架包含：时序漂移分析、SMOTE稀有事件优化、集成学习、来源感知编排、TreeSHAP可解释性；评估使用分类指标（召回率、F1、ROC AUC）、校准指标（ECE、Brier分数）及治理导向的审计追踪完整性指标。

Result: 通过分层五折交叉验证和配对显著性检验报告均值和标准差；框架在分类、校准和审计追踪完整性上优于统计分类器、异常检测方法、时序预测基线和阈值系统。

Conclusion: 该框架将ESG报告重新定义为确定性的气候风险治理基础设施，支持可重复性、可解释性和可操作审计性。

Original Abstract: ESG and climate risk data remain fragmented across heterogeneous Scope 1, Scope 2, and Scope 3 reporting environments, while conventional validation pipelines lack provenance aware auditability, hidden drift detection, and reproducibility oriented governance. This paper proposes a deterministic climate risk intelligence framework integrating single source of truth orchestration, temporal anomaly detection, imbalance aware ensemble learning, and explainability oriented governance for auditable ESG validation. To support open reproducibility, we construct and release a synthetic ESG validation benchmark calibrated against publicly reported characteristics of the GHG Protocol, PCAF, and ISSB standards. The methodology incorporates temporal drift analysis, SMOTE based rare event optimization, ensemble learning, provenance aware orchestration, and TreeSHAP based interpretability for governance inspection and audit reconstruction. We evaluate the framework against statistical classifiers, anomaly detection methods, temporal forecasting baselines, and a threshold based system using classification metrics (recall, F1, ROC AUC), calibration metrics (ECE, Brier score), and a governance oriented audit trace completeness metric measuring the fraction of flagged anomalies for which a deterministic source to escalation provenance chain can be reconstructed. Results are reported as mean and standard deviation across stratified five fold cross validation with paired significance testing. The framework reframes ESG reporting toward deterministic climate risk governance infrastructure supporting reproducibility, explainability, and operational auditability.

中文摘要翻译: ESG和气候风险数据在异构的Scope 1、Scope 2和Scope 3报告环境中仍然碎片化，而传统验证流程缺乏来源感知的可审计性、隐藏漂移检测以及以可重复性为导向的治理机制。本文提出一个确定性气候风险智能框架，整合单一数据源编排、时序异常检测、不平衡感知集成学习和可解释性导向治理，用于可审计的ESG验证。为支持开放可重复性，我们构建并发布了一个合成ESG验证基准数据集，该数据集针对GHG Protocol、PCAF和ISSB标准公开报告的特征进行校准。该方法融合了时序漂移分析、基于SMOTE的稀有事件优化、集成学习、来源感知编排以及基于TreeSHAP的可解释性，用于治理审查和审计重建。我们使用分类指标（召回率、F1、ROC AUC）、校准指标（ECE、Brier分数）以及一个治理导向的审计追踪完整性指标（衡量可重建确定性从源头到升级的来源链的标记异常比例），将框架与统计分类器、异常检测方法、时序预测基线和基于阈值的系统进行评估。结果通过分层五折交叉验证的均值和标准差报告，并附有配对显著性检验。该框架将ESG报告重新定义为支持可重复性、可解释性和可操作审计性的确定性气候风险治理基础设施。

</details>


### [151] [Cross-Modal Contrastive Learning of ECG and Angiography Representations for Severe Stenosis Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02605)
*Nikola Cenikj,Özgün Turgut,Alexander Müller,Alexander Steger,Jan Kehrer,Marcus Brugger,Daniel Rueckert,Philip Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出StenCE预训练框架，从心电图中提取特征进行冠状动脉狭窄风险分层，实现了对严重狭窄的高性能分类。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 冠状动脉狭窄是常见心血管疾病，现有诊断依赖有创的冠状动脉造影，而心电图虽无创、廉价、广泛使用，但缺乏可靠的狭窄特异性信号，无法用于狭窄风险分层，导致无症状患者可能漏诊。

Method: 提出StenCE预训练框架，直接从心电图中提取特征进行患者风险分层。

Result: 在不同狭窄严重度阈值和额外的心电图疾病分类任务上，StenCE在不同心电图编码器上均取得一致的性能提升，优于先前工作；首次在严重狭窄分类中实现高性能。

Conclusion: 成功从心电图中检测到用于狭窄诊断的信号，并首个在严重狭窄分类中达到高性能。

Original Abstract: Coronary artery stenosis is a common cardiovascular disease, with severe, untreated cases posing significant risks of heart attack. Although coronary (X-ray) angiograms remain the standard for stenosis diagnosis, they are invasive, time- and resource-intensive, and therefore only performed on patients with a high probability of disease based on symptoms and prior clinical tests. However, a subset of patients, especially those without symptoms, may remain undiagnosed. Detecting indications of stenosis from ECGs, which are fast, cheap, non-invasive, and thus routinely acquired even in asymptomatic patients, would support early diagnosis. However, as no reliable stenosis-specific signal has been identified in ECGs, they can not currently be used for stenosis risk stratification. To address this, we introduce StenCE, a pretraining framework, allowing stratification of patients based on features derived directly from ECGs. Evaluations across varying stenosis severity thresholds and additional ECG disease classification tasks demonstrate consistent performance improvements across different ECG encoders, outperforming previous work. The obtained models successfully detect signals for stenosis diagnosis in ECGs and are the first to achieve high performance in severe stenosis classification. The source code is available at https://github.com/NikolaCenic/ecg-stenosis-cls.

中文摘要翻译: 冠状动脉狭窄是一种常见的心血管疾病，未经治疗的严重病例存在显著的心梗风险。尽管冠状动脉（X射线）造影仍是狭窄诊断的金标准，但其具有侵入性、耗时且资源密集，因此仅对基于症状和既往临床检查具有高疾病概率的患者进行。然而，部分患者，尤其是无症状患者，可能仍未被诊断。从心电图中检测狭窄指征将有助于早期诊断，因为心电图快速、廉价、无创，甚至对无症状患者也是常规检查。然而，由于心电图中尚未识别出可靠的狭窄特异性信号，目前无法将其用于狭窄风险分层。为解决这一问题，我们提出了StenCE——一个预训练框架，允许基于直接从心电图衍生的特征对患者进行分层。在不同狭窄严重度阈值和额外心电图疾病分类任务上的评估表明，该框架在不同心电图编码器上均取得一致的性能提升，优于先前工作。所获得的模型成功检测到心电图中用于狭窄诊断的信号，并且是首个在严重狭窄分类中实现高性能的工作。源代码可在 https://github.com/NikolaCenic/ecg-stenosis-cls 获取。

</details>


### [152] [ERP-XTTN: Interpretable Prototype-Guided Cross-Attention for Cross-Subject ERP Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02939)
*Charlotte Genevier Wyman,Leanne Hirshfield*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ERP-XTTN，一种基于原型交叉注意力的可解释脑机接口分类器，在无需校准的跨被试泛化场景下，以极小的精度代价实现了结构可解释的ERP分类。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有可解释脑机接口分类器难以在无需校准的条件下实现跨被试泛化，且多数可解释方法依赖事后(post-hoc)分析，缺乏结构上的透明性。

Method: 提出ERP-XTTN交叉注意力架构：将输入EEG片段路由到固定差分波原型，采用仅查询-键的交叉注意力（无值投影），分类完全依赖注意力路由；原型自动从训练集差分波极值中提取。

Result: 在三个公开数据集（BNCI Horizon 2020、HRI Cursor、ERP CORE）上，覆盖8种ERP成分，采用留一被试外(LOSO)评估。3通道时与最佳基线差距为0.018 AUROC，全通道时为0.034 AUROC；误差源于时间灵活性成本（相对EEGNet）和空间利用成本（相对xDAWN+RG）。错误分类具有神经生理可解释性。

Conclusion: ERP-XTTN在因果、无需校准条件下实现了跨多种ERP的泛化，以极小可解释性代价达到竞争性精度；首次在ERP CORE上完成epoch级LOSO基准测试。

Original Abstract: Interpretable brain-computer interface classifiers that generalize across subjects without calibration remain an open challenge. We test whether prototype-based cross-attention can provide competitive, interpretable event-related potential (ERP) classification under deployment-compatible conditions. We propose ERP-XTTN, a cross-attention architecture that routes input EEG patches to fixed difference-wave prototypes via query-key-only cross-attention with no value projection, so classification depends entirely on attention routing and attention faithfulness is structural rather than post-hoc. Prototypes are derived automatically from extrema in the training-fold difference wave. We evaluate across three public sources (BNCI Horizon 2020, HRI Cursor, and ERP CORE) spanning eight ERP components (ERN, LRP, ErrP, N170, P300, N2pc, MMN, N400), using leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) evaluation with causal filtering at two channel counts (3-channel and full montage), against EEGNet and xDAWN with Riemannian geometry (xDAWN+RG). The mean gap between the best baseline and ERP-XTTN was .018 AUROC at 3 channels and .034 at full montage, arising from two largely distinct sources: a temporal-flexibility cost relative to EEGNet and a spatial-exploitation cost relative to xDAWN+RG, the latter driven by signal-to-noise ratio at full montage. Beyond accuracy, the transparent routing reveals cross-subject signal structure that black-box models cannot: false positives resembled true positives more than true negatives did, indicating that classification errors are neurophysiologically explicable. ERP-XTTN generalizes across diverse ERPs under causal, calibration-free conditions with a small interpretability cost at minimal montages. To our knowledge, this is the first epoch-level LOSO benchmark on ERP CORE.

中文摘要翻译: 在无需校准的跨被试泛化场景下，实现可解释的脑机接口分类器仍然是一个开放挑战。我们测试了基于原型交叉注意力机制在满足部署兼容条件下能否提供具有竞争力且可解释的事件相关电位(ERP)分类。我们提出ERP-XTTN，一种交叉注意力架构，通过仅查询-键的交叉注意力（无值投影）将输入EEG片段路由到固定差分波原型，使得分类完全依赖于注意力路由，并且注意力忠实性是结构性的而非事后(post-hoc)的。原型自动从训练折差分波的极值中导出。我们在三个公开来源（BNCI Horizon 2020、HRI Cursor和ERP CORE）上进行评估，涵盖八种ERP成分（ERN、LRP、ErrP、N170、P300、N2pc、MMN、N400），采用留一被试外(LOSO)评估，在两种通道数（3通道和全导联）下使用因果滤波，并以EEGNet和xDAWN结合黎曼几何(xDAWN+RG)作为基线。在3通道设置下，最佳基线与ERP-XTTN的平均AUROC差距为0.018，全导联下为0.034，这种差距源于两个大体独立的部分：相对于EEGNet的时间灵活性代价和相对于xDAWN+RG的空间利用代价，后者在全导联下受信噪比驱动。除精度外，透明的路由机制揭示了黑箱模型无法揭示的跨被试信号结构：假阳性与真阳性的相似度高于真阴性，表明分类错误在神经生理学上是可以解释的。ERP-XTTN在因果、无需校准的条件下实现了跨多种ERP的泛化，在最小导联设置下仅付出极小的可解释性代价。据我们所知，这是ERP CORE上首次的epoch级LOSO基准测试。

</details>


### [153] [ReLoRA: Knowledge-Reusing Adaptation for Fast Rollout of Evolving LLM Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02606)
*Yang Xu,Zihuai Xu,Hongli Xu,Yunming Liao,Zhiwei Yao,Xitong Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReLoRA框架，利用贝叶斯优化实现自适应LoRA初始化，结合计划正则化微调，高效恢复大模型服务中因基座模型更新而失效的LoRA适配器，实现服务就绪加速8.9倍、准确率提升4.6%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大语言模型作为持续演进的在线服务，频繁的基座模型更新会导致先前部署的任务专用LoRA适配器失效。重新训练每个适配器计算成本极高且延迟服务上线，而直接复用原适配器又会因不兼容导致服务质量下降。

Method: ReLoRA包含两个关键优化步骤：1) 自适应LoRA初始化，利用贝叶斯优化融合先前任务适配器信息和基座模型演化信息，构建兼容性感知的初始点；2) 带计划正则化的微调，先通过强正则化将适配器快速导向高质量区域，再放松正则化进行任务特异性精调。

Result: 与基线方法相比，ReLoRA将服务就绪时间降低至原来的1/8.9（加速8.9倍），准确率提升高达4.6%。

Conclusion: ReLoRA通过知识复用的再适配框架，以较低的计算开销高效恢复服务就绪的LoRA适配器，在保持或提升任务性能的同时大幅缩短了模型更新后的恢复时间。

Original Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as continuously evolving services, where frequent base-model updates may invalidate previously deployed task-specific Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapters. For service providers managing numerous downstream model services, retraining each LoRA adapter from scratch for every updated base model is computationally prohibitive and delays service rollout. Meanwhile, the simpler alternative, i.e., naively applying the original LoRA adapter to the updated base model, often leads to degraded service quality due to adapter-backbone incompatibility. To address this problem, we propose ReLoRA, a knowledge-reusing re-adaptation framework that efficiently restores service-ready LoRA adapters for evolving LLM services while preserving or improving task performance. Specifically, ReLoRA comprises two key optimization steps: 1) Adaptive LoRA initialization leverages Bayesian optimization to construct a compatibility-aware starting point by fusing information from both the previously deployed task adapter and the base model's evolution; 2) Fine-tuning with scheduled regularization first rapidly steers the adapter to a high-quality region via strong regularization, followed by relaxed regularization for task-specific refinement. This design enables rapid service-quality recovery with reduced re-adaptation overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReLoRA reduces time-to-readiness by up to 8.9$\times$ and improves accuracy by up to 4.6\% compared to baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型（LLM）越来越多地被部署为持续演进的服务，频繁的基座模型更新可能导致先前部署的任务专用低秩适配（LoRA）适配器失效。对于管理大量下游模型服务的提供商而言，为每个更新的基座模型从头重新训练每个LoRA适配器在计算上代价高昂，并会延迟服务上线。同时，更简单的替代方案——即直接将原始LoRA适配器应用于更新的基座模型——往往因适配器与骨干网络不兼容而导致服务质量下降。针对这一问题，我们提出ReLoRA，一种知识复用的再适配框架，能够高效地为演进的LLM服务恢复服务就绪的LoRA适配器，同时保持或提升任务性能。具体而言，ReLoRA包含两个关键优化步骤：1) 自适应LoRA初始化，利用贝叶斯优化，通过融合先前部署的任务适配器和基座模型演化信息，构建兼容性感知的起始点；2) 带计划正则化的微调，首先通过强正则化将适配器快速引导至高质量区域，随后放松正则化以进行任务特异性精调。该设计能够以较低的再适配开销实现服务质量的快速恢复。大量实验表明，与基线方法相比，ReLoRA将服务就绪时间降低至原来的8.9分之一，准确率提升高达4.6%。

</details>


### [154] [Limit Analysis of Graph Neural Networks with Wireless Conflict Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03794)
*Romina Garcia Camargo,Zhiyang Wang,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了基于稀疏随机几何图（RGG）的图神经网络（GNN）在不同规模无线网络间的可迁移性，通过建立RGG与确定性网格图（DGG）之间的近似关系来量化迁移性能损失，并在链路调度任务中验证了理论结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有GNN在无线资源分配中展现出良好的可迁移性，但缺乏对稀疏无线网络场景（如基于RGG的冲突图）下可迁移性的严格理论分析。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 利用稀疏随机几何图（RGG）与确定性网格图（DGG）之间的近似关系，推导GNN在不同规模图之间迁移时的性能损失上界；通过链路调度问题对理论结果进行实验验证。

Result: 理论推导了GNN在RGG派生图上跨尺度迁移的性能损失界；链路调度实验表明所学策略在规模扩展时持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于RGG与DGG的近似关系可以有效地量化GNN在稀疏无线网络中的可迁移性，所提方法在实际链路调度任务中表现出优异的大规模泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as a powerful tool for wireless resource allocation that leverages the underlying graph structure of communication networks. Their transferability property enables models trained on small-scale graphs to generalize to large-scale deployments with little performance deterioration, a desirable property for currently growing networks. Wireless networks are sparse regimes, where a single node is connected to a small number of other users. This work establishes theoretical results for transferability of GNNs over graphs derived from sparse Random Geometric Graphs (RGGs). In particular, we focus on conflict graphs of RGGs used to model interference among links. Our approach considers the closeness between RGGs and Deterministic Grid Graphs (DGG) to establish bounds in the performance loss when a model is transferred across scales. We validate our theoretical findings through the problem of link scheduling, demonstrating that our learned policies consistently outperform existing benchmarks at scale. Finally, we examine the impact of our theoretical assumptions on empirical performance.

中文摘要翻译: 图神经网络（GNN）已成为一种利用通信网络底层图结构进行无线资源分配的强大工具。其可迁移性使得在小规模图上训练的模型能够泛化到大规模部署中，且性能下降很小，这对于当前不断增长的网络来说是一个理想的特性。无线网络属于稀疏场景，其中单个节点仅与少量其他用户相连。本文针对基于稀疏随机几何图（RGG）的图上的GNN可迁移性建立了理论结果。具体地，我们聚焦于用于建模链路间干扰的RGG冲突图。我们的方法考虑了RGG与确定性网格图（DGG）之间的近似性，从而建立了模型跨尺度迁移时性能损失的上界。我们通过链路调度问题验证了理论发现，证明所学策略在规模扩展时持续优于现有基准方法。最后，我们考察了理论假设对经验性能的影响。

</details>


### [155] [Geometry-Aware Tabular Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02607)
*David Turtora Zagardo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出几何感知表格扩散（GATD），通过显式监督列间角度和长度关系来增强表格数据扩散模型，在更少参数下达到SOTA，证明了关系监督是可迁移的归纳偏置。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散模型依赖隐式机制捕捉表格数据的列间关系，缺乏显式的关系建模，导致生成质量受限。

Method: 在扩散去噪器中引入成对角度和长度作为额外输入和辅助目标，这些几何特征由列值差异计算得到；在MLP、GNN和Transformer三种去噪器架构上验证。

Result: MLP实例化平均减少3.5倍参数量（分类任务最多25倍），在10个数据集上Shape指标8/10、Trend指标7/10、下游效用9/10取得最优，Shape和Trend误差分别降低27%和20%；默认损失权重可迁移到GNN和Transformer，在27/30的架构-数据集单元上提升Shape，25/30提升Trend；消融实验表明收益来自监督信号而非额外输入或容量。

Conclusion: 显式关系监督是一种可迁移的归纳偏置，能够有效提升表格扩散模型的性能。

Original Abstract: Tabular synthesis is critical for privacy-preserving sharing and augmentation, yet diffusion models rely on implicit mechanisms to capture inter-column relationships. We introduce Geometry-Aware Tabular Diffusion (GATD), which augments tabular diffusion denoisers with pairwise angles and lengths computed from column value differences and used as inputs and auxiliary targets. Our MLP instantiation achieves state-of-the-art benchmark performance while using 3.5x fewer parameters on average (up to 25x for classification tasks): on ten datasets, it wins 8/10 Shape, 7/10 Trend, and 9/10 downstream utility (F1/RMSE), reducing Shape and Trend error by 27% and 20%. Default loss weights transfer to GNN and Transformer denoisers, improving Shape on 27/30 and Trend on 25/30 architecture-dataset cells. A matched ablation shows supervision (not extra inputs or capacity) drives the gain. This shows explicit relational supervision is a portable inductive bias for tabular diffusion.

中文摘要翻译: 表格数据合成对于隐私保护的共享和数据增强至关重要，然而扩散模型依赖隐式机制来捕捉列间关系。我们提出了几何感知表格扩散（GATD），该方法通过从列值差异计算得到的成对角度和长度来增强表格扩散去噪器，并将其作为输入和辅助目标。我们的MLP实例化在使用平均3.5倍更少参数（分类任务最多25倍）的情况下实现了最先进的基准性能：在十个数据集上，它在8/10的Shape指标、7/10的Trend指标和9/10的下游效用指标（F1/RMSE）上获胜，将Shape和Trend误差分别降低了27%和20%。默认损失权重可迁移到GNN和Transformer去噪器，在27/30的架构-数据集单元上改善了Shape，在25/30上改善了Trend。匹配的消融实验表明，收益来自监督信号而非额外输入或容量。这表明显式关系监督是一种用于表格扩散的可迁移归纳偏置。

</details>


### [156] [Pruning Deep Neural Networks via the Marchenko--Pastur Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02608)
*Leonid Berlyand,Theo Bourdais,Houman Owhad,Yitzchak Shmalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Marchenko–Pastur随机矩阵理论的深度神经网络剪枝方法，在极短的微调预算下仍能保持模型精度，并提供了确定性的数据路径理论保证。理论分析给出了剪枝对弹性网络目标函数的影响、零预算下的完美剪枝、权重恢复建模以及L2正则化下随机分量消失的条件。在ImageNet-1k上，ViT-B/16 2:4+ToMe达到83.41% top-1精度，稀疏执行MAC降低59.81%，A40后端加速1.388倍；结构化稀疏下ViT-L/16 8:16达85.33%（-0.51 pp），ConvNeXtV2-Base 12:16达86.35%（-0.37 pp）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在极小的后剪枝微调预算下实现深度神经网络的高效剪枝，避免传统方法需要长时间重新优化的缺陷，同时提供理论上的确定性保证。

Method: 利用Marchenko–Pastur随机矩阵理论对剪枝过程建模：给出移除分量对logit影响的确定性上界（L_s ‖R ψ_1(s)‖_∞），证明当该扰动较小时剪枝降低弹性网络目标函数且保留裕度大于两倍扰动的样本；提出零预算完美剪枝、修剪-恢复扩展（固定稀疏模式内的权重恢复）以及加性L2正则化模型（分析随机分量消失与持久尖峰稳定条件）；基于iid高斯充分条件，使用拟合的MP边缘σ_+作为高概率的逐层预算信号。

Result: ImageNet-1k上：ViT-B/16 2:4+ToMe经3轮蒸馏达83.41%（-1.70 pp），MAC降低59.81%，A40原生2:4后端加速1.388倍（无ToMe的A100端点达2.705倍）；结构化稀疏ViT-B/16 6:12达83.74%，ViT-L/16 8:16密集+置换达85.33%（-0.51 pp），ConvNeXtV2-Base 12:16达86.35%（-0.37 pp）；CNN方面ResNet50 8:16密集+置换达75.87%（-0.26 pp），ResNet152d CAST-conv+置换达81.33%（-1.53 pp），MAC降低约50%，A40 im2col+2:4稀疏GEMM审计加速1.62倍。

Conclusion: 基于Marchenko–Pastur随机矩阵理论的剪枝方法能够在极小的微调预算下有效保持模型精度，理论框架提供了确定性的剪枝保证，实验验证了该方法在多种视觉架构（ViT、ConvNeXtV2、ResNet）和稀疏模式下的有效性和实际加速效果。

Original Abstract: We study a Marchenko--Pastur (MP) random-matrix approach to pruning deep neural networks with very small post-pruning fine-tuning budgets. The main practical contribution is accuracy retention under short calibration and fine-tuning schedules, rather than a long post-pruning reoptimization pipeline. The theory gives deterministic data-path certificates: if the removed component $R$ has small propagated logit effect $L_s \| R ψ_1(s) \|_\infty$, pruning decreases an elastic-net objective and preserves samples whose dense margin exceeds twice the perturbation. The zero-budget case gives perfect pruning; a prune--restore extension models weight restoration inside a fixed sparse-execution pattern; and an additive $L_2$-regularized model shows admissible random-like components vanish at the training limit, with persistent spikes stabilizing as the MP bulk collapses. Under iid-Gaussian sufficient conditions, the fitted MP edge $σ_+$ gives a high-probability layerwise budget signal.
  On ImageNet-1k, after only three distillation epochs, ViT-B/16 $2{:}4{+}$ToMe reaches $83.41\%$ top-1 ($-1.70$ pp from dense) at $59.81\%$ sparse-execution MAC reduction, with $1.388\times$ best-observed A40 native-$2{:}4$ backend speedup for the same checkpoint and ToMe graph; a separate no-ToMe A100 endpoint gives $2.705\times$. At structured sparsity, ViT-B/16 $6{:}12$ reaches $83.74\%$, ViT-L/16 $8{:}16$ dense+permutation reaches $85.33\%$ ($-0.51$ pp), and ConvNeXtV2-Base $12{:}16$ reaches $86.35\%$ ($-0.37$ pp). For CNNs, ResNet50 $8{:}16$ dense+permutation reaches $75.87\%$ ($-0.26$ pp), and ResNet152d CAST-conv+permutation reaches $81.33\%$ ($-1.53$ pp) at ${\sim}50\%$ MAC accounting with a $1.62\times$ A40 im2col$+2{:}4$ sparse-GEMM audit.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了一种基于Marchenko–Pastur（MP）随机矩阵方法，用于在极小的后剪枝微调预算下对深度神经网络进行剪枝。主要的实践贡献在于，在短标定和微调计划下保持精度，而非采用漫长的后剪枝重新优化流程。该理论提供了确定性的数据路径保证：若移除分量R的传播logit效应L_s ‖R ψ_1(s)‖_∞很小，则剪枝会降低弹性网络目标函数，并保留那些密集裕度超过两倍扰动的样本。零预算情形实现了完美剪枝；修剪-恢复扩展在固定的稀疏执行模式内对权重恢复进行建模；加性L2正则化模型表明，可容许的随机类分量在训练极限处消失，而持久尖峰随着MP体坍塌而稳定。在iid高斯充分条件下，拟合的MP边缘σ_+提供了一个高概率的逐层预算信号。在ImageNet-1k上，仅经过三个蒸馏轮次后，ViT-B/16 2:4+ToMe在59.81%的稀疏执行MAC缩减下达到了83.41%的top-1准确率（相较于密集模型下降1.70个百分点），在同一检查点和ToMe图下实现了1.388倍的最优A40原生2:4后端加速；一个独立的无ToMe的A100端点达到了2.705倍加速。在结构化稀疏方面，ViT-B/16 6:12达到了83.74%，ViT-L/16 8:16密集+置换达到了85.33%（下降0.51个百分点），ConvNeXtV2-Base 12:16达到了86.35%（下降0.37个百分点）。对于CNN，ResNet50 8:16密集+置换达到了75.87%（下降0.26个百分点），ResNet152d CAST-conv+置换在约50%的MAC占比下达到了81.33%（下降1.53个百分点），A40 im2col+2:4稀疏GEMM审计实现了1.62倍加速。

</details>


### [157] [Building Better Activation Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02609)
*Jan Bauer,Celeste De Schamphelaere,Adam Karvonen,Niclas Luick,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对激活预言机（AO）存在的幻觉、模糊性及文本混淆评估难题，通过四项训练改进（在线策略rollout训练、对话数据集优化、增加输入层数、改进注入公式）来提升AO质量，并开源了首个综合评估套件AObench，为可扩展端到端可解释性奠定基础。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的激活预言机（AO）存在幻觉（hallucinations）和模糊性（vagueness）等严重问题，同时文本反转混淆（text-inversion confounds）使其难以被有效评估，亟需改进训练方法和建立标准化评估体系。

Method: 在四个方面改进激活预言机训练流程：(1) 采用在线策略rollout（on-policy rollouts）进行训练；(2) 优化对话数据集的质量和多样性；(3) 向模型馈送更多层的残差流激活值；(4) 改进激活注入公式。

Result: 能力提升（capability improvements）幅度较小，但生活质量方面的改进（quality of life improvements）非常显著。同时开源了首个全面的AO质量评估套件AObench。

Conclusion: 本研究为改进激活预言机及其他遵循可扩展端到端可解释性范式的模型奠定了基础，有助于推动该方向的进一步发展。

Original Abstract: Activation Oracles (AOs) are promising methods for interpreting residual stream activations. However, current AOs face important issues, such as hallucinations and vagueness. Additionally, text-inversion confounds make them hard to evaluate. To this end, we improve the Activation Oracle (AO) training regime in four ways: training on on-policy rollouts, improving the conversational dataset, feeding more layers and an improvement to the injection formula. The capability improvements are marginal, but quality of life improvements are quite substantial. In addition, we open source the first comprehensive evaluation suite for AO quality, which we call AObench. Overall, we hope that our work sets a foundation that helps improve AOs and other models in the paradigm of scalable, end-to-end interpretability.

中文摘要翻译: 激活预言机（Activation Oracles, AOs）是解释残差流激活的有前景的方法。然而，当前的AOs面临重要问题，例如幻觉和模糊性。此外，文本反转混淆使得它们难以被评估。为此，我们从四个方面改进了激活预言机（AO）的训练流程：采用在线策略rollout进行训练、改进对话数据集、馈送更多残差流层以及改进注入公式。能力提升幅度虽有限，但生活质量方面的改进非常显著。此外，我们开源了首个全面的AO质量评估套件，称为AObench。总体而言，我们希望本研究能为改进AOs及其他遵循可扩展端到端可解释性范式的模型奠定基础。

</details>


### [158] [Hallucination Is Linearly Decodable from Mid-Layer Hidden States in Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02628)
*Aizierjiang Aiersilan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究开源大语言模型（LLM）的隐藏状态中是否编码了线性可分的真实性信号，以及该信号在哪个网络深度最强。实验发现，在中间层的线性探测能达到0.904-1.000 AUROC，显著优于基于采样的检测方法（≤0.541 AUROC）。真实性信号近似线性，最优探测层在不同模型家族的自然语言基准上呈现一致区间。首层注意力熵在知识接地场景下可提供互补信号（0.866-0.941 AUROC），且无额外推理成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究对LLM真实性信号的编码位置和线性可分性缺乏系统理解，需要明确不同探测方法在检测模型是否生成真实内容时的有效性，以及该信号在隐藏状态中的分布规律。

Method: 在三个7B-8B指令微调模型（Llama-3.1-8B、Mistral-7B、Qwen2.5-7B）上，使用4-bit NF4量化加载，提取四个幻觉基准数据集（TruthfulQA、HaluEval-QA、FEVER及合成数据集）中每个Transformer层的隐藏状态，比较四种检测方法：线性探针、MLP探针、INSIDE EigenScore、自一致性以及注意力熵。
