Result: 1）中间层线性探针在留出集上达到0.904-1.000 AUROC；2）基于采样的检测器在同一协议下AUROC不超过0.541；3）MLP探针相比线性探针AUROC提升不超过0.01，表明信号近似线性；4）最优探测层在自然语言基准上呈一致区间（Llama/Mistral: 13-18/32层，Qwen: 19-25/28层）；5）首层注意力熵在HaluEval-QA上达到0.866-0.941 AUROC。

Conclusion: 开源LLM的隐藏状态中存在线性可分的真实性信号，且集中于中间层。基于采样的方法在配对标签评估协议下表现不佳源于结构性失配而非方法本身缺陷。首层注意力熵可作为低成本互补信号。

Original Abstract: We investigate whether open-source LLMs encode a linearly separable truthfulness signal in their hidden states, and at which network depth this signal is strongest. Across three $7$B--$8$B instruction-tuned models (Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, Qwen2.5-7B) loaded in $4$-bit NF4 quantization, we extract per-layer hidden states on four hallucination benchmarks (TruthfulQA, HaluEval-QA, FEVER, and a controlled synthetic set) and compare four detection approaches: linear and MLP probes, INSIDE EigenScore, self-consistency, and attention entropy. A linear probe on a single mid-network layer achieves $0.904$--$1.000$ AUROC on held-out splits, while sampling-based detectors do not exceed $0.541$ AUROC under the same protocol. The truthfulness signal is approximately linear: MLP probes rarely surpass linear probes by more than $0.01$ AUROC. Peak probing layers fall in a consistent band across model families on natural-language benchmarks -- blocks~$13$--$18$ of~$32$ for Llama and Mistral, and blocks~$19$--$25$ of~$28$ for Qwen. First-block attention entropy provides a complementary signal in knowledge-grounded settings ($0.866$--$0.941$ AUROC on HaluEval-QA) at no additional inference cost. The low discriminability of sampling methods under this protocol reflects a structural mismatch between paired-label evaluation and the information these methods access, rather than an inherent limitation of those methods. Code and data are released for full reproducibility on a single $8$\,GB GPU.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了开源大语言模型（LLM）的隐藏状态中是否编码了线性可分的真实性信号，以及该信号在哪个网络深度最强。在三个以4-bit NF4量化加载的7B-8B指令微调模型（Llama-3.1-8B、Mistral-7B、Qwen2.5-7B）上，我们提取了四个幻觉基准数据集（TruthfulQA、HaluEval-QA、FEVER及一个受控合成数据集）中每个Transformer层的隐藏状态，并比较了四种检测方法：线性探针、MLP探针、INSIDE EigenScore、自一致性以及注意力熵。在留出集上，单个中间网络层的线性探针达到了0.904-1.000 AUROC，而基于采样的检测器在同一协议下AUROC不超过0.541。真实性信号是近似线性的：MLP探针相比线性探针的AUROC提升极少超过0.01。在自然语言基准上，最优探测层落在模型家族间一致的区间内——Llama和Mistral的32层中第13-18层，Qwen的28层中第19-25层。在知识接地场景下，首层注意力熵以零额外推理成本提供了互补信号（在HaluEval-QA上达到0.866-0.941 AUROC）。该协议下采样方法判别能力低反映了配对标签评估与这些方法获取信息之间的结构性失配，而非这些方法本身的固有限制。代码和数据已发布，可在单张8GB GPU上完全复现。

</details>


### [159] [Regime-Arrival Uncertainty in Generalization Bounds under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02657)
*Prince Poudel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，量化在马尔可夫切换环境下，由于体制组成不匹配导致的额外泛化风险，并证明该风险无法通过训练集单独预测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统泛化界假设训练与部署分布相同或静态，无法处理平静与危机状态比例不同的体制切换环境，需要量化这种不匹配带来的额外风险。

Method: 通过精确分解将体制不匹配与体制敏感性分离；将界限扩展到β混合数据，使用经谱间隙校正的有效样本量；并提出一个事后实现的泛化差距惩罚项。

Result: 在合成数据和25年全球股票指数上给出了极小极大下界；训练集单独估计器与泛化差距无显著相关性，表明可检测危机特征几何但无法预测其时间到来。

Conclusion: 该框架不是预测机器，未来体制组成的预测在体制变化的罕见情况下仍是一个开放问题。

Original Abstract: The standard generalization bounds assume that the training and deployment distributions are the same, or are static, and don't consider regime switching environments where the ratio of calm vs crisis states is different. This paper proposes a framework that generalizes regime-aware models by quantifying the extra risk due to regime composition mismatch, when distribution shifts are Markov-switching. We obtain an exact decomposition, separating regime mismatch from regime sensitivity; we extend the bound to beta-mixing data using the effective sample size corrected for the spectral gap; and we show a minimax lower bound for synthetic data and on 25 years of global equity indices. The proposed penalty is an ex post realized generalization gap, whereas the training-only estimator does not show significant correlation: the feature geometry of crises can be detected, but not the temporal arrival. Thus, the framework is not a forecast machine. Forecasting the composition of the future regime is an open question in the rare cases of regime change.

中文摘要翻译: 标准的泛化界假设训练分布和部署分布相同或静态，不考虑平静状态与危机状态比例不同的体制切换环境。本文提出一个框架，通过量化在分布转移为马尔可夫切换时，因体制组成不匹配而产生的额外风险，来推广体制感知模型。我们获得了精确的分解，将体制不匹配与体制敏感性分离；将界限扩展到β混合数据，使用经谱间隙校正的有效样本量；并在合成数据及25年全球股票指数上展示了极小极大下界。所提出的惩罚项是一种事后实现的泛化差距，而仅基于训练集的估计器未显示出显著相关性：危机特征几何可以被检测到，但其时间到来却无法预测。因此，该框架并非预测机器。在未来体制组成的预测方面，在体制变化的罕见情况下仍是一个开放问题。

</details>


### [160] [CL-DMDF:Dynamic Multimodal Data Fusion Model Based on Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02659)
*Dong Li,Lingling Zhang,Binghao Han,Linlin Ding,Yue Kou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于对比学习的动态多模态数据融合模型（CL-DMDF），通过跨特征和模态维度的注意力机制、实体质心对比学习以及自适应融合模块，有效应对现实场景中的模态缺失问题，在多个数据集上验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法通常针对特定任务设计且假设模态完整可用，但现实中常出现模态缺失或不确定的情况；传统模型过度关注缺失模态内的局部交互，忽略了多模态表示中蕴含的全局互补信息。

Method: CL-DMDF包含三个核心模块：(1) 跨特征维度和模态维度的新型注意力机制，计算可靠注意力分数以反映各层级重要性；(2) 实体质心对比学习模块，基于实体特征构建质心正样本以增强判别学习；(3) 自适应融合模块，提升动态融合策略的效率与准确性。

Result: 在三个数据集上的大量实验表明，CL-DMDF在多样化的多模态融合任务中均取得了有效表现。

Conclusion: 提出的CL-DMDF模型通过动态多模态数据融合与对比学习，有效解决了模态缺失场景下的全局信息捕获问题，提升了融合的鲁棒性和判别性能。

Original Abstract: Multimodal data fusion involves integrating and analyzing information from multiple modalities to uncover latent correlations and complementary patterns, thereby enhancing data processing and decision-making. While existing methods for structured multimodal inputs are typically designed around specific tasks and assume fully observed modalities, real-world applications often suffer from uncertain or missing modality inputs due to various factors. Some traditional models overly emphasize local interactions within missing modalities, neglecting the global complementary cues embedded in multimodal representations. To overcome these limitations, we propose a Dynamic Multimodal Data Fusion model based on Contrastive Learning (CL-DMDF). CL-DMDF introduces a novel attention mechanism that operates across both feature and modality dimensions to compute reliable attention scores, effectively reflecting importance at each level. The CL-DMDF further incorporates an entity-centroid contrastive learning module that constructs centroid-based positive samples from entity features to enhance discriminative learning. Additionally, an adaptive fusion module is employed to improve the efficiency and accuracy of dynamic fusion strategies. Extensive experiments conducted on three datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the CL-DMDF across diverse multimodal fusion tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态数据融合涉及对来自多种模态的信息进行整合与分析，以揭示潜在的关联和互补模式，从而增强数据处理与决策能力。现有针对结构化多模态输入的方法通常围绕特定任务设计，并假设模态完全可观测，然而现实应用常因多种因素导致模态输入不确定或缺失。部分传统模型过度关注缺失模态内的局部交互，忽略了多模态表示中蕴含的全局互补线索。为克服上述局限，我们提出了一种基于对比学习的动态多模态数据融合模型（CL-DMDF）。CL-DMDF引入了一种新颖的注意力机制，该机制同时在特征维度和模态维度上运算，以计算可靠的注意力分数，有效反映每个层级的重要性。CL-DMDF进一步集成了实体质心对比学习模块，通过从实体特征构建基于质心的正样本来增强判别学习。此外，采用自适应融合模块以提升动态融合策略的效率与准确性。在三个数据集上开展的大量实验证明了CL-DMDF在多样化多模态融合任务中的有效性。

</details>


### [161] [Improvise, Adapt, Overcome: An On-The-Fly Multifidelity Algorithm for Efficient Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02662)
*Vivin Vinod,Peter Zaspel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应多保真度机器学习框架（adaptive-MFML），该框架能够动态地按需确定训练数据集的组成，通过先饱和低保真度模型精度再升级到高保真度计算，显著降低了量子化学中高保真度训练数据的生成成本，相比单保真度方法最高可降低30倍成本，相比标准MFML最高可提升5倍效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 量子化学中机器学习方法虽已取得进展，但生成高保真度训练数据成本高昂，成为主要瓶颈。标准多保真度机器学习（MFML）方法依赖预定义的缩放因子来确定不同保真度间稀疏数据比例，往往产生冗余的多保真度数据，导致效率损失。

Method: 提出一种自适应在线多保真度机器学习框架（adaptive-MFML），该算法能够自主确定训练数据集的组成。具体通过在每个保真度上动态查询训练样本，先在低保真度上饱和模型精度，再升级到更昂贵的参考计算，从而避免数据冗余。

Result: 在耦合簇能量（计算化学金标准）和更具化学挑战性的激发能等不同化学性质上的实验表明，自适应算法相比单保真度方法将数据生成成本降低高达30倍，相比标准MFML提升高达5倍。

Conclusion: 该自适应方法有效缓解了数据冗余问题，为量子化学中可持续的、成本感知的机器学习建立了一条高精度、低成本的路径。

Original Abstract: Machine learning has accelerated quantum chemistry but is hindered by the prohibitive cost of generating high fidelity training data. Multifidelity machine learning (MFML) mitigates this overhead by systematically combining abundant low fidelity data with sparse high fidelity data. In spite of its success, standard MFML schemes rely on pre-defined scaling factors to determine sparse data ratio across fidelities, often generating redundant multifidelity data resulting in a loss of efficiency. Here, we introduce an adaptive on-the-fly multifidelity framework for machine learning that autonomously determines training dataset composition. By dynamically querying training samples at each fidelity, the algorithm saturates model accuracy at lower fidelities before moving up to more expensive reference calculations. We benchmark the novel adaptive-MFML across diverse chemical properties including the computational chemistry gold standard coupled cluster energies, and the more chemically challenging excitation energies. In our numerical experiments we show that our adaptive algorithm reduces data generation costs by up to a factor of 30 compared to single fidelity methods and improves upon standard MFML by up to a factor of 5. The mitigation of data redundancy establishes a high-accuracy low-cost pathway for sustainable cost-aware machine learning in quantum chemistry.

中文摘要翻译: 机器学习加速了量子化学发展，但生成高保真度训练数据的过高成本阻碍了其进展。多保真度机器学习（MFML）通过系统性地将丰富的低保真度数据与稀疏的高保真度数据相结合，降低了这一开销。尽管取得了成功，标准MFML方案依赖预定义的缩放因子来确定不同保真度间的稀疏数据比例，常常产生冗余的多保真度数据，导致效率损失。本文提出了一种自适应的在线多保真度机器学习框架，能够自主确定训练数据集的组成。通过在每个保真度上动态查询训练样本，该算法先在低保真度上饱和模型精度，再升级到更昂贵的参考计算。我们针对不同的化学性质（包括计算化学金标准的耦合簇能量和更具化学挑战性的激发能）对新型adaptive-MFML进行了基准测试。数值实验表明，我们的自适应算法相比单保真度方法将数据生成成本降低高达30倍，相比标准MFML提升高达5倍。缓解数据冗余为量子化学中可持续的、成本感知的机器学习建立了一条高精度、低成本的路径。

</details>


### [162] [AdaWeather: Adaptively Mixing Probabilistic Weather Forecasts with Logarithmic Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02663)
*Saptarishi Dhanuka,Sarvesh Iyer,Manmeet Singh,Mihir More,Rushil Gupta,Dhruman Gupta,Parthasarathi Mukhopadhyay,Sandeep Juneja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaWeather是一个自适应框架，通过结合机器学习和专家混合方法融合多个概率天气预报，并实现了对数级别的遗憾界，相比最佳静态专家混合在事后表现更优。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有概率天气预报模型中，没有一个模型能在时空上持续占优，相对性能高度依赖上下文，因此需要自适应方法来组合多个预报以提升效果和鲁棒性。现有组合方法要么依赖监督学习，要么依赖预测与专家建议方法，存在局限性。

Method: 提出AdaWeather自适应框架，结合机器学习和专家混合方法融合多个概率天气预报。将传统专家方法的遗憾界分析从与事后最佳单一专家比较扩展到与事后最佳静态专家混合比较，证明了算法具有对数级别的遗憾界。

Result: 在温度预测任务上进行实验，观察到AdaWeather相比现有方法取得了改进。

Conclusion: AdaWeather通过自适应融合多个概率天气预报，在理论上提供了比最佳静态专家混合更优的遗憾界保障，在实验上取得了优于现有方法的效果。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in machine learning have produced probabilistic weather forecasting models comparable to state-of-the-art numerical weather predictors. But no model consistently dominates spatio-temporally, and relative performance is highly context-dependent. This motivates adaptive methods for combining multiple forecasts to obtain improvements and robustness. While combined forecasts have been proposed in the literature, these are achieved either through supervised learning or through prediction with expert advice methods. We introduce AdaWeather, an adaptive framework that combines many probabilistic forecasts using both machine learning as well as mixture of experts to arrive at a unified improved probabilistic forecast. While traditional expert methods develop the regret bounds with respect to the best single expert in hindsight, we extend the algorithm and analysis to show our method has logarithmic regret compared to the best static mixture of experts in hindsight. Empirically, we focus on forecasting temperature, and observe improvements over existing methods.

中文摘要翻译: 机器学习的最新进展已产生出与最先进的数值天气预报模型相媲美的概率天气预报模型。然而，没有任何一个模型能在时空上持续占据优势，且相对性能高度依赖于具体情境。这激发了对自适应方法的需求，以组合多个预报来获得改进和鲁棒性。尽管文献中已有组合预报的提出，但这些方法要么通过监督学习实现，要么通过带专家建议的预测方法实现。我们提出了AdaWeather，一个自适应框架，它结合机器学习与专家混合方法融合多个概率预报，从而得到统一改进的概率预报。传统专家方法建立的是相对于事后最佳单一专家的遗憾界，而我们将算法和分析进行扩展，证明了我们的方法相对于事后最佳静态专家混合具有对数级别的遗憾界。在实验上，我们专注于温度预测任务，观察到相比现有方法有所改进。

</details>


### [163] [Anomalies in Multivariate Time Series Benchmarks Are Mostly Univariate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02670)
*Marc Pinet,Julien Cumin,Samuel Berlemont,Dominique Vaufreydaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过诊断框架评估了多变量时序异常检测（MT-SAD）中跨通道建模的必要性，发现当前基准测试中所有跨通道异常均伴随单变量偏差，且跨通道建模并未带来可测量的性能提升，结论是现有基准不适合验证跨通道建模能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MT-SAD模型普遍引入跨通道建模，隐含假设异常结构可能分布在多个通道上。然而这一假设是否成立缺乏系统验证，因此作者希望严格评估当前基准测试中跨通道异常的真实存在性及其对模型性能的实际贡献。

Method: 提出一种逐段（per-segment）诊断框架，对每个标注异常段标记三类情况：至少一个通道偏离其正常历史、跨通道相关结构发生变化、或两者兼具。同时构建合成数据（相位偏移正弦通道+共享噪声），通过保留边缘分布但破坏跨通道结构的通道级扰动来验证框架对跨通道结构的捕捉能力。

Result: 在八个基准测试上，所有跨通道异常都伴随单变量偏差；六个数据集上至少一半的异常段在79%-100%的时间步上存在单变量偏差（其中三个达100%）。合成数据上框架正确识别出纯跨通道异常，通道依赖（CD）模型成功利用跨通道信号而通道独立（CI）模型失败，但在真实基准上CD模型无显著增益。

Conclusion: 当前MTSAD基准测试不适合验证跨通道建模能力，需要开发更多样化的评估数据集。

Original Abstract: Many recent multivariate time series anomaly detection (MT-SAD) models incorporate cross-channel modeling, under the implicit assumption that the structure of anomalies may be spread across multiple channels. We evaluate this assumption on eight widely used public benchmarks by introducing a per-segment diagnostic framework that flags, for each labeled anomaly, whether at least one channel deviates individually from its normal history, whether the cross-channel correlation structure changes, or both. The framework shows that no crosschannel rupture occurs without an accompanying univariate deviation across a range of reasonable thresholds. A complementary metric also reveals that on six of the eight benchmarks, at least half of the labeled anomaly segments deviate univariately on 79% to 100% of their timesteps, reaching 100% on three of these datasets. To verify that our framework captures cross-channel structure when present, we construct synthetic data of phase-shifted sinusoidal channels with shared noise. Each anomalous segment is altered through one of two channelwise corruptions that preserve the per-channel marginal distribution while breaking cross-channel structure, and our framework correctly characterizes these segments as cross-channel-only. On these data, channel-dependent (CD) models successfully exploit the cross-channel signal whereas channel-independent (CI) ones fail. The CI/CD comparison of a recent SOTA detector on real benchmarks further confirms that CD modeling brings no measurable gain. We conclude that current MTSAD benchmarks are unsuitable for validating cross-channel modeling capabilities, and we call for the development of more structurally diverse evaluation sets. The code for this study is publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，许多多变量时间序列异常检测（MT-SAD）模型引入了跨通道建模，其隐含假设是异常结构可能分布在多个通道上。我们在八个广泛使用的公共基准上评估了这一假设，提出了一种逐段诊断框架，对每个标注的异常段进行标记：至少一个通道是否独立偏离其正常历史、跨通道相关结构是否发生变化，或两者同时发生。该框架表明，在合理的阈值范围内，没有跨通道破裂是在没有伴随单变量偏差的情况下发生的。一项补充指标还显示，在八个基准中的六个上，至少一半的标注异常段在79%至100%的时间步上存在单变量偏差，其中三个数据集上达到100%。为了验证我们的框架在存在跨通道结构时能够捕获它，我们构建了具有共享噪声的相位偏移正弦通道的合成数据。每个异常段通过两种通道级扰动之一进行改变，这些扰动在保留每个通道边缘分布的同时破坏了跨通道结构，我们的框架正确地将这些段表征为纯跨通道异常。在这些数据上，通道依赖（CD）模型成功利用了跨通道信号，而通道独立（CI）模型则失败。在真实基准上对近期SOTA检测器进行的CI/CD比较进一步证实，CD建模并未带来可测量的增益。我们得出结论，当前MTSAD基准不适合验证跨通道建模能力，并呼吁开发更多样化的评估集。本研究的代码已公开。

</details>


### [164] [Aligning Data-Driven Predictors with Allocation: A Decision-Focused Approach to Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02671)
*Itai Zilberstein,Ioannis Anagnostides,Tuomas Sandholm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有生存预测模型（如基于C-index优化）在器官分配等决策任务中可能产生任意差的分配结果，并提出基于信息检索指标NDCG的决策导向学习框架，通过在历史心脏移植数据上提升NDCG 50-100%，每年可额外获取数万生命年。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有机器学习预测模型通常独立于下游算法任务进行优化（如仅优化C-index等统计指标），导致在器官分配等高风险决策场景中，即使预测准确率很高，也可能产生比随机分配更差的结果，预测与决策之间存在严重失配。

Method: 提出决策导向学习（decision-focused learning）方法，将信息检索领域的主流指标归一化折损累计增益（NDCG）引入生存分析，并通过自举（bootstrapping）方法优化现有生存模型的NDCG，同时处理右删失（right censorship）对排序评估带来的挑战。

Result: 理论上证明了NDCG在生存分析中能够转化为分配性能的保证；在美国历史心脏移植数据上，该方法将基线模型的NDCG提升50-100%，转化为每年额外数万生命年的获益。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于NDCG的决策导向学习框架能够有效弥合生存分析与策略优化之间的鸿沟，在器官分配中显著提升实际效果，并有望推广到更广泛的预测驱动决策场景中。

Original Abstract: Machine learning predictors have become essential tools for guiding automated decision making. However, a major misalignment persists: predictive models are typically optimized in terms of standard statistical metrics in isolation from the algorithmic tasks they inform. We highlight this incongruity in the high-stakes domain of organ allocation by demonstrating that any algorithm relying on (even highly accurate) survival predictors optimized for standard metrics -- such as the Concordance index (C-index) -- can yield arbitrarily poor outcomes when used for allocation, failing to guarantee utility better than a uniform random selection. To bridge the gap between survival analysis and policy optimization, we introduce a decision-focused learning approach based on optimizing normalized discounted cumulative gain (NDCG), a mainstay metric in information retrieval. We establish the utility of NDCG in survival analysis by proving that it translates to guarantees on the performance of allocation. Empirically, we propose a bootstrapping approach to optimize the NDCG of existing survival models. Unlike prior work, we also address the challenge of right censorship when evaluating ranking. On historical heart transplant data from the US, our method dramatically boosts the NDCG of baseline models by 50-100%, which translates to tens of thousands of additional life years gained annually when deployed for transplant allocation. We anticipate that our framework will find broader applications in decision making with predictions.

中文摘要翻译: 机器学习预测器已成为指导自动化决策的重要工具。然而，一个主要的错配问题依然存在：预测模型通常依据标准统计指标进行优化，而与其所服务的算法任务相脱离。我们在器官分配这一高风险领域中指出了这一不协调性，证明任何依赖（甚至高度准确的）生存预测器——这些预测器针对标准指标（如一致性指数C-index）进行优化——的算法，在用于分配时可能产生任意差的结果，无法保证比均匀随机选择更好的效用。为弥合生存分析与策略优化之间的鸿沟，我们引入了一种基于优化归一化折损累计增益（NDCG）的决策导向学习方法，NDCG是信息检索领域的主流指标。我们通过证明NDCG能够转化为分配性能的保证，确立了其在生存分析中的效用。在实验上，我们提出了一种自举方法，用于优化现有生存模型的NDCG。与以往工作不同，我们还解决了在评估排序时右删失带来的挑战。在美国历史心脏移植数据上，我们的方法将基线模型的NDCG大幅提升了50-100%，当部署于移植分配时，这相当于每年额外获得数万生命年。我们预期该框架将在预测驱动的决策中找到更广泛的应用。

</details>


### [165] [Before Fusion, Ask What to Keep: Contextual Calibration of Multimodal Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02679)
*Jiyuan Liu,Liangwei Nathan Zheng,Wei Emma Zhang,Xinpei Wang,Weitong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种即插即用的多模态校准模块，在融合前对各模态特征进行实例级和维度级调制，抑制干扰、增强可靠证据，在五个基准任务上提升了性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态系统中，不同模态的特征可能存在冲突或干扰，简单的特征融合无法有效鉴别哪些模态信息可靠、哪些是干扰，导致性能下降。

Method: 设计一个轻量级校准模块，在特征融合前对各模态进行摘要级对比，提取跨模态支持与冲突线索，转换为实例级和维度级的调制信号，对原始模态特征进行校准，抑制误导性成分并增强可靠证据。该模块为即插即用组件，可适配不同融合骨干网络。

Result: 在情感理解、动作识别、音视频事件检测和音视频情感分类五个基准上，融合前校准策略在序列和卷积两种融合设置下均提升了性能；模态移除、合成损坏、训练动态分析和特征可视化等附加分析表明，该方法能减少不可靠模态的干扰，产生更稳定的多模态优化。

Conclusion: 在融合前对多模态特征进行校准，能够有效减少模态间干扰，提升多模态系统在多种任务上的鲁棒性和性能。

Original Abstract: Multimodal systems often benefit from combining information across language, sound, and visual streams, but this benefit is not guaranteed. A modality that is useful for one input may become distracting for another, and local feature responses within the same modality can disagree with evidence from other sources. This work investigates how to adjust multimodal representations before they are merged by a downstream predictor. We develop a compact calibration module that compares each modality with the others at the summary level, extracts cues of cross-source support and conflict, and converts these cues into instance-wise and dimension-wise modulation signals. The calibration is applied to the original modality features rather than to already fused representations, enabling the model to suppress misleading components, preserve weak but useful evidence, and emphasize responses that are better supported by the current multimodal context. The module is designed as a plug-in component and can be attached to different fusion backbones without changing their prediction heads. Across five benchmarks covering sentiment understanding, action recognition, audio-visual event detection, and audio-visual emotion classification, the proposed pre-combination calibration strategy improves performance under both sequence-based and convolutional fusion settings. Additional analyses under modality removal, synthetic corruption, training dynamics, and feature-level visualization show that calibrating signals before fusion can reduce interference from unreliable modalities and produce more stable multimodal optimization.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态系统通常受益于跨语言、声音和视觉流的信息整合，但这种收益并非必然。对一个输入有用的模态可能对另一个输入产生干扰，同一模态内的局部特征响应也可能与其他来源的证据不一致。本文研究如何在多模态表示被下游预测器合并之前对其进行调整。我们开发了一个紧凑的校准模块，该模块在摘要级别上对各模态进行相互比较，提取跨源支持和冲突的线索，并将这些线索转化为实例级和维度级的调制信号。校准应用于原始模态特征而非已融合的表示，从而使模型能够抑制误导性成分、保留微弱但有用的证据，并强调在当前多模态上下文中得到更好支持的响应。该模块设计为即插即用组件，可附加到不同的融合骨干网络上，而无需改变其预测头部。在涵盖情感理解、动作识别、音视频事件检测和音视频情感分类的五个基准测试中，所提出的融合前校准策略在序列和卷积两种融合设置下均提升了性能。模态移除、合成损坏、训练动态和特征级可视化等附加分析表明，在融合前校准信号可以减少来自不可靠模态的干扰，并产生更稳定的多模态优化。

</details>


### [166] [Locality Does Not Imply Reachability: Boundary Repair in Block-Sparse Causal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02680)
*Zhibo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了块稀疏因果注意力中序列局部性与注意力图可达性之间的失配问题，通过结构依赖集形式化边界伪影，提出相位条件覆盖函数预测稀疏模式失效位置，并设计边界桥接注意力作为零额外参数的修复方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有稀疏因果注意力通常基于序列局部性假设（近处易访问、远处可丢弃），但论文发现固定块因果注意力中相邻token可能在每一深度都被注意力图断开，这种边界伪影导致架构级的性能上限，需要理论诊断和修复。

Method: (1) 用结构依赖集形式化固定块因果掩码下的可达性边界伪影；(2) 推导相位条件覆盖函数，描述可达性依赖于源-目标距离和目标在块内偏移；(3) 提出边界桥接注意力（Boundary Bridge Attention），在保持固定块路径的同时，用共享投影在块边界添加零额外参数的辅助因果边。

Result: (1) 理论证明固定块因果注意力的top-1准确率上界为1/K，期望交叉熵下界为log K；(2) 覆盖函数预测了稀疏模式何时失效、修复何时有效，并解释滑动窗口注意力和边界修复不可互换；(3) 1024 token实验显示增益集中在覆盖对齐的诊断任务上；(4) 8K token Qwen2.5-7B固定检查点探测验证了相同的覆盖不可比性模式。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一个理论指导的诊断框架，用于块稀疏因果注意力中的局部性-可达性失配问题，包含相位条件覆盖分析和最小构造性修复方案。

Original Abstract: Sparse causal attention is usually described by sequence locality: nearby tokens should remain easy to access, while distant tokens may be dropped to reduce cost. This paper studies a mismatch between sequence locality and attention-graph reachability. In fixed block causal attention, two adjacent tokens can be disconnected in the attention graph at every depth. We formalize this boundary artifact through structural dependency sets: if every attention layer uses the same fixed block causal mask and all remaining operations are positionwise, a target representation can depend only on tokens in its own block prefix. This yields an architecture-level boundary-copy separation for a constructed K-way boundary-copy distribution, with top-1 accuracy upper bound 1/K and expected cross-entropy lower bound log K. We then derive phase-conditioned coverage functions showing that reachability depends on both source-target distance and the target's offset within its block. These coverage laws predict when a sparse pattern should fail, when a repair can help, and why sliding-window attention and boundary repair are not interchangeable. Boundary Bridge Attention is treated as a constructive witness: it preserves the fixed block path and adds zero-additional-parameter auxiliary causal edges near block boundaries using shared projections. Controlled 1024-token experiments show that gains concentrate in coverage-aligned diagnostics. As secondary external-validity evidence, a fixed-checkpoint 8K-token Qwen2.5-7B probe shows the same coverage-incomparability pattern. The contribution is a theory-guided diagnostic framework for locality-reachability mismatch in block-sparse causal attention, together with phase-conditioned coverage analysis and a minimal constructive repair.

中文摘要翻译: 稀疏因果注意力通常通过序列局部性来描述：邻近的token应保持易于访问，而 distant 的 token 可以被丢弃以降低计算成本。本文研究了序列局部性与注意力图可达性之间的失配问题。在固定块因果注意力中，两个相邻的 token 可能在每一深度都被注意力图断开。我们通过结构依赖集形式化了这种边界伪影：如果每个注意力层使用相同的固定块因果掩码且所有其余操作都是位置级别的，那么目标表示只能依赖于其所在块前缀内的 token。这导致对于构造的 K 路边界-拷贝分布，存在架构级的边界-拷贝分离，其 top-1 准确率上界为 1/K，期望交叉熵下界为 log K。我们进一步推导了相位条件覆盖函数，表明可达性同时依赖于源-目标距离和目标在其块内的偏移。这些覆盖律预测了稀疏模式何时失效、修复何时有效，以及为何滑动窗口注意力与边界修复不可互换。边界桥接注意力被作为一种构造性证明：它保留固定块路径，并使用共享投影在块边界附近添加零额外参数的辅助因果边。受控的 1024 token 实验表明，增益集中在覆盖对齐的诊断任务上。作为次要的外部有效性证据，在固定检查点的 8K token Qwen2.5-7B 探针上观察到相同的覆盖不可比性模式。本文的贡献是一个理论指导的诊断框架，用于块稀疏因果注意力中的局部性-可达性失配问题，同时包含相位条件覆盖分析和最小构造性修复。

</details>


### [167] [Filter, Then Reweight: Rethinking Optimization Granularity in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02684)
*Yuying Li,Leqi Zheng,Yongzi Yu,Wenrui Zhou,Xuchang Zhong,Xing Hu,Jing Jin,Huangjie Yuan,Tao Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FiRe-OPD，在轨迹级过滤低质量样本，在token级对信息量大的token进行软重加权，实现更细粒度的在线策略蒸馏优化，在强到弱、单教师、多教师等场景下均取得显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有在线策略蒸馏（OPD）方法虽然从全轨迹KL监督转向选择性训练范式，但大多仅在单一粒度（轨迹级或token级）进行选择，忽略了两个粒度的联合优化，且硬选择方式会导致信息损失和优化不稳定。

Method: 提出FiRe-OPD（Filter, then Reweight），分为两步：1) 轨迹级过滤：剔除低质量的rollout样本；2) token级软重加权：在保留下来的轨迹内部，对信息量大的token赋予更高权重，采用软加权机制而非硬选择，从而保留更多信息并提升优化稳定性。

Result: 在强到弱场景下AIME 2024上提升+6.25，在多教师场景下Miner上提升+18.81，在单教师场景下也优于现有token级OPD方法。

Conclusion: FiRe-OPD通过联合轨迹级过滤和token级软重加权，实现了更细粒度的OPD优化，有效缓解了信息损失并增强了优化稳定性，在多个基准场景下均优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: On-Policy distillation (OPD) in large language models is shifting from full-trace KL supervision toward more selective training paradigms. Recent OPD methods increasingly focus on selecting which trajectories to learn from, which tokens are most informative, and which supervision signals are most reliable. Motivated by this trend, we rethink optimization granularity of OPD and propose \fireicon\ FiRe-OPD (Filter, then Reweight), which jointly adjusts supervision signals at both trajectory and token levels. In details, FiRe-OPD first filters trajectories to remove low-quality rollout samples, and then applies soft reweighting within the retained trajectories to emphasize informative tokens. Compared with hard token selection, FiRe-OPD leverages a soft-weighting mechanism to effectively mitigate information loss and enhance optimization stability, thereby achieving finer-grained OPD optimization. We validate the effectiveness of FiRe-OPD across strong-to-weak, single-teacher, and multi-teacher settings, and demonstrate its superiority over recent token-level OPD methods ( (e.g., +6.25 on AIME 2024 in strong-to-weak, +18.81 on Miner in multi-teacher). Our code is available at https://github.com/YuYingLi0/FiRe-OPD.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型中的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）正从全轨迹KL监督转向更具选择性的训练范式。最近的OPD方法越来越关注选择哪些轨迹进行学习、哪些token信息量最大以及哪些监督信号最可靠。受这一趋势启发，我们重新审视OPD的优化粒度，提出FiRe-OPD（先过滤，再重加权），该方案在轨迹级和token级联合调整监督信号。具体而言，FiRe-OPD首先过滤轨迹以剔除低质量的rollout样本，然后在保留的轨迹内部应用软重加权以强调信息量大的token。与硬token选择相比，FiRe-OPD利用软加权机制有效缓解信息损失并增强优化稳定性，从而实现更细粒度的OPD优化。我们在强到弱、单教师和多教师设置下验证了FiRe-OPD的有效性，并展示了其相较于近期token级OPD方法的优越性（例如，在强到弱场景下AIME 2024提升+6.25，在多教师场景下Miner提升+18.81）。我们的代码已开源至https://github.com/YuYingLi0/FiRe-OPD。

</details>


### [168] [$Ψ$-Bench: Evaluating Persona-Sensitive Influencing in Persuasive Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02754)
*Peixuan Han,Hongyi Du,Jiayu Liu,Yihang Sun,Yutong Liu,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ψ-Bench基准，用于评估大语言模型在真实交互场景中通过对话主动说服用户的能力。实验发现现有模型在说服力上仍有较大提升空间，而获取用户画像平均可提升18.24%的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前个性化语言代理主要被动响应用户偏好，缺乏主动与用户交互并提供建议或引导的能力，因此需要系统性地评估模型在真实交互中的主动个性化能力。

Method: 设计Ψ-Bench基准，包含三个涉及说服的真实世界交互场景，通过对话历史构建显式用户画像赋予模拟客户端个性特征，评估10个前沿大语言模型的说服能力。

Result: 大多数模型能生成连贯合理的论点，但即使是顶级模型在说服力上仍有较大改进空间；提供用户画像信息平均带来18.24%的性能提升，凸显用户特定信息对有效说服的重要性。

Conclusion: 个性化感知影响（persona-sensitive influencing）是评估和开发更主动的个性化LLM代理的一个具有挑战性且实用的方向。

Original Abstract: Personalization is a crucial capability of modern language agents. However, current research primarily positions personalized agents as passive responders to user preferences, limiting their ability to interact with users and provide suggestions or guidance proactively. To systematically evaluate such proactive personalization in realistic interactions, we propose $Ψ$-Bench, a benchmark for assessing LLMs' ability to influence realistic users through conversation. We design three real-world interaction scenarios that involve persuasion in $Ψ$-Bench, and endow simulated clients with personal characteristics through explicit user profiles derived from dialogue histories. We evaluate 10 frontier LLMs on $Ψ$-Bench and find that while most models can produce coherent and reasonable arguments, even state-of-the-art models still leave considerable room for improvement in persuasion. We also find that providing access to client profiles yields an average performance gain of 18.24\%, highlighting the importance of user-specific information for effective persuasion. Overall, our work highlights persona-sensitive influencing as a challenging yet practical direction for evaluating and developing more proactive personalized LLM agents. Codes are available at: https://github.com/Hanpx20/Psi-Bench.

中文摘要翻译: 个性化是现代语言代理的关键能力。然而，当前研究主要将个性化代理定位为用户偏好的被动响应者，限制了其主动与用户交互并提供建议或引导的能力。为了在真实交互中系统性地评估这种主动个性化能力，我们提出Ψ-Bench——一个评估大语言模型通过对话影响真实用户能力的基准。我们在Ψ-Bench中设计了三个涉及说服的真实世界交互场景，并通过从对话历史中提取的显式用户画像赋予模拟客户端个性化特征。我们在Ψ-Bench上评估了10个前沿大语言模型，发现尽管大多数模型能生成连贯合理的论点，即使是当前最先进的模型在说服力上仍有相当大的改进空间。我们还发现，提供用户画像信息平均带来18.24%的性能提升，这凸显了用户特定信息对于有效说服的重要性。总体而言，我们的工作突出了个性化感知影响（persona-sensitive influencing）作为评估和开发更主动的个性化LLM代理的一个具有挑战性且实用的方向。代码已在 https://github.com/Hanpx20/Psi-Bench 开源。

</details>


### [169] [Binary Road Surface Classification Using Machine Learning on Production Vehicle Signals During Cruising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02762)
*Vishal Hariharan,Salar Basiri,Kanwar Bharat Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于机器学习的框架，利用车辆动态信号（轮速、扭矩、加速度等）在巡航工况下实时估计路面摩擦状态（抓地/打滑），解决了传统动力学方法因无法测量微滑移而失效的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基于动力学的路面摩擦估计方法依赖滑移斜率计算，但在车辆巡航（无滑移或微滑移）时，现有生产级传感器无法精确测量微滑移，导致方法失效。因此需要利用机器学习挖掘巡航工况下车辆信号与路面条件之间的关联。

Method: 采用特征工程框架和端到端数据驱动框架两种方法，以滑动窗口方式批量处理轮速、轮扭矩、纵向加速度、转向角和横摆角速度等信号的时间序列，输入机器学习模块进行二分类（抓地/干或湿 vs. 打滑/雪或冰）。

Result: 在公共道路数据上的验证结果表明，即使在巡航工况下，数据驱动方法也能正确识别路面状态，展示了数据驱动摩擦状态估计在轮胎与车辆动力学领域的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的机器学习方法能够有效解决巡航工况下路面摩擦状态估计的难题，为车辆预警与干预控制系统提供可靠的实时输入。

Original Abstract: Knowledge of real-time road slipperiness, or even better, a refined estimate of peak grip potential, is a critical input for vehicle warning and intervention control systems. Typically, friction is estimated through dynamics-based recursive estimators by calculating the slip slope; however, its efficacy is heavily constrained by the vehicle dynamic scenario. When the vehicle is cruising and there is little to no slip, these methods become ineffective due to the inability of present-day production-grade sensors, such as wheel speed sensors, and methods to either measure or accurately estimate micro slip, which is crucial for distinguishing different surfaces. To address this challenge, the correlation between vehicle signals and road surface condition during cruising needs to be uncovered using machine learning. In this paper, a feature-based framework and an end-to-end data-driven framework are used to correlate the statistics of vehicle dynamics behavior with the condition of the road surface and perform binary classification into grip, dry or damp, and slip, snow or ice, conditions. A sliding-window approach is adopted to batch a short buffered window of wheel speeds, wheel torques, longitudinal acceleration, steering angle, and yaw rate, which are fed into a machine learning module for predicting the road state. Validation results on public-road data show scenarios where the data-driven method identifies the road surface correctly even during cruising, showing promise for accurate data-driven friction-related state estimators in the field of tire and vehicle dynamics.

中文摘要翻译: 实时道路滑溜程度的知识，甚至是对峰值抓地潜力的精细估计，是车辆预警和干预控制系统的关键输入。通常，摩擦通过基于动力学的递归估计器计算滑移斜率来估计，但其有效性受到车辆动态场景的严重制约。当车辆巡航且几乎没有滑移时，由于现有生产级传感器（如轮速传感器）和方法无法测量或精确估计微滑移——而这对于区分不同路面至关重要——这些方法变得无效。为解决这一挑战，需要使用机器学习揭示巡航过程中车辆信号与路面条件之间的关联。本文采用基于特征的框架和端到端数据驱动框架，将车辆动力学行为的统计特征与路面条件相关联，进行二分类：抓地（干燥或潮湿）与打滑（雪或冰）。采用滑动窗口方法对短时缓冲窗口内的轮速、轮扭矩、纵向加速度、转向角和横摆角速度进行批量处理，并将其输入机器学习模块以预测路面状态。在公共道路数据上的验证结果表明，即使在巡航工况下，数据驱动方法也能正确识别路面状态，展示了在轮胎与车辆动力学领域中实现精确数据驱动摩擦状态估计的前景。

</details>


### [170] [Representational Capacity: Geometric Limits on Feature Representation in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02765)
*Alexander Guha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer语言模型中模型维度$d_{model}$对特征表示几何极限的影响。基于线性表示和叠加假设，通过嵌入矩阵的余弦相似度分布估计模型对正交性的可接受偏差$\varepsilon$，并推导出调整后的表示容量公式，发现容量对$\varepsilon$呈指数敏感，且大模型倾向于更严格的正交约束而非最大化原始容量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 模型维度$d_{model}$是Transformer语言模型的基本超参数，但其在设置特征表示几何极限方面的作用尚未得到充分探索。作者希望基于线性表示和叠加假设，量化模型在潜在空间中能支持多少近正交方向。

Method: (1) 将嵌入矩阵作为潜在空间中近正交约束的可测量代理，利用成对余弦相似度分布中"有意义的token关系"与"偶然相似性"之间的边界来估计模型可接受的偏离完美正交性的偏差$\varepsilon$。(2) 在数十个开源模型上应用该度量，识别出高$\varepsilon$（缺乏近正交结构）和低$\varepsilon$（保持近正交结构）两类模型。(3) 证明标准Johnson-Lindenstrauss引理严重低估了训练表示的打包效率，推导出调整后的容量公式：近正交方向的数量取决于向量与维度的比率($k/d$)而非原始计数，这一修改无需额外参数即可将预测误差减少两个数量级。

Result: (1) 发现了两种模型类别：高$\varepsilon$模型（嵌入缺乏近正交结构）和低$\varepsilon$模型（保持近正交结构）。(2) 调整后的容量公式（基于$k/d$比率）比标准Johnson-Lindenstrauss引理预测误差降低两个数量级。(3) 表示容量对$\varepsilon$呈指数敏感，大模型倾向于更严格的正交约束而非最大化原始容量。

Conclusion: 定义了表示容量（representational capacity）为模型潜在空间中特征和嵌入可用的可区分方向数量上界。容量对$\varepsilon$呈指数敏感，大模型偏好更严格的正交约束而非最大化原始容量，这一模式可能与稳定性-容量权衡、可用概念上限或模型规模的混杂因素有关，留待未来工作探索。

Original Abstract: Model dimension ($d_{model}$) is a fundamental hyperparameter in transformer language models, yet its role in setting the geometric limits of feature representation remains under-explored. Grounded in the Linear Representation and Superposition Hypotheses - which propose that models encode features as near-orthogonal directions in latent space - we develop a framework for estimating how many such directions a model can support. We first establish the embedding matrix as a measurable proxy for near-orthogonality constraints across the latent space: the boundary between meaningful token relationships and incidental similarity in the pairwise cosine similarity distribution gives a concrete estimate of the model's accepted deviation $\varepsilon$ from perfect orthogonality. Applying this metric across dozens of open-source models reveals two classes: models with high $\varepsilon$ whose embeddings lack near-orthogonal structure, and models with low $\varepsilon$ that maintain it. We then show that the standard Johnson-Lindenstrauss lemma greatly underestimates the packing efficiency of trained representations, and derive an adjusted capacity formula in which the number of near-orthogonal directions depends on the ratio of vectors to dimensions ($k/d$) rather than the raw count - a single modification that cuts prediction error by two orders of magnitude with no extra parameters. Combining these results, we define representational capacity as an upper bound on the number of distinguishable directions available for features and embeddings in a model's latent space. Capacity is exponentially sensitive to $\varepsilon$, and larger models favor tighter orthogonality constraints over maximizing raw capacity - a pattern compatible with several explanations (a stability-capacity trade-off, a ceiling on usable concepts, or confounds with model scale) that we leave to future work.

中文摘要翻译: 模型维度（$d_{model}$）是Transformer语言模型中的一个基础超参数，但其在设定特征表示几何极限方面的作用尚未得到充分探索。基于线性表示和叠加假设——即模型将特征编码为潜在空间中近正交的方向——我们开发了一个用于估计模型能支持多少此类方向的框架。我们首先将嵌入矩阵确立为跨潜在空间近正交约束的可测量代理：成对余弦相似度分布中有意义的token关系与偶然相似性之间的边界，给出了模型对完美正交性的可接受偏差$\varepsilon$的具体估计。将该度量应用于数十个开源模型后，我们发现了两种类别：高$\varepsilon$模型（其嵌入缺乏近正交结构）和低$\varepsilon$模型（保持近正交结构）。然后，我们证明标准Johnson-Lindenstrauss引理严重低估了训练表示的打包效率，并推导出一个调整后的容量公式，其中近正交方向的数量取决于向量与维度的比率（$k/d$）而非原始计数——这一单一修改无需额外参数即可将预测误差减少两个数量级。综合这些结果，我们将表示容量定义为模型潜在空间中特征和嵌入可用的可区分方向上界。容量对$\varepsilon$呈指数敏感，且更大的模型倾向于更严格的正交约束而非最大化原始容量——这一模式与多种解释（稳定性-容量权衡、可用概念上限或模型规模的混杂因素）兼容，我们将其留待未来工作。

</details>


### [171] [QUIVER: Quantum-Informed Views for Enhanced Representations in Large ML Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02785)
*Aritra Bal,Michael Binder,Markus Klute,Benedikt Maier,Michael Spannowsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QUIVER 是一种利用量子 Fisher 信息矩阵（从变分量子电路中提取）作为互补模态，增强经典机器学习模型的多模态学习范式。在 QM9 和 JetClass 两个基准数据集上，该方法显著提升了标准性能指标，表明量子几何特征在容错量子硬件成熟之前即可为经典机器学习任务带来可衡量的价值。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大型机器学习模型受益于多模态输入，但传统的特征增强方法难以捕捉数据中高阶统计结构。量子 Fisher 信息矩阵编码了量子态流形的内在几何性质，能够揭示经典数据或模型容量难以学习的统计结构，从而提供真正互补而非冗余的信息模态。

Method: QUIVER 框架将经典数据驱动特征与"量子 Fisher 视图"相结合。量子 Fisher 视图是从与任务相同目标训练的变分量子电路（VQC）中提取的量子 Fisher 信息矩阵，它是对高阶相关性进行基无关的几何摘要。通过针对性地修改基础模型架构，将这种量子几何信息融合到广泛的模型架构中。

Result: 在两个跨领域的基准数据集上验证了有效性：QM9（分子性质预测）和 JetClass（大型强子对撞机喷注风味识别）。实验结果表明，量子 Fisher 视图作为互补模态能够提升标准性能指标，且这种提升是领域无关的。

Conclusion: 从模拟变分电路中提取的量子几何特征，在容错量子硬件尚未实现之前，即可为经典机器学习任务带来可衡量的性能提升。该方法具有领域无关性，可广泛适用于多种模型架构。

Original Abstract: Large machine learning models benefit substantially from multimodal inputs that provide a complementary view of the same example. We introduce QUIVER (QUantum-Informed Views for Enhanced Representations, a paradigm that enriches classical data-driven features with a quantum Fisher view: a geometrically motivated, basis-independent summary of higher-order correlations captured by a variational quantum circuit (VQC) trained to perform the same task. Unlike classical feature augmentation, the quantum Fisher information matrix encodes the intrinsic geometry of the learned quantum state manifold. While this feature map, motivated by quantum information theory, is ordinarily non-trivial to model classically, it can surface statistical structure that additional classical data or model capacity finds difficult to learn. This makes the quantum Fisher view a genuinely complementary modality rather than a redundant one. We demonstrate that QUIVER improves standard performance metrics on two benchmark datasets from very different fields: QM9 for predicting molecule properties, and JetClass for predicting jet flavor at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC). The core contribution, however, is domain-agnostic: the quantum Fisher view can be fused into a broad class of model architectures via targeted modifications to the base architecture, to incorporate information about the quantum geometry of the problem. These results demonstrate that quantum-geometric features, extracted from simulated variational circuits, can deliver measurable value for standard machine learning tasks, well before the advent of fault-tolerant quantum hardware.

中文摘要翻译: 大型机器学习模型显著受益于能够提供同一示例互补视角的多模态输入。我们提出 QUIVER（量子启发的增强表示视图），这是一种将经典数据驱动特征与量子 Fisher 视图相结合的新范式：量子 Fisher 视图是一种基于几何驱动、基无关的高阶相关性摘要，来源于为执行相同任务而训练的变分量子电路（VQC）。与经典特征增强不同，量子 Fisher 信息矩阵编码了所学量子态流形的内在几何结构。尽管这种受量子信息理论启发的特征映射通常难以经典建模，但它能够揭示额外的经典数据或模型容量难以学习的统计结构。这使得量子 Fisher 视图成为一种真正互补而非冗余的模态。我们证明 QUIVER 在两个来自截然不同领域的基准数据集上提升了标准性能指标：用于预测分子性质的 QM9 数据集，以及用于大型强子对撞机（LHC）喷注风味识别的 JetClass 数据集。然而，其核心贡献是领域无关的：量子 Fisher 视图可以通过对基础架构进行针对性修改，融合到广泛的模型架构中，以融入关于问题量子几何的信息。这些结果表明，从模拟变分电路中提取的量子几何特征，在容错量子硬件到来之前，即可为标准的机器学习任务带来可衡量的价值。

</details>


### [172] [Qift: Shift-Friendly No-Zero W2 Post-Training Quantization for Rotated W2A4/KV4 LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02823)
*Chi-Wei Huang,Chia-Chi Tsai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现W2A4量化失败不仅是位宽问题，更是重构级别（level set）设计问题。基于预训练权重的近似零中心高斯分布特性，提出Qift：一种固定无零的W2级别集（{±0.5, ±1.5}或{±1, ±3}），无需训练、无需学习码本、无需分组和零点，在LLaMA-2-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B上有效缩小了与W3A4的差距。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准W2级别集{-2,-1,0,+1}在激进的W2A4/KV4设置下性能严重下降，说明问题不仅来自位宽限制，也来自重构级别的设计不合理。作者希望探索更优的固定级别集来提升W2A4量化性能。

Method: 提出Qift，一种固定无零的W2级别集：主级别集为{±0.5, ±1.5}（等价于半尺度重参数化后的{±1, ±3}），幂次变体为{±1, ±4}。该方法无需训练、无需学习码本、无需分组网格、无需零点，仅保留标准每通道缩放因子。基于尺度不变比率分析，确定有效内外质心比范围0.25~0.33。

Result: 在LLaMA-2-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B的224个线性模块上，无零级别集在纯W2A4困惑度、L层混合W2/W4困惑度、下游准确率和GPTQ残差行为上均优于标准W2级别集。在L=16混合精度下，显著缩小了与W3A4的差距，同时保持一半Transformer层为2比特精度。

Conclusion: 无零级别集为W2A4/KV4量化提供了一种简单、无需训练、部署友好的替代方案，优于更复杂的基于学习的W2码本方法。

Original Abstract: Two-bit weight quantization is attractive for memory-efficient LLM inference, but the standard W2 level set {-2,-1,0,+1} often collapses under aggressive W2A4/KV4 settings. We study the scalar level-set geometry of two-bit weights in a Hadamard-rotated quantization pipeline. Conventional asymmetric W2 substantially improves over the standard level set, indicating that W2A4 failure is not only a bit-width problem but also a reconstruction-level problem. Across all 224 linear modules in each of LLaMA-2-7B and LLaMA-3.1-8B, pretrained weights are already nearly zero-centered, while Hadamard rotation primarily Gaussianizes their standardized shape: excess kurtosis and Q-Q error drop by orders of magnitude. Based on this approximate zero-centered Gaussian-like source model, we propose Qift, a fixed no-zero W2 level set for rotated W2A4/KV4 inference. The main level set is {+/-0.5, +/-1.5}, equivalently {+/-1, +/-3} under a half-scale reparameterization; a power-of-two variant uses {+/-1, +/-4} for sign-and-shift decoded weight application. Qift redesigns the fixed two-bit code-to-level mapping and is training-free, learned-codebook-free, group-grid-free, and zero-point-free, retaining the standard per-channel scale. A scale-invariant ratio analysis identifies an effective inner/outer centroid ratio range of 0.25 to 0.33, explaining why mirror no-zero (MNZ), Lloyd, NF2, and PoT-MNZ perform well while {+/-1, +/-2} does not. On both models, the no-zero level sets consistently improve pure W2A4 perplexity, L-layer mixed W2/W4 perplexity, downstream accuracy, and GPTQ residual behavior over the standard W2 level set. At L=16 mixed precision, they substantially narrow the gap to W3A4 while keeping half of the transformer layers at two-bit precision, giving a simple, source-aware, and deployment-friendly alternative to more complex learned W2 codebooks.

中文摘要翻译: 两比特权重量化对于内存高效的LLM推理具有吸引力，但标准的W2级别集{-2,-1,0,+1}在激进的W2A4/KV4设置下常常性能崩溃。我们研究了Hadamard旋转量化流水线中两比特权重的标量级别集几何特性。传统的非对称W2显著优于标准级别集，这表明W2A4失败不仅仅是位宽问题，也是一个重构级别（reconstruction level）问题。在LLaMA-2-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B的共224个线性模块中，预训练权重已经近似零中心，而Hadamard旋转主要将其标准化形状高斯化：超额峰度和Q-Q误差下降了数个数量级。基于这种近似零中心的高斯状源模型，我们提出了Qift，一种用于旋转W2A4/KV4推理的固定无零W2级别集。主级别集为{±0.5, ±1.5}，等价于半尺度重参数化下的{±1, ±3}；其幂次变体使用{±1, ±4}用于符号-移位解码的权重应用。Qift重新设计了固定的两比特码到级别的映射，无需训练、无需学习码本、无需分组网格、无需零点，并保留了标准的每通道缩放因子。尺度不变比率分析确定了一个有效的内/外质心比范围0.25到0.33，解释了为什么镜像无零（MNZ）、Lloyd、NF2和PoT-MNZ表现良好，而{±1, ±2}则不然。在两个模型上，无零级别集在纯W2A4困惑度、L层混合W2/W4困惑度、下游准确率和GPTQ残差行为上均一致优于标准W2级别集。在L=16混合精度下，它们显著缩小了与W3A4的差距，同时保持一半的Transformer层为两比特精度，为更复杂的基于学习的W2码本提供了一种简单、基于源模型且部署友好的替代方案。

</details>


### [173] [Mitigating Spurious Correlations with Memorization-Guided Dataset De-Biasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02830)
*Arda Fazla,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段样本评分函数，解耦核心特征与虚假特征的学习动态，从而在无需组标签的情况下筛选信息性样本，有效缓解虚假相关性问题。仅用10%的训练数据即可超越现有去偏方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 真实数据集中常存在与目标标签无因果关系的虚假相关性，导致模型偏向多数样本而误分类少数样本。现有子集选择方法依赖的评分函数受虚假特征影响，无法准确评估核心特征的重要性，且通常需要未知的组标签。

Method: 提出两阶段样本评分函数，分别解耦核心特征和虚假特征的学习动态并独立评估其难度。基于该评分设计新算法，优先选择包含或不包含虚假相关性的信息性样本，无需组标签。

Result: 在标准ERM模型上使用所选样本训练，仅需10%原始数据即可达到超越现有最先进去偏技术的性能。

Conclusion: 通过解耦核心与虚假特征的学习动态并分别评分，可有效选择信息性样本，在极少量数据下显著缓解虚假相关性问题。

Original Abstract: Real-world datasets often contain spurious correlations that are not causally related to the target label. When such correlations dominate the majority of training samples, models tend to rely on them, leading to misclassification of minority samples that do not exhibit the same spurious patterns. While a potential approach is to select subsets of data to better represent the minority samples, this may require access to group labels, which are typically unknown. Furthermore, as we demonstrate, widely used sample scoring functions in the invariant subset or coreset selection literature largely depend on spurious features and therefore fail to accurately capture the importance or difficulty of core, causally relevant features. Accordingly, we propose to mitigate spurious correlations by developing a two-stage sample scoring function that disentangles the learning dynamics of core and spurious features and evaluates their difficulty separately. Based on our proposed metric, we introduce a new algorithm to find and prioritize informative samples both with and without spurious correlations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that a standard ERM model trained on our selected samples achieves superior performance compared to state-of-the-art debiasing techniques, while requiring as little as 10\% of the original training data.

中文摘要翻译: 现实世界的数据集通常包含与目标标签无因果关系的虚假相关性。当这类相关性主导大多数训练样本时，模型倾向于依赖它们，导致对不具备相同虚假模式的少数样本产生误分类。一种潜在方法是选择数据子集以更好地代表少数样本，但这可能需要访问通常未知的组标签。此外，正如我们所展示的，不变子集或核心集选择文献中广泛使用的样本评分函数在很大程度上依赖于虚假特征，因此无法准确捕捉核心因果相关特征的重要性或难度。为此，我们提出通过开发一种两阶段样本评分函数来缓解虚假相关性，该函数解耦了核心特征与虚假特征的学习动态，并分别评估它们的难度。基于我们提出的度量标准，我们引入了一种新算法来寻找并优先选择包含及不包含虚假相关性的信息性样本。大量实验表明，在我们选择的样本上训练的标准ERM模型，仅需原始训练数据的10%，就能达到超越现有最先进去偏技术的优越性能。

</details>


### [174] [Learning Coherent Representations: A Topological Approach to Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02841)
*Sigurd Gaukstad,Melvin Vaupel,Valdemar Kargård Olsen,Erik Hermansen,Benjamin Dunn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“相干性”（coherence）这一几何性质，受大脑神经编码启发，要求非负矩阵的行（样本）与列（特征）在几何上形成聚类对应，从而使得学到的特征具有可解释性。作者设计了可微损失函数Coh，在自编码器和BERT词嵌入上验证了该方法能生成具有拓扑一致性的可解释特征空间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 深度神经网络中学到的单个特征（神经元）往往缺乏可解释性，一个神经元可能对分散的、不相关的输入产生响应。受大脑中网格细胞、头朝向细胞等神经元对连续状态空间区域响应的启发，作者希望让学到的特征具有几何连续性，从而实现可解释性。

Method: 定义“相干矩阵”概念：非负矩阵中每行（样本）由几何上聚类的列（特征）描述，且每个样本可由某个特征良好描述、每个特征被某些样本需要。基于Fréchet方差设计可微目标函数Coh来强制学习过程满足相干性。从拓扑角度证明相干矩阵能保证样本空间和特征空间的Vietoris-Rips滤子具有有界交错，从而共享兼容的拓扑结构。

Result: 在合成数据、旋转MNIST数据集的自编码器以及BERT语言数据的词嵌入上验证，相比稀疏性，相干性不仅限制特征激活的样本数量，更限制哪些样本被激活（要求几何连通性），从而产生可解释的特征和可解释的特征空间。

Conclusion: 相干性作为一种新的几何正则化方法，能有效提升特征的可解释性，使特征空间具有与数据空间一致的拓扑结构，优于传统的稀疏性约束。

Original Abstract: Deep neural networks learn representations where individual features often lack interpretable meaning; a single neuron may activate for scattered, unrelated inputs. We introduce coherence, a geometric property inspired by neural coding in the brain, where neurons like grid cells and head direction cells respond to contiguous regions of state space. A non-negative matrix is coherent if each row (sample) attends to geometrically clustered columns (features) and vice versa, and in addition every sample is well described by some feature and every feature is needed by some sample. We prove that coherent matrices induce a bounded interleaving between the Vietoris-Rips filtrations of samples and features, guaranteeing that both spaces share compatible topological structure. This geometric constraint facilitates interpretability. For example, if data lies on a circle, coherent features must tile that circle into contiguous arcs. We introduce Coh, a differentiable objective function based on Fréchet variance that enforces coherence during training. Unlike sparsity, which bounds how many samples a feature activates on, coherence bounds which samples, requiring geometric connectivity rather than only rarity. This yields not just interpretable features but an interpretable feature space. We validate Coh in an auto-encoder using synthetic and rotated MNIST datasets and in a token embedding of BERT using language data.

中文摘要翻译: 深度神经网络学习的表示中，单个特征往往缺乏可解释的含义；单个神经元可能对分散的、不相关的输入产生响应。我们提出相干性这一几何性质，其灵感来源于大脑中的神经编码——如网格细胞和头朝向细胞对状态空间的连续区域产生响应。如果一个非负矩阵的每一行（样本）关注于几何上聚类的列（特征），且反之亦然，并且每个样本都能由某个特征良好描述、每个特征都被某些样本所需要，则该矩阵是相干的。我们证明了相干矩阵会在样本和特征的Vietoris-Rips滤子之间诱导出有界的交错，从而保证两个空间共享兼容的拓扑结构。这一几何约束促进了可解释性。例如，如果数据位于一个圆圈上，相干特征必须将该圆圈分割为连续的弧段。我们提出了Coh，这是一个基于Fréchet方差的可微目标函数，用于在训练过程中强制执行相干性。与稀疏性（限制特征激活多少个样本）不同，相干性限制的是哪些样本被激活，要求几何连通性而非仅稀有性。这不仅产生可解释的特征，还产生可解释的特征空间。我们在使用合成数据和旋转MNIST数据集的自编码器中，以及在使用语言数据的BERT词嵌入中验证了Coh的有效性。

</details>


### [175] [Spectral-Progressive Thought Flow for Lightweight Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02842)
*Yixian Shen,Zhiheng Yang,Qi Bi,Changshuo Wang,Shuai Wang,Jia-Hong Huang,George Floros,Prayag Tiwari,Anuj Pathania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SpecFlow框架，将中间视觉思维表示为固定大小的离散余弦空间，结合分类器无关引导实现轻量级多模态空间推理，在保持推理性能的同时将计算和KV缓存成本降低高达2.1倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态空间推理通常依赖冗长的中间文本和视觉思维链，视觉token累积和密集的跨模态注意力带来巨大的计算和内存开销，亟需一种轻量化的推理框架来解决该问题。

Method: 提出谱渐进式思维流（SpecFlow），将中间视觉思维表示为固定大小的离散余弦空间，利用能量压缩保留全局布局和关系结构，仅在需要高空间精度时引入高频细节；通过分类器无关引导实现自回归文本思维对基于流的视觉工作空间更新的控制，无需扩展上下文。

Result: SpecFlow在实现竞争性或更优推理性能的同时，将计算和KV缓存成本降低了高达2.1倍。

Conclusion: SpecFlow通过固定大小的视觉工作空间和仅依赖当前视觉状态与累积文本轨迹的更新机制，实现了长程推理中稳定的延迟和内存占用，证明了该方法的有效性。

Original Abstract: Multimodal spatial reasoning often relies on long chains of intermediate textual and visual thoughts, where accumulating visual tokens and dense cross-modal attention incur substantial computation and memory overhead. To address this challenge, we propose Spectral-Progressive Thought Flow (SpecFlow), a novel lightweight multimodal spatial reasoning framework that represents intermediate visual thoughts in a fixed-size discrete cosine space. By exploiting strong energy compaction, SpecFlow preserves global layout and relational structure while introducing high-frequency details only when increased spatial precision is required. To align visual state evolution with linguistic intent, classifier-free guidance enables autoregressive textual thoughts to steer flow-based updates of the visual workspace/state without expanding the context. As a result, SpecFlow maintains a bounded visual workspace whose updates depend only on the current visual state and accumulated textual trace, enabling long-horizon inference with stable latency and memory usage independent of reasoning depth. Empirical results show that SpecFlow achieves competitive or superior reasoning performance while reducing computation and KV cache costs by up to 2.1 times.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态空间推理通常依赖冗长的中间文本和视觉思维链，其中视觉token的累积和密集的跨模态注意力会带来巨大的计算和内存开销。为解决这一挑战，我们提出谱渐进式思维流（SpecFlow），一种新颖的轻量级多模态空间推理框架，将中间视觉思维表示为固定大小的离散余弦空间。通过利用强能量压缩特性，SpecFlow在保留全局布局和关系结构的同时，仅在需要更高空间精度时引入高频细节。为使视觉状态演化与语言意图对齐，分类器无关引导使自回归文本思维能够驱动基于流的视觉工作空间/状态更新，而无需扩展上下文。因此，SpecFlow保持有界的视觉工作空间，其更新仅取决于当前视觉状态和累积的文本轨迹，从而在延迟和内存使用与推理深度无关的情况下实现长程推理。实验结果表明，SpecFlow在实现竞争性或更优推理性能的同时，将计算和KV缓存成本降低了高达2.1倍。

</details>


### [176] [A Systematic Evaluation of Current Architectures in Wind Power Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02849)
*Vinicius Bortolini,Gilson Adamczuk Oliveira,Erick Oliveira Rodrigues,Matheus Henrique Dal Molin Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了结合深度学习、模态分解与统计方法的混合方法在风电区间预测中的应用，发现集成VMD、EEMD等分解技术能通过缩窄预测区间并保持覆盖率来提升预测精度与可靠性，同时指出了评估指标标准化不足、计算复杂度和缺乏真实世界验证等挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 风电具有天然的不确定性，区间预测能够量化这种不确定性以促进风电并网效率。现有研究虽多，但缺乏对混合方法（深度学习+模态分解+统计方法）的系统性综述，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，运用潜在狄利克雷分配（LDA）进行主题建模以指导论文筛选；重点分析混合模型（如VMD/EEMD+LSTM/ELM）的双模型策略（独立预测上下界）、数据分解技术及区间质量评估指标。

Result: 混合模型结合VMD、EEMD等分解技术能显著提高预测准确性和可靠性，通过缩窄预测区间且不牺牲覆盖率来实现。大多数研究采用双模型策略分别预测上下界，并使用EMD/EEMD/VMD分解输入数据后输入LSTM或ELM分别训练。区间质量通常通过平衡覆盖率和区间宽度的指标评估。

Conclusion: 研究强调了区间预测对风电运行的价值，指出了标准化评估指标缺失、计算复杂度高和缺乏真实世界验证等挑战，为提升模型鲁棒性和决策能力提供了见解。

Original Abstract: Interval wind speed forecasting is essential for the efficient integration of wind energy into power systems, as it accounts for the inherent uncertainty of wind resources. This study presents a systematic literature review focused on hybrid approaches to interval forecasting of wind generation, exploring the combination of deep learning, modal decomposition, and statistical methods. To guide the paper selection, Latent Dirichlet Allocation (LDA) was applied for topic modeling, enabling the identification of patterns and research trends. The findings emphasize that integrating hybrid models with decomposition techniques-such as Variational Mode Decomposition (VMD) and Ensemble Empirical Mode Decomposition (EEMD)-enhances forecast accuracy and reliability by narrowing prediction intervals without compromising coverage. Regarding interval construction, most studies adopt a dual-model strategy, independently forecasting the lower and upper bounds. Input data are commonly decomposed using techniques like EMD, EEMD, or VMD, which extract frequency-based components. These components serve as inputs to models such as LSTM or ELM, trained separately for each bound. This approach allows for targeted modeling of uncertainty, improving flexibility and precision, Interval quality is typically evaluated through metrics that balance coverage and interval width. The review also highlights challenges, including the lack of standardized evaluation metrics, computational complexity, and limited real-world validation. Overall, the study reinforces the value of interval forecasting for wind energy operations and offers insights for advancing model robustness and decision-making.

中文摘要翻译: 区间风速预测对于风能高效集成到电力系统至关重要，因为它能够量化风资源的固有不确定性。本文提出了一项系统性的文献综述，聚焦于风电出力区间预测的混合方法，探索深度学习、模态分解和统计方法的组合。为指导论文筛选，采用潜在狄利克雷分配（LDA）进行主题建模，从而识别研究模式与趋势。研究结果强调，将混合模型与变分模态分解（VMD）和集成经验模态分解（EEMD）等分解技术相结合，能够通过在不牺牲覆盖率的前提下缩窄预测区间，从而提升预测的准确性与可靠性。在区间构建方面，大多数研究采用双模型策略，分别独立预测下界和上界。输入数据通常使用EMD、EEMD或VMD等分解技术提取基于频率的分量，这些分量作为LSTM或ELM等模型的输入，分别针对每个边界进行训练。这种方法能够有针对性地对不确定性进行建模，提高了灵活性与精度。区间质量通常通过平衡覆盖率与区间宽度的指标进行评估。综述还指出了若干挑战，包括缺乏标准化的评估指标、计算复杂度高以及缺乏真实世界验证。总体而言，本研究强调了区间预测对风电运行的价值，并为提升模型鲁棒性和决策能力提供了见解。

</details>


### [177] [RESCAST-100K: A Comprehensive Dataset for Cross-Domain Residential Load and Indoor Temperature Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02852)
*Jainam Dhruva,Yousaf Raza,A. B. Siddique,Simone Silvestri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RESCAST-100K，一个大规模住宅建筑预测基准，用于系统评估跨域泛化（迁移学习、域适应、零样本泛化），包含约10万EnergyPlus仿真美国家庭数据和五个真实数据集，支持多目标（总负荷、HVAC负荷、室内温度）预测，实验表明交叉注意力与MLP-mixer模型在域偏移下优于循环网络和经典Transformer基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有住宅建筑预测研究受限于缺乏全面的住宅数据集：已有基准覆盖范围窄，且很少支持结构化的跨域评估，难以系统研究域适应和迁移学习在数据异质性和稀疏性下的表现。

Method: 构建RESCAST-100K基准，包含约10万EnergyPlus仿真的美国住宅（源自ResStock）和五个统一模式的真实数据集；提供配置驱动接口，沿地理、气候区、墙体构造、供暖设备等可解释轴实例化源域和目标域，支持受控域偏移下的系统评估；涵盖总负荷、HVAC负荷、室内温度三个耦合目标，15分钟分辨率时间序列，配以天气、HVAC设定点和40余个静态建筑协变量。

Result: 在零样本跨域性能、缺失输入条件和多预测任务上，交叉注意力模型和MLP-mixer模型一致优于循环神经网络和经典Transformer基线。

Conclusion: RESCAST-100K旨在帮助机器学习和建筑分析社区推进家庭、社区和电网尺度下的跨域住宅建筑预测研究。

Original Abstract: Accurate short-term forecasting of residential energy load and indoor temperature is essential for home energy management systems, grid-level demand response, and community energy efficiency efforts. Domain adaptation and transfer learning have shown promise for improving forecasting accuracy under data heterogeneity and scarcity commonly seen in residential settings. However, progress is limited by the lack of comprehensive residential datasets: existing benchmarks are narrow in target coverage and rarely support structured cross-domain evaluation. We introduce RESCAST-100K, a large-scale residential forecasting benchmark for studying cross-domain generalization. It provides a configuration-driven interface that instantiates source and target domains along interpretable axes, including geography, climate zone, wall construction, and heating equipment, enabling systematic evaluation of transfer learning, domain adaptation, and zero-shot generalization under controlled domain shifts. The benchmark covers approximately 100,000 EnergyPlus-simulated U.S. homes derived from ResStock, with 15-minute time series for three coupled targets per home: total load, HVAC load, and indoor temperature. These are paired with weather channels, HVAC setpoints, and over 40 static building covariates. RESCAST-100K also integrates five real-world residential datasets under a unified schema, supporting sim-to-real evaluation on the same tasks. We benchmark recurrent, attention-based, and MLP-mixer architectures for zero-shot performance across domains, missing-input conditions, and forecasting tasks. Cross-attention and MLP-mixer models consistently outperform recurrent and classical transformer baselines under domain shift. RESCAST-100K is intended to aid the machine learning and building analytics communities advance cross-domain residential forecasting at home, community, and grid scale.

中文摘要翻译: 住宅能源负荷与室内温度的准确短期预测对家庭能源管理系统、电网级需求响应以及社区能效提升至关重要。域适应和迁移学习在改善住宅场景常见的数据异质性和稀疏性下的预测精度方面展现出前景。然而，由于缺乏全面的住宅数据集，进展受到限制：现有基准在目标覆盖范围上狭窄，且很少支持结构化的跨域评估。我们提出了RESCAST-100K，一个用于研究跨域泛化的大规模住宅预测基准。它提供了一个配置驱动的接口，可沿地理区域、气候区、墙体构造和供暖设备等可解释轴实例化源域和目标域，从而在受控域偏移下实现迁移学习、域适应和零样本泛化的系统评估。该基准涵盖约10万个基于EnergyPlus仿真的美国住宅（源自ResStock），每个住宅提供三个耦合目标的15分钟分辨率时间序列：总负荷、暖通空调（HVAC）负荷和室内温度。这些数据与天气通道、HVAC设定点以及超过40个静态建筑协变量配对。RESCAST-100K还以统一模式整合了五个真实世界住宅数据集，支持在相同任务上进行仿真到真实的评估。我们对循环神经网络、注意力机制和MLP-mixer架构在跨域零样本性能、缺失输入条件和多预测任务上进行了基准测试。交叉注意力和MLP-mixer模型在域偏移下始终优于循环网络和经典Transformer基线。RESCAST-100K旨在帮助机器学习和建筑分析社区在家庭、社区和电网尺度上推进跨域住宅预测研究。

</details>


### [178] [GRZO: Group-Relative Zeroth-Order Optimization for Large Language Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02857)
*Liyan Tan,Yequan Zhao,Yifan Yang,Ruijie Zhang,Xinling Yu,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRZO提出了一种基于组相对归一化的零阶优化器，通过为每个小批量样本生成一个伪独立扰动并聚合组相对归一化后的逐样本损失，在不增加前向计算成本的情况下将有效梯度方向数从1提升至批量大小，显著降低了梯度估计方差并提升了模型微调性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 零阶优化是反向传播的内存高效替代方案，但其梯度估计的高方差限制了部署。现有方法（如MeZO）梯度方差大，导致收敛慢、性能受限。

Method: GRZO为每个小批量样本生成一个伪独立扰动，并通过组相对归一化聚合逐样本损失，从而将有效梯度方向数从1提升至批量大小，同时保持推理级内存占用。

Result: 在RoBERTa-large、Llama3-8B和OPT-13B上的多个任务中，GRZO在Llama3-8B上相比MeZO平均准确率提升+3.0，峰值GPU显存降低23%；作为MeZO核心的即插即用替代，将稀疏、低秩和量化ZO变体平均提升+6.0。

Conclusion: GRZO是一种方向无偏、方差随批量大小比例缩减的零阶优化器，具有更紧的非凸收敛界，是MeZO的高效替代方案。

Original Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization is a memory-efficient alternative to backpropagation for fine-tuning large language models, but its deployment is limited by the high variance of gradient estimation. We propose GRZO, a Group-Relative Zeroth-Order optimizer that draws one pseudo-independent perturbation per mini-batch example and aggregates the per-example losses through group-relative normalization, raising the effective gradient-direction count from one to the batch size at no additional forward cost while preserving inference-level memory. We prove that GRZO is directionally unbiased with variance shrinking proportionally to the batch size, yielding a tighter nonconvex convergence bound than MeZO. Across RoBERTa-large, Llama3-8B, and OPT-13B over multiple tasks, GRZO improves average accuracy on Llama3-8B by $+3.0$ over MeZO at $23\%$ lower peak GPU memory; as a drop-in replacement for the MeZO core, it lifts sparse, low-rank, and quantized ZO variants by $+6.0$ on average.

中文摘要翻译: 零阶优化是反向传播的一种内存高效替代方案，用于微调大语言模型，但其部署受限于梯度估计的高方差。我们提出GRZO，一种组相对零阶优化器，它为每个小批量样本生成一个伪独立扰动，并通过组相对归一化聚合逐样本损失，从而在不增加前向计算成本且保持推理级内存占用的前提下，将有效梯度方向数从1提升至批量大小。我们证明GRZO是方向无偏的，其方差随批量大小比例缩减，从而获得比MeZO更紧的非凸收敛界。在RoBERTa-large、Llama3-8B和OPT-13B的多个任务上，GRZO在Llama3-8B上的平均准确率相比MeZO提升+3.0，峰值GPU显存降低23%；作为MeZO核心的即插即用替代，它使稀疏、低秩和量化零阶变体的平均性能提升+6.0。

</details>


### [179] [Forgetting is Not Erasure: Recovering Latent Knowledge via Transport Keys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02860)
*Archie Chaudhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了灾难性遗忘完全由表征丢失导致的观点，提出相当一部分遗忘源于网络内部阶段之间的接口漂移（interface drift），而非任务相关计算的永久擦除。通过引入“传输键”（transport key）——一种轻量级的接口对齐算子——在连续学习后恢复早期任务的性能，实验表明该方法能在不阻止权重更新的情况下有效缓解遗忘。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究通常将灾难性遗忘归因于模型在顺序训练后丢失了支持早期任务的特征表示。本文旨在挑战这一强形式观点，探究遗忘是否部分源于网络内部阶段间的接口漂移，而非特征的永久擦除。

Method: 提出一种拼接评估协议（stitched evaluation protocol），将更新后网络的前期计算与原始网络的后期计算结合，并引入轻量级、任务特定的“传输键”（transport key）作为接口对齐算子。传输键通过少量配对锚点激活估计得到，并采用模型拼接（model stitching）进行评估。

Result: 在split CIFAR-100数据集上使用ResNet风格网络，传输键在顺序训练任务B后恢复了大部分任务A的性能；在紧凑型视觉Transformer上也观察到类似的恢复模式。这表明相当一部分遗忘可通过接口对齐被恢复。

Conclusion: 连续学习可能需要更好的机制来索引和重新访问潜在计算，而不仅仅是防止权重变化的方法。遗忘更多是接口漂移问题，而非特征永久丢失。

Original Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is often framed as a representational problem: after sequential training, a model appears to lose the features that supported performance on earlier tasks. We challenge the stronger form of this view. Across controlled continual-learning settings, we find that a significant portion of apparent forgetting can be attributed to interface drift between internal stages rather than permanent erasure of task-relevant computation. We study this phenomenon through a stitched evaluation protocol that combines early computation from a post-update network with late computation from its predecessor, optionally mediated by a compact, task-specific transport key. We describe transport keys at a systems level as compact interface-alignment operators estimated from a small set of paired anchor activations and evaluated through model stitching. On split CIFAR-100 with a ResNet-style network, transport keys recover most of the original Task A performance after sequential training on Task B. On a compact vision transformer, we observe a similar recovery pattern. These results suggest that continual learning may require better mechanisms for indexing and re-accessing latent computations, not only methods that prevent weight change.

中文摘要翻译: 灾难性遗忘通常被描述为一个表征问题：在顺序训练之后，模型似乎丢失了支撑早期任务性能的特征。我们挑战了这种观点的强形式。在受控的连续学习场景中，我们发现相当一部分表观上的遗忘可以归因于内部阶段之间的接口漂移（interface drift），而非任务相关计算的永久擦除。我们通过一种拼接评估协议（stitched evaluation protocol）来研究这一现象，该协议将更新后网络的前期计算与其前身网络的后期计算相结合，并可选择由一个紧凑的、任务特定的传输键（transport key）作为中介。我们从系统层面将传输键描述为紧凑的接口对齐算子，通过少量配对的锚点激活估计得到，并通过模型拼接（model stitching）进行评估。在split CIFAR-100数据集上使用ResNet风格网络，传输键在顺序训练任务B后恢复了大部分任务A的性能。在紧凑型视觉Transformer上，我们观察到类似的恢复模式。这些结果表明，连续学习可能需要更好的机制来索引和重新访问潜在计算，而不仅仅是防止权重变化的方法。

</details>


### [180] [RRISE: Robust Radius Inference via a Surrogate Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02876)
*Jong-Ik Park,Shreyas Chaudhari,Carlee Joe-Wong,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RRISE框架，通过训练一个替代模型（surrogate）将随机平滑（RS）的认证过程压缩为单次前向传播，用共形校准保证保守性，在图像分类基准上以极小的认证精度损失替代了最多10^4次蒙特卡洛采样。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随机平滑虽然能提供架构无关的ℓ₂鲁棒性认证，但其每个输入都依赖大量蒙特卡洛（MC）采样，导致计算成本过高，不适合实时系统。作者认为这种成本是结构性的而非本质性的，可以通过在部署流中共享信息来大幅降低。

Method: 提出RRISE框架：（1）训练一个替代模型（surrogate），以预先计算的MC类计数目标为监督，使用软标签交叉熵损失进行训练；（2）通过一次性共形校准（conformal calibration）将替代模型的预测转化为可证明保守的认证半径。最终得到的认证是可部署验证的：当校准半径为正时，替代模型的预测与平滑分类器一致，且平滑分类器在该半径的球内是常数。

Result: 在图像分类基准上，RRISE与固定预算MC的认证精度差距在0.84个百分点以内，但将每次查询需要的10^4次噪声基模型评估替换为单次替代模型前向传播，并在约10^5次部署查询后即可回收MC训练成本。在CIFAR-100和Tiny ImageNet上，先前唯一的离线替代方法失效，而RRISE的认证精度提高了1.23到1.91倍。

Conclusion: RRISE证明了高效的随机平滑可以作为重复部署场景下实现认证鲁棒性的实用路径，通过离线替代模型加共形校准的方式大幅降低了认证开销。

Original Abstract: Randomized smoothing (RS) uses a smoothed classifier to provide architecture-agnostic certificates of $\ell_2$ classification robustness, but its dependence on per-input Monte Carlo (MC) sampling undermines its use in real-time systems. We argue that this cost is structural rather than fundamental, such that it can be significantly reduced by sharing information across the deployment stream. We introduce RRISE, an RS framework that compresses certification into a single forward pass through a learned surrogate. RRISE trains the surrogate against precomputed MC class-count targets via a soft-label cross-entropy loss and converts surrogate predictions into provably conservative certified radii through a one-time conformal calibration step. The resulting certificate is deployment-verifiable: whenever the calibrated radius is positive, the surrogate's prediction provably matches the smoothed classifier's and the smoothed classifier is constant on a ball of that radius around the input. Across image classification benchmarks, RRISE matches fixed-budget MC certified accuracy within $0.84$ percentage points while replacing up to $10^4$ noisy base-model evaluations per query with a single surrogate forward pass, recouping MC training cost after $\approx 10^5$ deployment queries. On CIFAR-100 and Tiny ImageNet, where the only prior offline-surrogate method collapses, RRISE achieves $1.23$ to $1.91\times$ higher certified accuracy, establishing efficient randomized smoothing as a practical path to certified robustness in repeated-deployment settings.

中文摘要翻译: 随机平滑 (RS) 利用平滑分类器提供架构无关的 ℓ₂ 分类鲁棒性认证，但其依赖于每个输入的蒙特卡洛 (MC) 采样，这限制了它在实时系统中的应用。我们认为这种成本是结构性的而非本质性的，因此可以通过在部署流中共享信息来显著降低。我们引入了 RRISE，一种将认证压缩为通过学习的替代模型进行单次前向传播的 RS 框架。RRISE 通过软标签交叉熵损失，以预先计算的 MC 类计数目标为监督训练替代模型，并通过一次性共形校准步骤将替代模型的预测转化为可证明保守的认证半径。得到的证书是可部署验证的：每当校准半径为正值时，替代模型的预测可证明与平滑分类器的预测一致，且平滑分类器在输入周围该半径的球内是常数。在多个图像分类基准测试中，RRISE 与固定预算 MC 的认证精度差距在 0.84 个百分点以内，同时将每次查询所需的多达 10^4 次带噪基模型评估替换为单次替代模型前向传播，在约 10^5 次部署查询后即可收回 MC 训练成本。在 CIFAR-100 和 Tiny ImageNet 上，唯一先前的离线替代方法失效，而 RRISE 实现了 1.23 到 1.91 倍更高的认证精度，将高效随机平滑确立为在重复部署场景下实现认证鲁棒性的实用路径。

</details>


### [181] [Are we really tilting? The mechanics of reward guidance in flow and diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02884)
*Sanjit Dandapanthula,Nicholas M. Boffi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了奖励引导扩散模型中奖励破解的根本原因——Doob h函数的有限粒子plug-in估计近似，并提出了无需额外计算成本的闭环奖励阻尼调度来修正模式内偏差，同时阐明了best-of-n采样对模式选择失败的补偿作用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有奖励引导算法虽经验有效但易出现奖励破解（过度优化奖励而损害生成保真度），先前工作归因于神经奖励函数复杂性或扩散训练隐式偏差，但其根本起源尚不明确。本文旨在从理论层面厘清奖励破解的根本原因。

Method: 在高斯和高斯混合目标（二次奖励函数）的最简非平凡设定下，通过闭式推导隔离了有限粒子plug-in估计Doob h函数的两种失败模式：模式内奖励破解和无法选择高奖励模式。据此提出闭环奖励阻尼调度（无需额外计算）修正模式内偏差，并分析best-of-n采样的作用。

Result: 理论分析表明有限粒子plug-in估计是奖励破解的根本原因；提出的闭环奖励阻尼调度可有效修正模式内偏差；在高斯混合、2D棋盘和FLUX.1文本到图像生成实验上验证了理论结论的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 奖励破解源于实际实现中有限粒子plug-in估计Doob h函数的近似，而非神经网络的复杂性；可通过闭环奖励阻尼调度修正模式内偏差，而best-of-n采样可补偿模式选择失败。

Original Abstract: Reward guidance algorithms steer a learned generative process toward the reward-tilted measure at inference time. While empirically powerful, these methods are prone to reward hacking: the guided model over-optimizes the reward at the cost of fidelity to the learned distribution. Prior work has attributed this to the complexity of neural reward functions or implicit biases in diffusion training, but its fundamental origins remain poorly understood. We show that reward hacking arises from an approximation made in most practical implementations of reward-guided diffusion -- finite-particle plug-in estimation of the Doob h-function -- even in the simplest non-trivial settings of Gaussian and Gaussian mixture targets with quadratic rewards. In closed form, we isolate two distinct failure modes of the plug-in estimator: it leads to reward hacking within each mode and it cannot select high-reward modes. We propose a closed-form reward damping schedule that corrects the within-mode bias with no additional compute, and clarify the role of best-of-n sampling in compensating for the mode selection failure. Experiments on Gaussian mixture targets, a 2D checkerboard, and FLUX.1 text-to-image generation confirm that our theoretical insights carry over to practical settings.

中文摘要翻译: 奖励引导算法在推理阶段引导学习到的生成过程朝向奖励倾斜的测度。虽然这些方法经验上很强大，但它们容易产生奖励破解：引导模型过度优化奖励，以牺牲对学习分布的保真度为代价。先前的工作将其归因于神经奖励函数的复杂性或扩散训练的隐式偏差，但其根本起源仍知之甚少。我们证明，即使在高斯和高斯混合目标（具有二次奖励）的最简非平凡设定下，奖励破解也源于大多数实际奖励引导扩散实现中采用的一种近似——有限粒子plug-in估计Doob h函数。通过闭式推导，我们隔离了plug-in估计器的两种不同失败模式：它在每个模式内导致奖励破解，并且无法选择高奖励模式。我们提出了一种闭环奖励阻尼调度，无需额外计算即可修正模式内偏差，并阐明了best-of-n采样在补偿模式选择失败中的作用。在高斯混合目标、2D棋盘和FLUX.1文本到图像生成上的实验证实了我们的理论见解可以迁移到实际场景中。

</details>


### [182] [Scalable Uncertainty Quantification for Extreme Weather Forecasting via Empirical Neural Tangent Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02886)
*Jose Marie Antonio Miñoza,Rex Gregor Laylo,Sebastian C. Ibañez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于神经正切核的不确定性量化方法（NTK-UQ），利用最后一层经验特征为深度学习天气模型提供不确定性估计。理论分析揭示了UQ质量依赖于架构的两种机制：方差坍缩机制和分解性能机制。相比分裂共形预测，NTK-UQ在90%覆盖率下预测区间锐减31-37%，并能生成随极端事件严重程度自适应的区间，无需重新训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 深度学习天气模型虽能快速生成确定性预测，但缺乏不确定性估计，这在极端天气事件的高风险决策中是一个关键缺陷。

Method: 基于神经正切核（NTK）的不确定性量化方法，利用最后一层经验特征。理论分析架构依赖性的两种机制：方差坍缩（解释UQ何时失效，特征谱集中架构需激进截断，注意力模型可容忍全秩计算）和分解性能（独立成分分析利用高阶统计量分离重尾极端事件特征，优于仅捕获二阶方差的奇异值分解）。数据驱动规则根据特征谱集中比选择ICA或SVD。

Result: 相比分裂共形预测，NTK-UQ在90%覆盖率下预测区间锐减31-37%，且能生成随极端事件严重程度自适应的区间，共形预测无法实现。推理时仅需每个样本一次矩阵-向量乘积。

Conclusion: NTK-UQ为深度学习天气模型提供了高效、无需重新训练的不确定性量化，显著优于共形预测基线，并能自适应极端事件严重程度。

Original Abstract: Deep learning weather models now match numerical weather prediction accuracy while running orders of magnitude faster, but produce deterministic forecasts without uncertainty estimates, a critical gap for high-stakes decisions during extreme weather events. This paper proposes Neural Tangent Kernel-based uncertainty quantification (NTK-UQ) using last-layer empirical features. Theoretical analysis predicts that UQ quality is architecture-dependent through two mechanisms. First, a variance collapse mechanism explains when UQ fails: when the eigenvalue truncation rank approaches the effective rank of the feature space, the GP correction term consumes nearly all prior variance, destroying discrimination between tropical cyclones and routine conditions; architectures with concentrated spectra (spectral operators) require aggressive truncation ($k \leq 10$), while attention-based models tolerate full-rank computation. Second, decomposition performance depends on the non-Gaussian, heavy-tailed structure of extreme weather: Independent Component Analysis exploits higher-order statistics (kurtosis, negentropy) to isolate heavy-tailed extreme-event features, achieving higher discrimination than singular value decomposition, which captures only second-order variance. A data-driven selection rule chooses ICA or SVD from the feature eigenspectrum concentration ratio, correctly prescribing the superior decomposition for all four evaluated architectures. Compared to split conformal prediction (the natural post-hoc baseline), NTK-UQ achieves 31--37\% sharper prediction intervals at 90\% coverage, and uniquely produces \emph{adaptive} intervals that scale with extreme event severity, which conformal prediction cannot achieve by construction. The framework requires no retraining; inference-time uncertainty requires only a single matrix-vector product per sample.

中文摘要翻译: 深度学习天气模型现在能够达到数值天气预报的精度，同时运行速度快数个数量级，但生成的是确定性预测，缺乏不确定性估计——这在极端天气事件的高风险决策中是一个关键缺陷。本文提出基于神经正切核的不确定性量化方法（NTK-UQ），利用最后一层经验特征。理论分析预测，UQ质量通过两种机制依赖于架构。第一，方差坍缩机制解释了UQ何时失效：当特征值截断秩接近特征空间的有效秩时，高斯过程修正项几乎消耗所有先验方差，破坏了热带气旋与常规天气条件之间的区分能力；谱集中（谱算子）的架构需要激进截断（k≤10），而基于注意力的模型可以容忍全秩计算。第二，分解性能取决于极端天气的非高斯、重尾结构：独立成分分析利用高阶统计量（峰度、负熵）分离重尾极端事件特征，实现了比奇异值分解更高的区分度，后者仅捕获二阶方差。数据驱动的选择规则根据特征谱集中比选择ICA或SVD，为所有四种评估架构正确指定了更优的分解方法。与分裂共形预测（自然的事后基线）相比，NTK-UQ在90%覆盖率下实现了31-37%更尖锐的预测区间，并且独特地生成了随极端事件严重程度自适应的区间，而共形预测在构造上无法实现。该框架无需重新训练；推理时的不确定性仅需每个样本一次矩阵-向量乘积。

</details>


### [183] [A Nonmonotone Gradient-Based Algorithm for Symmetric Nonnegative Matrix Factorization and Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02887)
*Ryan Swart,Johannes Brust*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将非单调投影Barzilai-Borwein方法引入对称非负矩阵分解（Symmetric NMF），提出SNMPBB及其变体（Graph-SNMPBB和LAI-SNMPBB），在收敛速度和精度上显著优于现有方法，并证明了全局收敛性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 对称非负矩阵分解在图形聚类和机器学习中有广泛应用，但投影梯度法在该问题中收敛速度慢，远不及其在标准NMF中的表现。本文旨在通过引入非单调投影Barzilai-Borwein方法来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出SNMPBB——首个将非单调投影Barzilai-Borwein方法适配到对称NMF的算法。进一步扩展为图拉普拉斯正则化的Graph-SNMPBB用于图形聚类，以及低秩近似的LAI-SNMPBB用于大规模问题。对所有变体证明了全局收敛到一阶驻点，并证明了随机近似下Barzilai-Borwein曲率信息得以保持。

Result: 在合成数据上，SNMPBB相比SymANLS实现了6倍加速，且在高秩时优势更大。在六个真实聚类基准上，Graph-SNMPBB匹配或超越SymANLS的精度。在34个SuiteSparse矩阵上，LAI-SNMPBB在运行时间和残差质量上均优于最先进的LAI-SymPGNCG。

Conclusion: 梯度算法在对称NMF中的效果远超先前认知，所提出的非单调投影BB方法系列在计算效率和精度上均具有显著优势，为大规模图聚类和机器学习问题提供了高效解决方案。

Original Abstract: Symmetric nonnegative matrix factorization (Symmetric NMF) approximates a matrix as $WW^T$ with nonnegative rectangular factor $W$. It has broad applications in graph clustering and machine learning. In contrast to the NMF, projected gradient methods for the symmetric problem had been associated with slow convergence. To address this, we introduce SNMPBB, the first adaptation of nonmonotone projected Barzilai-Borwein methods to Symmetric NMF, demonstrating that gradient algorithms are significantly more effective than previously understood. We further extend SNMPBB to graph clustering using the graph Laplacian regularization (Graph-SNMPBB) and to large problems with low-rank approximations (LAI-SNMPBB). For all variants we prove global convergence to first-order stationary points and also that Barzilai-Borwein curvature information is preserved with randomized approximations. On synthetic data, SNMPBB achieves 6 times speedup over the alternative SymANLS for similar residuals, with advantages growing at higher ranks. Across six real-world clustering benchmarks, Graph-SNMPBB matches or exceeds SymANLS accuracy. Lastly, LAI-SNMPBB outperforms state-of-the-art LAI-SymPGNCG on 34 SuiteSparse matrices in both runtime and residual quality.

中文摘要翻译: 对称非负矩阵分解（Symmetric NMF）将一个矩阵近似为$WW^T$，其中$W$为非负矩形因子。它在图形聚类和机器学习中具有广泛应用。与标准NMF不同，投影梯度法在对称问题中曾被认为收敛缓慢。为解决这一问题，我们引入了SNMPBB——首个将非单调投影Barzilai-Borwein方法适配到对称NMF的工作，证明梯度算法的效果远超先前认知。我们进一步将SNMPBB扩展至基于图拉普拉斯正则化的图形聚类（Graph-SNMPBB）以及采用低秩近似的大规模问题（LAI-SNMPBB）。对所有变体，我们证明了全局收敛到一阶驻点，并且Barzilai-Borwein曲率信息在随机近似下得以保持。在合成数据上，SNMPBB在相似残差下比替代方法SymANLS实现了6倍加速，且在高秩时优势更加明显。在六个真实世界聚类基准上，Graph-SNMPBB匹配或超越了SymANLS的精度。最后，在34个SuiteSparse矩阵上，LAI-SNMPBB在运行时间和残差质量上均优于最先进的LAI-SymPGNCG。

</details>


### [184] [Multi-Modal Machine Learning for Breast Cancer Recurrence Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02892)
*Jiahao Shao,Xudong Wang,Anam Nawaz Khan,Christopher Brett,Xueping Li,Bing Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种多模态临床数据融合方法，通过规则表达式与优先级冲突消解策略从自由文本病理报告中提取肿瘤特征，结合结构化数据提升乳腺癌复发预测准确性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统预测模型通常仅使用结构化或非结构化单一模态数据，难以捕获完整的临床上下文信息，导致复发风险评估不够准确。

Method: 采用基于规则的规则表达式提取机制结合优先级冲突消解策略，从自由文本病理报告中恢复确定的肿瘤特征，以增强结构化记录；并在多种机器学习模型上对单模态与多模态输入进行性能评估与对比。

Result: 多模态融合方法在预测准确性上始终优于仅使用单一模态数据的方法。

Conclusion: 整合治疗记录、病理报告和临床笔记等多模态临床数据能够有效提升乳腺癌复发预测性能，为临床决策提供更全面的支持。

Original Abstract: Breast cancer recurrence, a leading cause of long-term mortality among survivors, requires timely and accurate risk assessment to guide follow-up care and treatment planning. Traditional predictive models, often limited to either structured or unstructured data alone, struggle to capture the full clinical context. This study examines the impact of integrating multi-modal clinical data, including treatment records, pathology reports, and clinician notes, on recurrence prediction. By integrating a rule-based regular expression extraction mechanism with a rigorous precedence-based conflict reconciliation strategy, our approach effectively recovers definitive tumor characteristics from free-text pathology narratives to augment structured records. We also benchmark performance against commonly used feature sets from prior breast cancer studies to assess the added value of multi-modal integration. Single-source and multi-modal inputs are evaluated across a range of machine learning models. Results show that multi-modal integration consistently improves predictive accuracy compared to single-modal methods.

中文摘要翻译: 乳腺癌复发是导致幸存者长期死亡的主要原因之一，及时准确的风险评估对于指导随访护理和治疗规划至关重要。传统的预测模型通常仅局限于结构化数据或非结构化数据中的一种，难以捕获完整的临床上下文。本研究探讨了整合多模态临床数据（包括治疗记录、病理报告和临床笔记）对复发预测的影响。通过将基于规则的规则表达式提取机制与基于优先级的冲突消解策略相结合，我们的方法有效地从自由文本病理叙述中恢复确定的肿瘤特征，以增强结构化记录。我们还对先前乳腺癌研究中常用的特征集进行了基准性能比较，以评估多模态融合的附加价值。在多种机器学习模型上对单模态和多模态输入进行了评估。结果表明，与单模态方法相比，多模态融合在预测准确性上持续表现出更优的效果。

</details>


### [185] [Fast Unlearning at Scale via Margin Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02920)
*Federico Di Gennaro,Alexander Shevchenko,Fanny Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MASC（Margin Self-Correction），一种基于在线停止规则的高效模型反学习（unlearning）方法，通过缩小遗忘序列中原始token与替代token之间的logit差距来判断停止时机，无需下游评估，在TOFU、MUSE News和MUSE Books上以更低的计算成本达到竞争性的遗忘-保留权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有反学习方法存在两个不必要的计算开销限制了可扩展性：一是固定训练预算下可能训练过度，超出遗忘-保留权衡的最佳点；二是需要保存多个检查点并在下游验证数据上反复评估来选择最终模型，浪费存储和计算资源。

Method: MASC通过在线停止规则实现高效反学习：给定待遗忘的文本序列，主动缩小原始下一个token与最可能替代token之间的logit差距；当所有遗忘序列中足够大比例的token位置平均logit差距足够小时，输出最终模型，无需下游验证数据。

Result: 在TOFU、MUSE News和MUSE Books数据集上，MASC以远低于现有基线的计算成本达到了具有竞争力的遗忘-保留权衡。此外，随着模型规模（参数量）增大，MASC和SimNPO的遗忘指标保持可比，而保留效用提升。

Conclusion: MASC通过在线停止规则消除了冗余训练和下游评估的需求，实现了计算高效的反学习，并且在更大模型上表现出更好的遗忘-保留权衡。

Original Abstract: Language-model unlearning updates a trained model to behave as if it had not seen selected training examples, while preserving utility and avoiding costly retraining. Existing approaches typically fine-tune the pretrained model with a fixed training budget and select the final model afterwards by evaluating several saved checkpoints on downstream validation data. Two sources of unnecessary computation limit scalability: training beyond the desired forget-retain trade-off, and checkpoint selection that requires extra storage and repeated evaluations. To address these limitations, we introduce MArgin Self-Correction (MASC), an efficient unlearning method with an online stopping rule that does not require downstream evaluation. Given a text sequence to be forgotten, MASC actively reduces the logit gap between the original next token and the most likely alternatives. It outputs a final model once this gap is small on average over a sufficiently large proportion of token positions across all forget sequences. On TOFU, MUSE News, and MUSE Books, MASC achieves a competitive forget-retain trade-off at a fraction of the computational cost of existing baselines. We further observe that as we increase model size (a.k.a. number of parameters), the trade-offs improve for both MASC and SimNPO -- the forget metrics remain comparable while retain utility increases.

中文摘要翻译: 语言模型反学习（unlearning）通过更新已训练模型，使其表现得好像从未见过某些选定的训练样本，同时保留模型效用并避免昂贵的重新训练。现有方法通常采用固定训练预算对预训练模型进行微调，之后通过在下游验证数据上评估若干保存的检查点来选择最终模型。两种不必要的计算限制了可扩展性：一是训练超出了理想的遗忘-保留权衡范围；二是检查点选择需要额外的存储和重复评估。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了MArgin Self-Correction（MASC），一种带有在线停止规则的高效反学习方法，无需下游评估。给定待遗忘的文本序列，MASC主动缩小原始下一个token与最可能替代token之间的logit差距。当所有遗忘序列中足够大比例的token位置上的平均差距足够小时，MASC输出最终模型。在TOFU、MUSE News和MUSE Books上，MASC以远低于现有基线的计算成本达到了具有竞争力的遗忘-保留权衡。我们进一步观察到，随着模型规模（即参数量）增大，MASC和SimNPO的权衡均有所改善——遗忘指标保持可比，而保留效用提升。

</details>


### [186] [Hierarchical RBF-KAN and RBF-SKAN Architectures for Multidimensional Function Approximation and Random Field Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02936)
*Mingtao Xia,Qijing Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了两类基于径向基函数的层次化Kolmogorov-Arnold网络：层次RBF-KAN用于高维确定性函数逼近，层次RBF-SKAN用于随机场学习。理论上证明了通用逼近性质，并推导了定量逼近估计，显示该方法能部分缓解维数灾难。实验表明该网络能有效学习多元函数和随机场模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 旨在解决高维函数逼近和随机场学习中的维数灾难问题，提出一种基于径向基函数激活的层次化Kolmogorov-Arnold网络架构，在理论上和实验上验证其有效性。

Method: 提出了两种层次化神经网络架构：(1) 层次RBF-KAN，采用径向基函数作为激活函数，用于多维确定性函数逼近；(2) 层次RBF-SKAN，用于随机场学习。理论上建立了通用逼近性质，并推导了定量逼近估计以说明降维效果。

Result: 理论上证明了两种架构的通用逼近性质；推导了层次RBF-KAN的定量逼近估计，表明其能通过降低有效维数部分缓解维数灾难；证明了层次RBF-SKAN在Wasserstein-2度量下可逼近随机场模型；实验上验证了所提网络能有效学习多元函数和随机场模型。

Conclusion: 基于径向基函数的层次化Kolmogorov-Arnold网络在高维函数逼近和随机场学习中具有理论保证和实际效果，为缓解维数灾难提供了新的有效途径。

Original Abstract: In this manuscript, we propose and analyze hierarchical Kolmogorov--Arnold neural network architectures employing radial basis functions as activation functions for approximating deterministic functions and random field models. Specifically, we develop a hierarchical radial-basis-function Kolmogorov--Arnold network (hierarchical RBF-KAN) for multidimensional deterministic function approximation and a hierarchical radial-basis-function stochastic Kolmogorov--Arnold network (hierarchical RBF-SKAN) for random field learning. From a theoretical perspective, we establish universal approximation results for both architectures. In particular, we derive quantitative approximation estimates for the hierarchical RBF-KAN, showing that the proposed framework has the potential to partially alleviate the curse of dimensionality in learning high-dimensional functions by reducing the effective dimensionality of the approximation problem. Furthermore, we show that the hierarchical RBF-SKAN can approximate random field models under the Wasserstein-2 metric. Empirically, we show that our proposed radial-basis-function-based neural network structure could effectively learn multivariate functions and random field models.

中文摘要翻译: 在本手稿中，我们提出并分析了采用径向基函数作为激活函数的层次化Kolmogorov-Arnold神经网络架构，用于逼近确定性函数和随机场模型。具体而言，我们开发了用于多维确定性函数逼近的层次化径向基函数Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（层次RBF-KAN），以及用于随机场学习的层次化径向基函数随机Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（层次RBF-SKAN）。从理论角度出发，我们为两种架构建立了通用逼近性质。特别地，我们推导了层次RBF-KAN的定量逼近估计，表明所提出的框架通过降低逼近问题的有效维数，具有部分缓解高维函数学习中维数灾难的潜力。此外，我们证明了层次RBF-SKAN可以在Wasserstein-2度量下逼近随机场模型。实验上，我们表明所提出的基于径向基函数的神经网络结构能够有效学习多元函数和随机场模型。

</details>


### [187] [Outsmarting the Chameleon: Counterfactual Decoupling for Tactical OOD Shifts in Live Streaming Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02946)
*Yiran Qiao,Jing Chen,Jiaqi Xu,Yang Liu,Qiwei Zhong,Xiang Ao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对直播场景中恶意行为者通过不断重包装叙事策略来逃避检测的战术性分布偏移问题，提出了一种基于潜在因果的插件框架LPCD，通过在潜在层面建模意图与叙事变化并施加潜在反事实一致性约束，实现鲁棒的直播风险评估，在工业数据集和在线流量上均取得最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有OOD泛化范式在直播风控中面临两大难题：(1) 恶意意图与叙事策略紧密耦合、协同演化，难以解耦；(2) 原始层面的反事实定义不清晰。这导致传统方法难以应对恶意行为者不断变换叙事包装来规避检测的战术性分布偏移。
