Original Abstract: Muon has recently demonstrated strong empirical performance in large language model training, but the theoretical role of momentum in Muon remains unclear. Existing analyses of Muon either remove momentum to study spectral updates in isolation, or retain momentum without explaining why it improves empirical performance. Our work bridges this gap by showing momentum in Muon acts as a spectral filter. Under a structured signal-plus-perturbation gradient model, we prove that momentum suppresses perturbations while preserving the dominant signal, thereby enlarging the spectral gap between them. This enlarged gap stabilizes the singular subspaces of the matrix passed to Muon's orthogonalization step, making the resulting update more reliable. We further show that applying momentum before orthogonalization achieves provably stronger alignment with the signal component of the gradient than either reversing this order or simply removing momentum. Experiments across diverse tasks, including LLM pretraining, support our theoretical analysis. More broadly, our theory offers a starting point for understanding the benefits of momentum in other matrix-based optimizers.

中文摘要翻译: Muon近期在大语言模型训练中展现了强劲的实证性能，但动量在Muon中的理论作用仍不明确。现有对Muon的分析要么移除动量以单独研究谱更新，要么保留动量但未解释其为何提升实证性能。本文通过证明Muon中的动量充当频谱滤波器，弥合了这一空白。在结构化的信号加扰动梯度模型下，我们证明了动量能够抑制扰动同时保留主导信号，从而扩大二者之间的谱间隙。这一被放大的谱间隙稳定了传入Muon正交化步骤的矩阵的奇异子空间，使得最终更新更加可靠。我们进一步证明，相比先正交化后动量或直接移除动量，在正交化之前应用动量能够实现与梯度信号分量更强（可证明）的对齐。涵盖LLM预训练在内的多任务实验支持了我们的理论分析。更广泛地，我们的理论为理解其他基于矩阵的优化器中动量的优势提供了一个起点。

</details>


### [278] [Contrastive Neural Algorithmic Reasoning for Graph Coloring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03923)
*Thien Le,Tianyu Zhao,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对比学习框架用于近似图着色问题，通过学习可迁移的着色几何结构（同类节点嵌入对齐，相邻节点嵌入正交），实现了跨图大小和分布的泛化能力，并在理论和实验上证明了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无监督GNN方法直接优化每个实例，无法泛化到不同大小和分布的图。作者希望提出一种能学习可迁移着色知识的方法，使模型能够泛化到未见过的图。

Method: 提出对比学习框架：让相同颜色的节点嵌入对齐，相邻节点的嵌入推向不同方向。分析了单位范数嵌入下的群体目标，发现了线-原型结构（同类节点嵌入塌缩到共享一维子空间，边连接正交子空间）。研究了有监督下的平稳性条件、投影次梯度动力学的保持性质，以及未归一化变体下的最大间隔偏差。

Result: 理论分析揭示了最优解的线-原型几何结构；实验表明对比GNN编码器在合成图和真实图上泛化效果好，产生的着色冲突少，匹配甚至优于贪心方法。

Conclusion: 对比学习框架能够有效学习可迁移的图着色几何结构，实现跨图泛化，是一种有前景的近似图着色方法。

Original Abstract: Graph coloring seeks to assigns colors to a graph's nodes so that adjacent nodes receive different colors, using as few colors as possible. Here, we study approximate $k$-coloring, where the goal is to use at most $k$ colors while minimizing the number of monochromatic edges. This problem is central to graph theory and has applications in areas such as scheduling and resource allocation. Recent unsupervised GNN approaches optimize each instance directly, precluding generalization across graph sizes and distributions. We instead propose a contrastive learning framework that learns transferable coloring geometry where the embeddings of same-color nodes align, while adjacent nodes' representations are pushed toward distinct directions. We analyze the resulting population objective over bounded-size graphs. For unit-norm embeddings, we show that its optima have a line-prototype structure: Representations of nodes of the same color collapse to a shared one-dimensional subspace, and edges connect orthogonal subspaces. This geometry yields stationarity conditions in the supervised setting and is preserved by projected subgradient dynamics under a balanced-coloring assumption. In an unnormalized variant, gradient descent has a max-margin bias governed by a quotient-graph hard-margin problem. Experiments on synthetic and real-world graphs show that contrastive GNN encoders generalize effectively and produce low-conflict colorings, matching and sometimes improving on greedy approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 图着色旨在为图的节点分配颜色，使得相邻节点颜色不同，并尽可能使用最少的颜色数。本文研究近似$k$-着色问题，其目标是在使用至多$k$种颜色的同时最小化单色边的数量。该问题是图论的核心问题，在调度和资源分配等领域有广泛应用。近期无监督GNN方法直接优化每个实例，无法泛化到不同图大小和分布。本文提出一种对比学习框架，学习可迁移的着色几何结构，其中同色节点的嵌入相互对齐，而相邻节点的表示被推往不同方向。我们分析了有界尺寸图上群体目标的最优解。对于单位范数嵌入，我们证明其最优解具有线-原型结构：同色节点的表示塌缩到共享的一维子空间，边连接正交子空间。该几何结构在有监督设置下产生平稳性条件，并在平衡着色假设下被投影次梯度动力学保持。在未归一化变体中，梯度下降具有由商图硬间隔问题控制的最大间隔偏差。合成图和真实图上的实验表明，对比GNN编码器能有效泛化并产生低冲突的着色，匹配甚至有时优于贪心方法。

</details>


### [279] [FFR: Forward-Forward Learning for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03927)
*Xinyang Liu,Xuanyu Liang,Shiqi Ding,Boyang Li,Zhiqiang Que,Jiayang Li,Guosheng Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FFR框架，首次将前向-前向（FF）算法扩展到回归任务，通过序数竞争 goodness 函数、分层阶梯架构和分层预测与不确定性估计三大创新，在五个真实回归基准上平均恢复BP 98.6%的精度，同时将峰值训练内存降至BP的27%（深度8）和8%（深度32）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: FF算法虽然计算高效且生物合理，但天然用于分类（依赖正负对比样本对），无法直接应用于回归任务，因为连续目标空间缺乏自然的"对立面"，且标准goodness函数不携带目标量级或排序信息。因此需要将FF扩展到回归领域。

Method: (1) 序数竞争 goodness 函数：用分区神经元组之间的竞争学习替代对比对，在距离感知的序数监督下进行；(2) 分层阶梯架构：浅层学习粗粒度序数判别，深层细化到细粒度回归，并通过多尺度特征聚合实现层间协作；(3) 分层预测与不确定性估计：多尺度预测器联合提供鲁棒预测和预测置信度。

Result: 在五个真实回归基准上平均恢复BP 98.6%的精度；峰值训练内存深度8时仅为BP的27%，深度32时为8%；每轮迭代时间约为BP的72%；显著优于所有无BP的竞争方法。

Conclusion: FFR首次成功将FF算法扩展到回归任务，在保持计算效率和内存优势的同时，实现了与BP相当的回归精度，为生物合理且高效的回归学习提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm offers a computationally efficient and biologically plausible alternative to backpropagation (BP) by training neural networks through purely local, layer-wise optimization. However, FF is inherently designed for classification via contrastive positive-negative sample pairs, and extending it to regression poses fundamental challenges: continuous target space lack natural "opposites" for contrastive learning, and the standard goodness function carries no information about target magnitude or ordering. We propose FFR (Forward-Forward for Regression), to our knowledge, the first framework to extend FF to real-world regression and demonstrate competitive performance across diverse real-world datasets. FFR introduces three key innovations: (1) an ordinal competitive goodness function that replaces contrastive pairs with competitive learning between partitioned neuron groups under distance-aware ordinal supervision; (2) a stratified ladder architecture where shallow layers learn coarse ordinal discrimination and deeper layers refine into fine-grained regression, with multi-scale feature aggregation for inter-layer collaboration; and (3) hierarchical prediction with uncertainty estimation, where multi-scale predictors jointly provide robust predictions and prediction confidence as a free-lunch. Extensive experimental results show FFR recovers on average 98.6% of BP's accuracy across five real-world regression benchmarks while reducing peak training memory to only 27% of BP's at depth 8 and 8% at depth 32, with per-iteration time around 72% of BP's, and substantially outperforms all BP-free competitors.

中文摘要翻译: 前向-前向（FF）算法通过纯局部、逐层优化来训练神经网络，为反向传播（BP）提供了一种计算高效且生物合理的替代方案。然而，FF天然设计用于通过对比性正负样本对进行分类，将其扩展到回归任务面临根本性挑战：连续目标空间缺乏对比学习所需的自然"对立面"，且标准goodness函数不携带目标量级或排序信息。我们提出FFR（Forward-Forward for Regression），据我们所知，这是首个将FF扩展到实际回归任务的框架，并在多种真实数据集上展示了具有竞争力的性能。FFR引入了三项关键创新：(1) 序数竞争goodness函数，用分区神经元组之间的竞争学习替代对比对，在距离感知的序数监督下进行；(2) 分层阶梯架构，其中浅层学习粗粒度序数判别，深层细化到细粒度回归，并通过多尺度特征聚合实现层间协作；(3) 分层预测与不确定性估计，多尺度预测器联合提供鲁棒预测和预测置信度作为额外收益。大量实验结果表明，在五个真实回归基准上，FFR平均恢复了BP 98.6%的精度，同时将峰值训练内存降至BP在深度8时的27%和深度32时的8%，每轮迭代时间约为BP的72%，并显著优于所有无BP的竞争方法。

</details>


### [280] [Value-Aware Stochastic KV Cache Eviction for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03928)
*Ting-Yun Chang,Harvey Yiyun Fu,Deqing Fu,Chenghao Yang,Jesse Thomason,Robin Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VaSE（值感知随机KV缓存驱逐）方法，通过保护大幅值状态和引入随机驱逐决策，在推理模型中实现4倍KV缓存压缩，性能优于现有选择性和驱逐性稀疏注意力方法，弥合了效率与准确性之间的差距。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 推理模型通过长链式思维提升准确性，但长输出导致内存和计算瓶颈。现有KV缓存驱逐方法虽能降低开销，但其准确性低于基于选择的稀疏注意力方法。作者发现两个关键因素：小幅值状态被驱逐会导致灾难性重复推理循环，而驱逐时引入随机性可提高准确性。基于此，提出VaSE方法。

Method: VaSE是一种无需训练的KV缓存驱逐方法，核心包括两点：1) 保护具有异常大幅值的价值状态（value states）不被驱逐；2) 在驱逐决策中引入随机性以增加缓存多样性。该方法兼容FlashAttention2，支持静态内存占用。

Result: 在六个推理任务上，Qwen3模型使用VaSE在4倍KV缓存压缩下，平均准确率高于最强选择性方法（SOTA selection method），同时比最强驱逐方法高出超过4%。

Conclusion: VaSE有效弥合了KV缓存驱逐方法的效率与准确性之间的差距，在实现高效内存占用的同时保持高准确率，且兼容FlashAttention2。

Original Abstract: Reasoning models improve accuracy through extended chains of thought, but their long outputs create a memory and compute bottleneck. KV cache eviction methods reduce this cost by evicting unimportant key-value pairs from the cache, yet they often yield worse accuracy than selection-based sparse attention alternatives, which keep the full KV cache. We identify key factors crucial to KV cache eviction accuracy. First, a small fraction of value states have abnormally large magnitudes, and evicting them causes catastrophic failure where models enter repetitive reasoning loops. Second, introducing stochasticity during eviction improves accuracy by increasing cache diversity. Based on these findings, we propose Value-aware Stochastic KV Cache Eviction (VaSE), a training-free recipe that protects large-magnitude value states and promotes diverse eviction decisions. Across six reasoning tasks, Qwen3 models using VaSE with 4x KV cache compression yield higher average accuracies than SOTA selection method at the same sparsity, while outperforming the strongest eviction method by more than 4%. Overall, VaSE bridges the gap between efficiency and accuracy, supporting FlashAttention2 and enabling a static memory footprint for reasoning models.

中文摘要翻译: 推理模型通过扩展链式思维来提升准确性，但其长输出造成了内存和计算瓶颈。KV缓存驱逐方法通过从缓存中驱逐不重要的键值对来降低这一开销，但这类方法往往比基于选择的稀疏注意力替代方案准确性更低，后者会保留完整的KV缓存。我们识别出对KV缓存驱逐准确性至关重要的关键因素。首先，少量价值状态具有异常大的幅值，驱逐它们会导致灾难性失败——模型进入重复推理循环。其次，在驱逐过程中引入随机性可通过增加缓存多样性来提升准确性。基于这些发现，我们提出了值感知随机KV缓存驱逐（VaSE），这是一种无需训练的方法，能够保护大幅值价值状态并促进多样化的驱逐决策。在六个推理任务上，使用VaSE的Qwen3模型在4倍KV缓存压缩下，其平均准确率高于相同稀疏度下的最强选择性方法（SOTA selection method），同时比最强驱逐方法高出超过4%。总体而言，VaSE弥合了效率与准确性之间的差距，支持FlashAttention2，并为推理模型实现了静态内存占用。

</details>


### [281] [Correcting Neural Operator Spectral Bias via Diffusion Posterior Sampling with Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03936)
*Niccolò Perrone,Fanny Lehmann,Stefania Fresca,Filippo Gatti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FreqNO-DPS框架，通过频率依赖的扩散后验采样，融合神经算子代理与稀疏传感器观测，消除频谱偏差，实现高精度物理场预测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 神经算子代理虽计算快但存在频谱偏差（高频成分被系统性衰减），而稀疏传感器测量虽点精度高但覆盖范围有限。现有方法无法有效结合两者优势并克服偏差。

Method: 将神经算子预测作为辅助观测，融入无条件扩散后验采样框架；提出闭式频谱成形引导分数，按频率依赖精度加权代理输出，无需去噪器反向传播；通过无分布分析界定了频率-扩散-时间平面上的近似误差。

Result: 在3D弹性波场预测中，5%和2%传感器覆盖率下，该方法在所有频段达到近零频谱偏差；而代理单独使用和纯传感器DPS均表现系统性高频衰减；各向同性引导虽改善点精度但将偏差几乎完整带入后验。

Conclusion: 频率依赖的校正是消除频谱偏差的关键而非仅仅有益；框架仅需成对的代理/参考数据，适用性广，可通过提供的相干性诊断验证新代理的适用条件。

Original Abstract: Neural operator surrogates (NO) approximate PDE solutions orders of magnitude faster than numerical solvers, but suffer from spectral bias: high-frequency content is systematically attenuated, limiting reliability where fine-scale structure matters. Sparse sensor measurements of the field are often available too, offering pointwise accuracy without spectral distortion but covering only a small fraction of the domain. We address this by treating NO predictions as auxiliary observations in a diffusion posterior sampling framework. Our method, FreqNO-DPS (https://github.com/niccoloperrone/FreqNO-DPS), combines an unconditional score-based diffusion prior, trained on high-fidelity simulations, with diffusion posterior sampling (DPS) conditioned on sparse observations and guided by a frozen neural operator. Naive integration reintroduces the surrogate's spectral bias; we resolve this with a closed-form, spectrally shaped guidance score that weights the surrogate by its frequency-dependent accuracy and needs no denoiser backpropagation. A distribution-free analysis bounds the approximation error across the frequency-diffusion-time plane and shows the guidance's frequency dependence is preserved regardless of distributional assumptions. On 3D elastic wavefield prediction at 5% and 2% sensor coverage, the method reaches near-zero spectral bias across all bands, where both the surrogate and sensor-only DPS show systematic high-frequency attenuation. Isotropic guidance, the natural baseline, improves pointwise accuracy but carries the bias into the posterior nearly intact, confirming that frequency-dependent calibration is essential, not merely beneficial. The framework needs only paired surrogate/reference data and exploits no problem-specific structure beyond the residual's approximate spectral diagonality, verifiable for new surrogates via the coherence diagnostic we provide.

中文摘要翻译: 神经算子代理（NO）求解PDE的速度比数值求解器快数个数量级，但存在频谱偏差：高频内容被系统性衰减，限制了其在需要精细尺度结构的场景中的可靠性。同时，场分布上的稀疏传感器测量通常可用，提供无频谱失真的点精度，但仅覆盖域的一小部分。我们通过将神经算子预测视为扩散后验采样框架中的辅助观测来解决这一问题。我们的方法FreqNO-DPS（https://github.com/niccoloperrone/FreqNO-DPS）结合了基于高保真仿真训练的无条件分数扩散先验，以及以稀疏观测为条件、由冻结神经算子引导的扩散后验采样（DPS）。直接集成会重新引入代理的频谱偏差；我们通过一种闭式的频谱成形引导分数来解决这一问题，该分数按代理的频率依赖精度进行加权，且无需去噪器反向传播。一种无分布分析界定了频率-扩散-时间平面上的近似误差，并表明引导的频率依赖性不依赖于分布假设。在3D弹性波场预测中，当传感器覆盖率为5%和2%时，该方法在所有频段达到近零频谱偏差，而代理单独和纯传感器DPS均表现出系统性高频衰减。作为自然基线的各向同性引导虽改善了点精度，却将偏差几乎完整地带入后验，这证实了频率依赖校正是必要的，而不仅仅是有益的。该框架仅需成对的代理/参考数据，且除了残差近似的频谱对角性外，无需利用任何问题特定结构——我们提供的相干性诊断可用于验证新代理的适用条件。

</details>


### [282] [q0: Primitives for Hyper-Epoch Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03938)
*Bishwas Mandal,Shmuel Berman,Akshay Vegesna,Samip Dahal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出超周期预训练（q0），将多周期训练预算转化为多样化的模型群体，通过反相关学习率与权重衰减的循环调度、链式蒸馏以及学习先验选择成员，在更少的训练周期内达到甚至超越强集成基线，实现了约12.9倍的数据效率提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着计算量增长快于高质量文本供给，多周期训练成为常态，但单模型预训练会在少量周期后饱和，无法充分利用计算预算。因此需要从训练单一模型转向探索模型群体并聚合其预测。

Method: 提出超周期预训练（q0），包含三个核心原语：(1) 反相关学习率与权重衰减的循环调度，从少数并行轨迹中收集多样模型；(2) 链式蒸馏，每个模型对前一个模型进行蒸馏，使模型质量在群体中累积提升；(3) 学习先验，在留出集上拟合，为任意推理预算选择和加权成员。

Result: 在1.8B参数模型、100M FineWeb token上，q0使用约56周期（4.6倍更少）匹配256周期强集成基线，约67周期（3.8倍更少）匹配基线集成大小，并持续超越。在Slowrun设置下累积约12.9倍数据效率，并迁移到下游基准。

Conclusion: 最优分配随预算变化，因此给出了从单周期到最大预算下如何分配给定周期预算以最大化泛化性能的处方性策略。

Original Abstract: Multi-epoch training is becoming the standard now that compute is growing faster than the supply of high-quality text. But pretraining a single model saturates within a few passes, long before the compute budget is exhausted. We argue this calls for a conceptual shift from training a single model toward exploring a population of models and aggregating their predictions. We introduce hyper-epoch pretraining (q0), which turns a multi-epoch budget into a population of diverse models whose combined predictions reach a lower validation loss than a single refined model. q0 reduces to three core primitives. A cyclic schedule with anti-correlated learning rate and weight decay collects diverse models from a few parallel trajectories. Chain distillation trains each model against its predecessor so that model quality compounds across the population. A learned prior, fit on a held out set, selects and weights members for any inference budget. On a 1.8B-parameter model trained on 100M FineWeb tokens, q0 matches a strong 256-epoch ensemble baseline using only ${\sim}56$ epochs (${\sim}4.6\times$ fewer), or ${\sim}67$ epochs (${\sim}3.8\times$ fewer) when matched to the baseline's ensemble size, and continues to improve beyond it. These gains reach cumulative ${\sim}12.9\times$ data efficiency under the Slowrun setting and transfer to downstream benchmarks. Crucially, the optimal allocation shifts with the budget, so we give prescriptive recipes for how to spend a given epoch budget to maximize generalization, from a single epoch up to the largest budgets.

中文摘要翻译: 多周期训练正成为标准做法，因为计算量的增长速度已超过高质量文本的供应量。但预训练单个模型会在寥寥数轮后饱和，远在计算预算耗尽之前就已停滞。我们认为，这要求从训练单一模型转向探索一个模型群体并聚合其预测。我们引入了超周期预训练（q0），将多周期预算转化为多样化的模型群体，其组合预测的验证损失低于单一精炼模型。q0归结为三个核心原语：(1) 采用学习率与权重衰减反相关的循环调度，从少数几条并行轨迹中收集多样化的模型；(2) 链式蒸馏，使每个模型对其前驱进行蒸馏，从而让模型质量在群体中累积提升；(3) 在留出集上拟合学习先验，为任意推理预算选择和加权成员。在1.8B参数模型、100M FineWeb token上，q0使用约56个周期（约4.6倍更少）即可匹配强大的256周期集成基线，若匹配基线的集成大小则需约67个周期（约3.8倍更少），并持续超越。在Slowrun设置下，这些增益累积达到约12.9倍的数据效率，并迁移到下游基准。关键的是，最优分配随预算变化，因此我们给出了从单周期到最大预算下如何花费给定周期预算以最大化泛化性能的处方性策略。

</details>


### [283] [FlashbackCL: Mitigating Temporal Forgetting in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03939)
*Mubarak A. Ojewale,Adriana E. Chis,Jorge M. Cortes-Mendoza,Bernardo Pulido-Gaytan,Horacio Gonzalez-Velez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FlashbackCL，通过时序衰减标签计数、设备感知回放缓冲区和服务器端核心集筛选，扩展了Flashback方法，解决了联邦学习中客户端数据分布随时间漂移导致的时序遗忘问题，在CIFAR-10上相对Flashback提升6.9%-10.0%，时序遗忘减少68%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有联邦学习遗忘缓解方法假设各客户端数据分布是平稳的，但实际部署中客户端数据分布会随时间漂移。Flashback方法使用单调累积的每类标签计数作为知识代理，在时序分布漂移下会变得失准，使全局模型锚定在过时的类别平衡上。

Method: (i) 时序衰减标签计数替代单调累积标签计数；(ii) 设备感知回放缓冲区，采用类别平衡水库采样(CBRS)；(iii) 在公共蒸馏集上进行服务器端主动核心集筛选。

Result: 在50个客户端和三种受控时序漂移模式下的CIFAR-10实验中，FlashbackCL相对Flashback提升6.9%-10.0%，时序遗忘减少高达68%。5变体消融实验表明CBRS回放是关键组件。在平稳CIFAR-100上，FlashbackCL也提升了3.5个百分点。

Conclusion: FlashbackCL有效解决了联邦学习中客户端分布时序漂移下的遗忘问题，类别平衡回放不仅能缓解时序漂移，还能正则化空间异质性。

Original Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) of foundation and edge models increasingly targets deployments where client data distributions drift over time, yet existing forgetting-mitigation methods assume each client's distribution is stationary. Flashback, the strongest recent FL method against cross-client (spatial) forgetting, uses monotonically accumulating per-class label counts as a knowledge proxy; this proxy becomes miscalibrated under temporal distribution shift and anchors the global model to an outdated class balance. We formalise temporal forgetting in FL with a per-phase metric isolated from protocol-level fluctuations and propose Flashback Continual Learning (FlashbackCL), a drop-in extension of Flashback with (i) temporally-decayed label counts; (ii) a device-aware replay buffer with Class-Balanced Reservoir Sampling (CBRS); and (iii) server-side active coreset curation on the public distillation set. The results show that FlashbackCL achieves 6.9% to 10.0% relative improvement relative to Flashback, on CIFAR-10 with 50 clients and three controlled temporal shift modes, while simultaneously reducing temporal forgetting by up to 68%. A 5-variant ablation identifies CBRS replay as the critical component. FlashbackCL also improves Flashback by 3.5 points on stationary CIFAR-100, suggesting that class-balanced replay regularises spatial heterogeneity as well as temporal shift.

中文摘要翻译: 基础模型和边缘模型的联邦学习越来越多地部署在客户端数据分布随时间漂移的场景中，然而现有的遗忘缓解方法假设每个客户端的数据分布是平稳的。Flashback——当前针对跨客户端（空间）遗忘的最强联邦学习方法——使用单调累积的每类标签计数作为知识代理；这种代理在时序分布漂移下会变得失准，并将全局模型锚定在过时的类别平衡上。本文形式化了联邦学习中时序遗忘的概念，使用一个独立于协议层面波动的逐阶段度量指标，并提出了Flashback Continual Learning（FlashbackCL），这是Flashback的一个即插即用扩展，包含：(i) 时序衰减的标签计数；(ii) 带有类别平衡水库采样的设备感知回放缓冲区；(iii) 在公共蒸馏集上的服务器端主动核心集筛选。实验结果表明，在50个客户端和三种受控时序漂移模式的CIFAR-10数据集上，相对于Flashback，FlashbackCL实现了6.9%至10.0%的相对提升，同时时序遗忘降低了高达68%。一项包含5个变体的消融实验表明，类别平衡水库采样回放是最关键的组件。在平稳CIFAR-100数据集上，FlashbackCL相比Flashback提升了3.5个百分点，这表明类别平衡回放不仅能正则化时序漂移，还能正则化空间异质性。

</details>


### [284] [Using Reward Uncertainty to Induce Diverse Behaviour in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03962)
*Anthony GX-Chen,Ankit Anand,Gheorghe Comanici,Zaheer Abbas,Eser Aygün,David Smalling,Shibl Mourad,Doina Precup,André Barreto,Mark Rowland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的强化学习框架，将奖励函数由标量替换为分布，通过对动作集合应用非线性目标函数，使得多样性行为自然涌现，无需牺牲期望奖励。在上下文赌博机设定下，推导了梯度估计器，并证明了该方法可泛化策略梯度等传统方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统强化学习追求最大化标量奖励的期望和，但现代应用（如语言模型微调、科学发现）需要行为多样性。现有的熵正则化或多样性奖励等方法存在脆弱权衡（牺牲性能换取随机性）或依赖启发式指标（可能错误对齐策略排序）的问题。作者认为多样性应被理解为对奖励不确定性的理性反应。

Method: 将传统标量奖励替换为奖励函数上的分布，并对动作集合应用非线性目标函数。在上下文赌博机设定下，推导了该目标的原理性梯度估计器，并证明该公式自然泛化了传统策略梯度方法和近期提出的动作集方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架提供了一个稳健且具有理论基础的替代方案，能够解决传统RL公式无法诱发所需行为广度的问题。理论证明了该方法泛化策略梯度和动作集方法的能力。

Conclusion: 该框架能够使校准的行为多样性自然涌现，保持可控性（通过奖励函数分布调节），且无需牺牲期望奖励，为复杂RL任务提供了理论扎实的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Classical reinforcement learning (RL) typically seeks a deterministic policy that maximizes the expected sum of a scalar reward. Yet, modern applications such as language model fine-tuning or scientific discovery demand diversity. Existing remedies such as entropy regularization or diversity bonuses often require fragile trade-offs that sacrifice performance for stochasticity or rely on heuristic metrics that can misalign policy rankings. We argue that diversity is more naturally understood as the rational response to uncertainty in the reward. When the reward function is not perfectly known--as is the case with ambiguous preferences or imperfect reward models--committing to a single action can be sub-optimal. Building on this, we propose a fundamental reformulation of the RL objective by replacing the scalar reward with a distribution over reward functions, and applying a non-linear objective over sets of actions. The result is a framework in which calibrated behavioural diversity emerges naturally, remains controllable through the reward function distribution, and is obtained without sacrificing expected reward. Focusing on the contextual bandit setting, we derive a principled gradient estimator for this objective and prove that our formulation naturally generalizes both vanilla policy gradient and more recently developed action-set approaches. Our empirical results demonstrate that this framework offers a robust and theoretically grounded alternative for complex RL tasks where the traditional formulation of the problem fails to induce the desired breadth of agent behaviour.

中文摘要翻译: 经典强化学习通常寻求一个确定性策略，以最大化标量奖励的期望和。然而，诸如语言模型微调或科学发现等现代应用需要多样性。现有的补救措施，如熵正则化或多样性奖励，通常需要脆弱的权衡——牺牲性能换取随机性——或依赖可能错误对齐策略排序的启发式指标。我们认为，多样性更自然地应被理解为对奖励不确定性的理性反应。当奖励函数并非完全已知时——例如存在模糊偏好或不完善的奖励模型——承诺单一动作可能是次优的。基于此，我们提出对强化学习目标进行根本性重新表述：将标量奖励替换为奖励函数上的分布，并对动作集合应用非线性目标函数。其结果是，校准的行为多样性自然涌现，通过奖励函数分布保持可控性，且无需牺牲期望奖励。聚焦于上下文赌博机设定，我们推导了该目标的原理性梯度估计器，并证明我们的公式自然泛化了传统策略梯度方法以及近期发展的动作集方法。我们的实验结果表明，该框架为复杂的强化学习任务提供了一个稳健且具有理论基础的替代方案，在这些任务中，传统的问题表述无法诱导出预期的智能体行为广度。

</details>


### [285] [Language Models Need Sleep: Learning to Self-Modify and Consolidate Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03979)
*Ali Behrouz,Farnoosh Hashemi,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受人类睡眠启发的“Sleep”范式，使大语言模型能够通过记忆巩固（知识播种 + 蒸馏）和做梦（自我对弈式RL训练）实现持续学习，将短期上下文知识转化为长期参数知识。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有模型缺乏持续学习能力，无法将时间上下文知识有效迁移到长期参数中，受人类学习过程启发，希望让模型像人类一样通过“睡眠”巩固记忆、蒸馏知识并自我改进。

Method: 提出Sleep范式，包含两个阶段：(1) 记忆巩固：通过“知识播种”（Knowledge Seeding）将小模型记忆蒸馏到大模型，结合on-policy蒸馏与RL模仿学习；(2) 做梦：模型使用RL生成合成数据课程，自我排练新知识并完善已有能力。

Result: 在长程任务、持续学习、知识融入和少样本泛化任务上的实验支持了睡眠阶段的重要性。

Conclusion: 睡眠范式能有效提升模型的持续学习能力，通过记忆巩固和做梦机制实现短期知识向长期参数的转化与自我改进。

Original Abstract: The past few decades have witnessed significant advances in the design of machine learning algorithms, from early studies on task-specific shallow models to more general deep Large Language Models (LLMs). Despite showing promising results in tasks that require instant prediction or in-context learning, existing models lack the ability to continually learn and effectively transfer their temporal in-context knowledge to their long-term parameters. Inspired by human learning process, we introduce a ''Sleep'' paradigm that allows the models to continually learn, distill their short-term fragile memories into stable long-term knowledge with replay, and recursively improve themselves with ''Dreaming'' process. In more detail, sleep consists of two stages: (1) Memory Consolidation: an upward distillation process, called Knowledge Seeding, where the memories of a smaller-self are distilled into a larger network to provide more capacity while preserving the knowledge. As a proof of concept, we present a new Generalized Distillation process for {Knowledge Seeding} (i.e., the combination of on-policy distillation with Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based imitation learning); (2) Dreaming: a self-improvement phase, where the model uses RL to generate a curriculum of synthetic data to rehearse new knowledge and refine existing capabilities without human supervision. Our experiments on long-horizon, continual learning, knowledge incorporation, and few-shot generalization tasks support the importance of the sleep stage.

中文摘要翻译: 过去几十年间，机器学习算法的设计取得了显著进展，从早期针对特定任务的浅层模型研究发展到更通用的深度大语言模型（LLMs）。尽管在需要即时预测或上下文学习的任务中展现出令人期待的结果，现有模型仍缺乏持续学习的能力，无法将其时间上下文知识有效迁移到长期参数中。受人类学习过程的启发，我们引入了一种“睡眠”（Sleep）范式，使模型能够持续学习，通过回放将其短期脆弱记忆蒸馏为稳定的长期知识，并通过“做梦”（Dreaming）过程递归地自我改进。更具体地说，睡眠包括两个阶段：（1）记忆巩固：一个向上蒸馏的过程，称为知识播种（Knowledge Seeding），其中较小模型自身的记忆被蒸馏到更大的网络中，以在保留知识的同时提供更大的容量。作为概念验证，我们提出了一种用于知识播种的新型泛化蒸馏过程（即on-policy蒸馏与基于强化学习的模仿学习的结合）；（2）做梦：一个自我改进阶段，模型使用强化学习生成合成数据课程，以排练新知识并在没有人工监督的情况下完善已有能力。我们在长程任务、持续学习、知识融入和少样本泛化任务上的实验支持了睡眠阶段的重要性。

</details>


### [286] [Skill-RM: Unifying Heterogeneous Evaluation Criteria via Agent Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03980)
*Tao Chen,Gangwei Jiang,Pengyu Cheng,Siyuan Huang,Yihao Liu,Jingwei Ni,Jiaqi Guo,Mengyu Zhou,Kai Tang,Junling Liu,Qinliang Su,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Skill-RM框架，将奖励建模重构为可重用的“奖励评估技能”执行过程，通过结构化智能体任务动态编排异构证据，实现统一、一致的奖励评估，在多个基准和下游任务中超越传统方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前奖励评估依赖规则验证器、真实参考、程序化检查表等异构标准，缺乏统一机制来整合所有类型的证据。

Method: Skill-RM将奖励计算视为结构化的智能体任务，提供统一接口来编排异构资源，动态选择和聚合针对每个输入特定需求的证据。

Result: 在奖励基准测试以及最佳N选和强化学习等下游应用上的实验表明，Skill-RM持续优于传统的评判基线。

Conclusion: Skill-RM不仅为奖励建模提供了统一解决方案，还通过战略性、动态的证据编排实现了更优的性能。

Original Abstract: Reward models (RMs) provide critical feedback signals for LLM post-training, notably in reinforced fine-tuning (RFT) and reinforcement learning (RL) pipelines. However, current reward evaluation relies on heterogeneous criteria such as rule-based verifiers, ground-truth references, procedural checklists, and complex rubrics, where a unified mechanism to integrate all types of evidence remains unexplored. To this end, we propose Skill Reward Model (Skill-RM), a unified framework that reformulates reward modeling as the execution of a reusable Reward-Evaluation Skill. By treating reward computation as a structured agentic task, Skill-RM provides a consistent interface to orchestrate heterogeneous resources, dynamically selecting and aggregating evidence tailored to the specific requirements of each input. This approach enables the reward model to move beyond static evaluation, ensuring consistency and transparency across diverse tasks. Extensive experiments on reward benchmarks and downstream applications, including best-of-N selection and reinforcement learning, demonstrate that Skill-RM consistently outperforms traditional judge baselines. Our findings suggest that Skill-RM not only provides a unified solution for reward modeling but also achieves superior performance through the strategic and dynamic orchestration of evidence. The code is at https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/Skill-RM.

中文摘要翻译: 奖励模型（RM）为LLM的后训练（特别是在强化微调（RFT）和强化学习（RL）流程中）提供关键的反馈信号。然而，当前的奖励评估依赖于异构标准，如基于规则的验证器、真实参考、程序化检查表和复杂评分细则，目前尚缺乏统一机制来整合所有类型的证据。为此，我们提出技能奖励模型（Skill-RM），一种将奖励建模重构为可重用“奖励评估技能”执行的统一框架。通过将奖励计算视为结构化的智能体任务，Skill-RM提供了统一的接口来编排异构资源，动态选择和聚合针对每个输入特定需求的证据。该方法使奖励模型能够超越静态评估，确保跨不同任务的一致性和透明度。在奖励基准测试以及包括最佳N选和强化学习在内的下游应用上的大量实验表明，Skill-RM持续优于传统的评判基线。我们的研究结果表明，Skill-RM不仅为奖励建模提供了统一解决方案，还通过战略性、动态的证据编排实现了更优的性能。代码开源于https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/Skill-RM。

</details>
